### [1] [Lower bound on Dirac resonances with mass-type potentials in the semi-classical limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02356)
*Zhuo Chen,Michael Melgaard*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文为带质量型势的三维半经典 Dirac 算子建立了共振下界，证明在能量阈值附近至少存在 $C\hbar^{-3}$ 个共振（计重数），与已有上界一致，并给出了所需的相对半经典迹公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究带质量型势的三维半经典 Dirac 算子共振数量的下界，补足此前仅有上界（Khochman）的结果，并刻画共振与经典密度、波前奇异性以及捕获能量的联系。

Method: 对满足外部解析性、衰减和非退化假设的质量函数对，构造相对分布 $\mu$；将 $\mu$ 的解析波前奇异性转移到相对经典态密度上，并结合 Khochman 局部迹公式与相对半经典迹公式完成计数论证；同时证明一般 Hermitian 矩阵值扰动下的相对半经典迹公式。

Result: 若 $E_0>1$ 是 $\supp\mu$ 的边界点，则 $E_0$ 与 $-E_0$ 的任意复邻域内合计至少含 $C\hbar^{-3}$ 个两算子的共振（计重数）。对单个有界质量型势 $v$（外部扇区解析且幂次衰减，$\inf(1+v)>0$，$\sup v>0$），$\Dl=\Dl_0+\boldsymbol{\beta}v$ 在 $E_0=1+\sup v$ 及 $-E_0$ 的任意复邻域内至少有 $C\hbar^{-3}$ 个共振（$\hbar$ 充分小）；指数与 Khochman 上界吻合，两族共振由谱对称性联系，且 $E_0$ 必为捕获能量。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了与上界匹配的共振下界，确证了指数 $\hbar^{-3}$ 的尖锐性，揭示了共振族间的谱对称性及阈值能量的捕获性质，并提供了适用于一般 Hermitian 矩阵值扰动的相对半经典迹公式。

Abstract: We establish lower bounds on the number of resonances for three-dimensional semiclassical Dirac operators with mass-type potentials. For a pair $\Dl_j=\Dl_0+\mbfβv_j$, $j=1,2$, satisfying the exterior analyticity, decay and non-degeneracy assumptions of the paper, we associate a relative distribution $μ$ to the corresponding mass functions. We prove that if $E_0>1$ is a boundary point of $\suppμ$, then every complex neighbourhood of $E_0$, and likewise of $-E_0$, contains in total at least $C\hbar^{-3}$ resonances of the two operators, counted with multiplicity. The proof transfers an analytic wave-front singularity of $μ$ to the relative classical density of states and combines this with Khochman's local trace formula and a relative semiclassical trace formula. As a consequence, if $v$ is a single bounded mass-type potential, analytic with power decay in an exterior sector, with $\inf(1+v)>0$ and $\sup v>0$, then $\Dl=\Dl_0+\mbfβv$ has at least $C\hbar^{-3}$ resonances in every complex neighbourhood of $E_0=1+\sup v$ and of $-E_0$, for all sufficiently small $\hbar$. The exponent agrees with Khochman's upper bound, the two resonance families are related by spectral symmetry, and $E_0$ is necessarily a trapping energy. We also prove the relative semiclassical trace formula needed in the argument for general Hermitian matrix-valued perturbations.

</details>


### [2] [Blow-up for the integrable defocusing Ablowitz-Ladik equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02358)
*Dirk Hennig,Nikos I. Karachalios,Dionyssios Mantzavinos,Dimitrios Mitsotakis,Barbara Prinari*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究完全可积的散焦 Ablowitz–Ladik 方程在无限与有限格点上的有限时间爆破，并证明在闭单位球外的一般初值下，其动力学由有限时间爆破主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ablowitz–Ladik 方程是完全可积的格点系统，尽管其可积结构通常暗示良好行为，但本文关注闭单位球外的初值是否会导致有限时间爆破，并探讨一般初值下哪种动力学占主导。

Method: 首先在 ℓ^∞（一致有界序列）空间中建立任意初值的局部适定性；随后分析单位圆作为分离面，证明格点模长保持在初始所在区域；通过构造一类显式给出爆破时间的奇异初值证明有限时间爆破；并构造单点激励任意大但全局存在的解；最后将这些奇异解作为一般问题的下解，证明爆破主导性，并辅以数值研究。

Result: 证明了单位圆是晶格动力学的分离面，模长区域保持不变；闭 ℓ^∞ 单位球内初值产生全局解；有限时间爆破仅可能发生于初始模长在单位圆外的格点，并显式构造了具有精确爆破时间的奇异解；同时构造了单点激励任意大却全局存在的解；通过下解方法证明一般闭单位球外初值的动力学由有限时间爆破主导。

Conclusion: 完全可积的散焦 Ablowitz–Ladik 方程在闭 ℓ^∞ 单位球外的初值下仍会有限时间爆破，且爆破是主导动力学；单位圆充当分离面，区分全局有界与可能爆破的行为。

Abstract: This work establishes finite-time blow-up for the completely integrable defocusing Ablowitz-Ladik equation on both infinite and finite lattices. We first establish local well-posedness for arbitrary initial data in the space $\ell^\infty$ of uniformly bounded sequences, with no decay assumptions and under general boundary conditions. We then show that the unit circle acts as a separatrix for the lattice dynamics: sites with an initial modulus inside, on, or outside the unit circle maintain their modulus within their respective region for as long as the solution exists. As a consequence, initial data in the closure of the $\ell^\infty$ unit ball generate global-in-time solutions. On the other hand, the unit circle separatrix opens up the possibility for finite-time blow-up, which is restricted to sites with initial modulus outside the unit circle. We establish this scenario by constructing a class of initial data whose corresponding solutions are singular, with explicitly given exact blow-up times. We also construct solutions emanating from initial data with an arbitrarily large single-site excitation outside the unit circle that exist globally in time, a behavior driven by the convergence of the corresponding modulus to the unit circle equilibrium. In the presence of such solutions, along with other important classes of analytical, global-in-time solutions that remain outside the closed $\ell^\infty$ unit ball for all times, we examine the fundamental question of the dominant dynamics for general initial data outside the closed unit ball. By utilizing the constructed class of singular solutions as subsolutions for the general problem, we prove that the dynamics for the completely integrable defocusing Ablowitz-Ladik lattice is dominated by finite-time blow-up. We conclude with numerical studies of illustrative examples of general initial data

</details>


### [3] [Strictly positive optimal Hardy weights on manifolds and graphs via Green potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02365)
*Ujjal Das,Matthias Keller,Yehuda Pinchover*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文利用上解构造，在非紧黎曼流形区域上为次临界p-Schrödinger泛函Q构造了一族严格正的临界Hardy权，并揭示其在参数ε=0处由正临界向零临界的转变；最佳Hardy常数为c=[(p-1)/p]^{p-1}，ε=0时权最优，并在图上得到类似结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究次临界泛函的临界Hardy权及其最佳常数，刻画临界性由正临界向零临界转变的现象，并将结果推广至图结构。

Method: 基于具有严格正电荷且在无穷远处消失的Green位势，采用上解构造方法，引入参数0≤ε≤1构造一族严格正的临界Hardy权。

Result: 得到一族严格正的临界Hardy权，其在ε=0处发生从正临界到零临界的转变；最佳Hardy常数为c=[(p-1)/p]^{p-1}，且ε=0时的Hardy权为最优；图上获得类似结果。

Conclusion: 该构造统一刻画了带参数Hardy权的临界行为与最优性，并为流形与图上的p-Schrödinger型不等式提供了推广。

Abstract: Consider a subcritical functional $Q$ associated with a $p$-Schrödinger operator defined on a domain $Ω$ in noncompact Riemannian manifold. Employing the supersolution construction, we obtain a family of strictly positive critical Hardy weights for $Q$. The construction is based on the Green potential, with a strictly positive charge, which is assumed to vanish at infinity. An interesting new feature of this family, depending on a parameter $0\leq \varepsilon\leq 1$, is the transition from positive-criticality to null-criticality at $\varepsilon=0$. Moreover, we show that the best Hardy constant for this family is given by $c=[(p-1)/p]^{p-1}$, and that for $\varepsilon=0$ the corresponding Hardy weight is optimal. We furthermore, prove an analogous result on graphs.

</details>


### [4] [Finite-time blowup of the Euler equation without forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02509)
*Fan Zheng*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文阐述三维不可压缩无外力Euler方程有限时间爆破的构造方法（基于文献[Euler]），通过对光滑母解逐层添加振荡层构造新解：每层由有限展开与精确校正组成，使子解仍满足Euler方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三维不可压缩无外力Euler方程解的有限时间爆破问题，明确给出爆破构造，以加深对该方程解行为与奇点形成的理解。

Method: 采用逐层构造：从光滑母解出发，反复添加振荡层。每层先做有限展开，再作精确校正，从而保证得到的子解严格满足Euler方程。

Result: 成功给出三维无外力不可压缩Euler方程在R^3上的有限时间爆破构造，并保证每一步子解均为Euler方程的精确解。

Conclusion: 该笔记提供了构造三维Euler方程有限时间爆破解的系统框架，逐层添加振荡层并配合精确校正的方法可用于进一步研究爆破现象及方程解的性质。

Abstract: These notes present the construction in \cite{euler} of finite-time blowup for the three-dimensional incompressible, unforced Euler equations on $\mathbb R^3$: \begin{equation}
  \partial_tu+(u\cdot\nabla)u+\nabla p=0,\qquad \nabla\cdot u=0. \end{equation} The construction successively adds oscillatory layers to smooth parent solutions. Each layer is constructed by a finite expansion and an exact correction, so that the resulting child solves Euler.

</details>


### [5] [Asymptotic behavior of the one-dimensional Vlasov--Poisson system with a repulsive harmonic potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02551)
*Huang,Wenrui*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究一维带排斥谐振势的Vlasov–Poisson系统，证明任意大初值的修正散射并构造修正波算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 排斥自相互作用下的Vlasov–Poisson系统存在极长程相互作用，其大初值散射理论尚未完善；现有高维结果依赖对称性、紧支撑或空间矩等假设，本文旨在去除这些限制并补充一维情形。

Method: 在加权的Sobolev空间中，利用Hamilton–Jacobi动量修正以及排斥相互作用的正负号性质获得大初值估计，并据此构造修正波算子。

Result: 对任意大小的初值（无需对称性、紧支撑或空间矩假设）建立了修正散射，并构造了相应的修正波算子。

Conclusion: 结果与高维理论（HK26、VR24、BVR25）相互补充，所提方法有望推广至更广泛的具有极长程相互作用的动理学方程。

Abstract: We consider the one-dimensional Vlasov--Poisson system with a repulsive harmonic potential. For repulsive self-interactions, we establish modified scattering for initial data of arbitrary size in some weighted Sobolev spaces without symmetry, compact support, or spatial moment assumptions. The proof uses a Hamilton-Jacobi momentum correction and the sign of the repulsive interaction to obtain large-data bounds. We also construct modified wave operators. These results complement the higher-dimensional theory in \cite{HK26,VR24,BVR25}. We expect our methods to be useful in the study of a broader class of kinetic equations with very long-range interactions.

</details>


### [6] [Stability and Instability of the Unique Positive Steady State for a Nonlocal Population Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02620)
*Yuan Lou,Ming-Zhen Xin*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究非局部种群方程中空间异质性对唯一正稳态线性稳定性的影响：足够大扩散时线性稳定；在生长比极大集条件下足够小扩散时线性稳定；一维情形构造不稳定正稳态并得到Hopf分支，且可通过改变非局部积分权重得到超临界或亚临界Hopf分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因非局部项存在，线性化算子未必自伴或不满足极值原理，基于主特征值的常规稳定性论证不能直接适用；同时意在澄清唯一正稳态是否必然线性稳定。

Method: 对非局部种群方程的线性化算子进行稳定性分析，分别处理大扩散与小扩散情形；在一维空间中构造不稳定正稳态并做Hopf分支分析，通过改变非局部积分权重调控分支类型。

Result: 足够大扩散下线性稳定；在局部生长比极大集的适当条件下足够小扩散下线性稳定；一维情形存在不稳定正稳态，且在足够小扩散下出现Hopf分支，可为超临界或亚临界。

Conclusion: 唯一性不蕴含线性稳定性；非局部权重可改变分支性质，表明空间异质性对稳定性的重要影响。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with the effect of spatial heterogeneity on the linear stability of unique positive steady states for a nonlocal population equation. The main difficulty is that, because of the nonlocal term, the linearized operator need not be self-adjoint or satisfy the maximum principle, so the usual stability arguments based on the principal eigenvalue do not apply directly. We establish linear stability for sufficiently large diffusion and, under suitable conditions on the maximum set of the local growth ratio, for sufficiently small diffusion. In one space dimension, we also construct unstable positive steady states and obtain Hopf bifurcations for sufficiently small diffusion. Keeping the local coefficients fixed, we obtain either a supercritical or a subcritical Hopf bifurcation by changing the weight in the nonlocal integral. Our results show that uniqueness of a positive steady state does not imply its linear stability.

</details>


### [7] [A Compressible Miles Instability for Wind-Wave Generation in a Neutral Atmosphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02628)
*Tianyi Li*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究可压缩空气-不可压缩水界面的正模稳定性，建立可压缩版Miles临界层不稳定性，证明极限吸收原理与失稳判据，并给出M→0时的不稳定根收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Miles型临界层不稳定性原为不可压缩剪切流理论。当空气可压缩时，失稳机制是否保持、奇异系数如何变化、以及低马赫数极限是否收敛，均需严格分析。本文旨在将Bühler-Shatah-Walsh-Zeng的不可压缩严格理论推广到含重力与表面张力的可压缩大气情形。

Method: 设静止水层上方为静力、常熵、中性层结的剪切流U(z)，U(0)=0，含重力g与表面张力。引入空气/水密度比ε为小参数，并限制均匀亚声速波相对流。对实相速c_r，在正则临界层U(z_c)=c_r处，奇异系数留数正比于Q=U''+gU'/a^2=ρ̄_0(U'/ρ̄_0)'，以比涡度梯度替代不可压缩理论中的U''。对正则非共振相速证明极限吸收原理，并给出波数k>0处气侧压力响应边值Z_a^+(k,c_r)虚部的留数公式。以自由重力-毛细波相速c_k为中心，若Im Z_a^+(k,c_k)>0，则小ε下色散关系在c_k邻域内有唯一不稳定根c_ε；充分条件为所有临界层Q≤0且最高临界层Q<0。

Result: 获得可压缩情形的Miles临界层失稳判据：当Im Z_a^+(k,c_k)>0时，充分小ε下存在唯一不稳定根c_ε，且其位置不依赖ε。给出充分条件Q≤0（各临界层）且Q<0（最高临界层）。证明M→0时Z_a^+以O(M^2)速率收敛到不可压缩对应量；在相应不可压缩符号条件下，|c_ε(M)-c_ε(0)|≤CεM^2，常数C与ε、M无关。

Conclusion: 可压缩空气-水界面在亚声速剪切流下存在Miles型临界层不稳定性，其奇异系数由比涡度梯度Q刻画，而非不可压缩的曲率U''。极限吸收原理与留数公式给出明确失稳判据。低马赫数极限以O(M^2)收敛至不可压缩理论，且不稳定根偏差受CεM^2控制。

Abstract: We study the normal-mode stability of a flat interface between compressible Euler flow in the air and incompressible Euler flow in the water, including gravity and surface tension. The base state is quiescent water beneath a hydrostatic, constant-entropy shear flow $U(z)$ with $U(0)=0$, so the atmosphere is neutrally stratified. For a small ratio $\varepsilon$ of the air density at the interface to the water density, and for uniformly subsonic wave-relative flow, we establish a compressible analog of the critical-layer instability of Miles, extending the rigorous incompressible theory of Bühler, Shatah, Walsh, and Zeng. For a real phase speed $c_r$, the singular coefficient at a regular critical level $U(z_c)=c_r$ has a residue proportional to $Q=U''+gU'/a^2=\barρ_0(U'/\barρ_0)'$, with gravitational acceleration $g$, sound speed $a$, and normalized base-state air density $\barρ_0$. Thus the gradient of specific vorticity replaces the curvature $U''$ of the incompressible theory.
  We prove a limiting absorption principle at regular, non-resonant phase speeds and a residue formula for the imaginary part of the boundary value $Z_{\mathrm a}^+(k,c_r)$ of the air-side pressure response at wavenumber $k>0$. Let $c_k>0$ be the phase speed of the free gravity-capillary wave. If $\mathrm{Im}\,Z_{\mathrm a}^+(k,c_k)>0$, then, for sufficiently small $\varepsilon$, the dispersion relation has exactly one unstable root $c_\varepsilon$ in a neighborhood of $c_k$ that does not depend on $\varepsilon$. A sufficient condition is $Q\le0$ at every critical level and $Q<0$ at the highest one.
  As the Mach number $M\to0$, $Z_{\mathrm a}^+$ converges to its incompressible counterpart at rate $O(M^2)$. Under the corresponding incompressible sign condition, the unstable root satisfies $|c_\varepsilon(M)-c_\varepsilon(0)|\le C\varepsilon M^2$ with $C$ independent of $\varepsilon$ and $M$.

</details>


### [8] [Hopf bifurcation in a predator-prey model with diffusion and spatial heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02630)
*Yuan Lou,Ming-Zhen Xin*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究一维捕食者-猎物反应扩散系统（Neumann边界）中空间异质性对正平衡稳定性的影响，发现即便正平衡唯一，空间异质性仍可导致不稳定，并在临界参数处经Hopf分支产生严格正的非平稳时间周期解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨空间异质性是否会破坏正平衡的稳定性，尤其关注正平衡唯一的情形，以弥补对异质性诱导不稳定机制的认识。

Method: 构造光滑的空间异质系数族，分析其线性稳定性；在临界参数值处考察特征值穿越虚轴并应用Hopf分支理论。

Result: 正平衡可因空间异质性而失稳；临界参数处一对共轭特征值横截穿越虚轴，Hopf分支产生局部严格正的非平稳经典时间周期解。

Conclusion: 空间异质性本身可诱导唯一正平衡失稳并生成周期解，给出的三类系数族实例说明了该现象的多样性。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate how spatial heterogeneity affects the stability of positive equilibria in one-dimensional predator--prey reaction--diffusion systems with Neumann boundary conditions. Our examples show that spatial heterogeneity can lead to instability even when the positive equilibrium is unique. For the smooth, spatially heterogeneous coefficient families constructed here, this equilibrium loses linear stability as a parameter in the reaction coefficients varies. At the critical parameter value, a simple conjugate pair of eigenvalues crosses the imaginary axis transversely, and a Hopf bifurcation produces a local branch of strictly positive, nonstationary classical time-periodic solutions. The examples include two families with spatially concentrated prey, one with an everywhere positive and the other with an everywhere negative predator intrinsic growth rate. A third family has a spatially concentrated predator population and an everywhere negative predator intrinsic growth rate.

</details>


### [9] [A common structure for modified scattering: Vlasov--Riesz, Hartree, and their coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02643)
*Wenrui Huang,Mengyi Xie*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了Hartree方程、Vlasov–Riesz方程及其耦合系统的共同"作用量–密度"结构，并据此在三维小数据条件下建立了修正散射结果，覆盖强长程势区间 0<α≤1/2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hartree、Vlasov–Riesz及耦合Vlasov–Hartree系统在长程相互作用下的小数据全局适定性与修正散射此前尚缺乏统一处理，尤其是强长程区间 0<α≤1/2 的结果为空缺，作者希望用统一的结构性框架填补这一空白。

Method: 作者识别出三类方程共享的"作用量–密度"结构：沿自由射线，非线性作用量与重标度密度之间的反馈支配波的相位调制与动量平移。该结构在交换源（Hartree与Vlasov–Riesz）时保持不变，从而给出收敛的修正剖面与渐近修正项的显式递归构造。

Result: 在三维、小数据、势为 λ|x|^{-α}（λ∈R，0<α≤1/2）条件下，证明了Hartree方程、Vlasov–Riesz方程以及耦合Vlasov–Hartree系统的全局存在性与修正散射。渐近作用量包含有限多个相继修正项，超越其主项。

The upper threshold is sharp: for every $q>q_c(N,p)$ we exhibit positive bounded radial solutions stable outside a compact set. At the Sobolev endpoint, every $C^1$ solution stable outside a compact set has finite energy; for $p>2$ this removes the a priori $\mathcal D^{1,p}$ assumption from the low-Morse-index classification of Farina, Mercuri and Willem and answers their question on the Morse index of the Aubin--Talenti extremals: it is one.   <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 对每个 $p>1$：当 $2\le N\le5$ 时，唯一的界正解为轮廓 $w_0(x_N)$；当 $N\ge8$ 时，存在单参数有界正非一维解族。临界维数 $N=7$ 处，只要精确约化非线性的三次系数 $\kappa(p)>0$ 即发生非唯一性，特别地对所有 $p\ge2$ 有 $\kappa(p)>0$，并由连续性延伸至 $2$ 以下。相应振幅具有 Fowler 尾端，其极限能量、颈部尺寸、鼓包尺寸及对数周期均被确定到主阶。$N=6$ 及 $N=7$ 的互补区间仍开放。

Abstract: Let $w_0$ be the positive homoclinic solution of $-w''+w=w^p$ and set $c_p=w_0(0)$. This is the threshold boundary value left open by Fernández and Weth [Math.\ Ann.\ \textbf{383} (2022), 361--397]. We prove a dimension-dependent rigidity/nonuniqueness picture. For every $p>1$, the profile $w_0(x_N)$ is the unique bounded positive solution when $2\le N\le5$, whereas for $N\ge8$ there is a one-parameter family of bounded positive non-one-dimensional solutions. In the critical dimension $N=7$, nonuniqueness holds whenever a cubic coefficient $κ(p)$ of the exact reduced nonlinearity is positive; in particular $κ(p)>0$ for all $p\ge2$, and continuity extends this range below $2$. The corresponding amplitudes have Fowler ends, with their limiting energy, neck size, bulge size, and logarithmic period determined to leading order. The case $N=6$, as well as the complementary range in $N=7$, remain open.

For $N\ge8$ the reduced linearization is invertible in weighted spaces. In $N=7$ the critical resonance is bypassed by solving the projected radial equation nonlinearly and closing the transverse equation by a Schauder--Tychonoff fixed point.

</details>

The corresponding contributions cancel up to a small phase error, producing a contraction \textit{à la Döblin}. Iterating this contraction yields exponential Fourier decay estimates with enhanced dissipation.

For rough coefficients $A(t,v)$ independent of position, we obtain the estimate for every $p>2n+1$, where $n$ is the dimension of position and velocity. The Fourier decay also gives spatial smoothness, and parabolic regularity gives Hölder continuity in time and velocity. For autonomous coefficients $a(x,v)$ in dimension one, position serves as time away from zero velocity. A Harnack comparison controls returns to small velocity intervals and yields the estimate for every $p>4$. Both thresholds are optimal among those valid for all ellipticity ratios.

We prove enhanced dissipation with the optimal square-root frequency power and Gevrey regularity in position. For autonomous coefficients $A=A(v)$, we also obtain exponential convergence to equilibrium in $L^2$ weighted by the invariant velocity measure, and a quantitative spectral gap. To our knowledge, these are the first results on decay established for laws of kinetic equations with rough coefficients.

A structural feature of the reduction is the cancellation of the intermediate symbolic order; at the critical endpoint the construction also preserves the exact skew-adjoint structure of the original singular integral.

We first derive an autonomous contour-dynamics equation in canonical surface variables and then apply the abstract theorem directly to the singular integral operators generated by the Birkhoff--Rott formulation. This yields the complete paradifferential structure of the system without taking the Dirichlet--Neumann reduction as a starting point. After introducing an Alinhac good unknown and diagonalizing the resulting system, we obtain local well-posedness for small Sobolev perturbations in the stable Kelvin--Rayleigh--Taylor regime, uniformly on compact subsets of the stable parameter set.

Abstract: We study the well-posedness of small BV solutions to the one-dimensional isentropic Euler system on the half-line under inflow and outflow boundary conditions. These boundary conditions are formulated in terms of admissible trace sets determined by Navier--Stokes boundary layers and zero-speed shocks. For both the inflow and outflow problems, we construct small BV solutions taking values in the subsonic region. These solutions are unique and satisfy the quantitative stability estimate \begin{align*}
  \|U(\cdot, t)-V(\cdot, t)\|_{L^2} \lesssim \sqrt{\|U(\cdot, 0) - V(\cdot, 0)\|_{L^2}} \, , \end{align*} which holds for any such small BV solution $V$ and any $L^\infty$ entropy solution $U$ satisfying the strong trace property and the inflow/outflow boundary conditions. In particular, we emphasize that $U$ may take values outside the subsonic region and need not have bounded variation or be constructed by the front tracking scheme. This establishes the first weak-BV stability and uniqueness theory for inflow and outflow initial-boundary value problems. A key difficulty lies in verifying that the boundary set is compatible with the $a$-contraction framework, and in proving that the front-tracking limit satisfies the prescribed inflow/outflow conditions.

Abstract: We study almost Hermitian manifolds whose lowered Nijenhuis tensor is totally skew-symmetric and whose characteristic (Bismut) torsion is closed, the almost-SKT condition. This extends the SKT (pluriclosed) condition beyond the integrable setting. For a fixed almost complex structure, these constraints are linear in the metric. We introduce an overdetermined ellipticity condition which, on compact manifolds, makes the admissible infinitesimal deformation space finite-dimensional and yields short-time existence for a constrained Bismut--Ricci flow. In real dimension six, almost-SKT structures satisfy a dichotomy: either the almost complex structure is integrable, or the skew Nijenhuis tensor is nowhere vanishing with constant norm and the Bismut Ricci form vanishes. Hence, on compact non-integrable almost-SKT six-manifolds, the constrained Bismut--Ricci flow is stationary. In dimension eight, ellipticity always fails, while in higher dimensions Nijenhuis rigidity holds on an open dense locus. In arbitrary dimension, we characterize when total skew-symmetry of the lowered Nijenhuis tensor determines the compatible metric pointwise up to scale. On compact manifolds, with closed torsion, this implies uniqueness of the almost-SKT metric up to scaling; the constrained Bismut--Ricci flow is then an explicit homothetic solution. We also relate this flow to generalized Ricci flow and characterize the infinitesimal obstruction to lifting the evolution when the almost complex structure varies. Finally, we construct non-integrable almost-SKT structures with nowhere vanishing Nijenhuis tensor and non-flat characteristic connection on compact six-manifolds, including $S^1\times S^2\times S^3$ and $S^3\times S^3$. In these families, the metric is fixed and the compatible almost complex structures form an $S^2$-family. These structures have vanishing Bismut Ricci form and are therefore almost-CYT.

Consequently, $D^4F(C)[u,u,u,u]\geq 0$ for every smooth real normal field $u$, with equality exactly on the normal tangent space of the Möbius family of round circles. Abstract: Branson's $Q$-curvature is a natural fourth-order counterpart of scalar curvature in conformal geometry. We establish short-time existence and uniqueness using a biharmonic DeTurck gauge and derive global and local integral smoothing estimates under curvature bounds. In dimension four, we prove long-time existence assuming a uniform Sobolev inequality and sufficiently small total $L^2$ curvature energy throughout the maximal interval of existence. Under uniform curvature and Sobolev bounds, time integrability of the $L^2$-norm of $J$ implies smooth convergence in fixed coordinates to a $J$-flat metric. Explicit Einstein and product solutions illustrate the evolution and show that small curvature energy alone does not prevent degeneration. Positive initial Yamabe constant and positive total $Q$-curvature give a uniform positive Yamabe lower bound throughout the smooth four-dimensional flow, whereas in higher dimensions total $Q$-curvature can decrease even near flat metrics. Finally, we prove that every closed four-dimensional $J$-soliton is trivial, its metric is Bach-flat with constant $Q$-curvature, and its soliton vector field is Killing.

The space $\mathcal{M}_{\mathbb{C}}(Γ)$ is independent of the edge lengths and isomorphic to a finite-dimensional complex-symplectic quotient of $(T^*G_{\mathbb{C}})^E$, whereas the hyperkähler metric depends on them. Through cutting and gluing laws, $Γ\mapsto \mathcal{M}_{\mathbb{C}}(Γ)$ defines a 2D TQFT valued in complex Hamiltonian manifolds, in the spirit of Moore--Tachikawa, while its hyperkähler refinement fails to be functorial, since subdividing an edge into sub-edges of arbitrary lengths alters the metric. Retaining the edge lengths restores functoriality: the spaces $\mathcal{M}_{\mathbb{H}}(Γ)$ assemble into a metric field theory on metric cobordisms, valued in hyperkähler manifolds composed by hyperkähler reduction, which fibres over the tropical moduli space $M_{g,n}^{\mathrm{trop}}$ with fixed complex-symplectic fibre. We also describe the degenerations of the metric as an internal edge collapses or becomes infinitely long.

</details>

Abstract: We study a family of aperiodic metric graphs generated by one-dimensional tilings. We prove that, generically, the spectrum of the associated Kirchhoff Laplacian has Lebesgue measure zero and, apart from a discrete set of isolated eigenvalues, is a generalized Cantor set. For a large subclass of these graphs, we further show that isolated eigenvalues are generically absent. This extends several well-known results from the setting of discrete ergodic Schrödinger operators on $\mathbb{Z}$ to a large family of metric graphs. Earlier results of this kind for metric graphs typically assume equilateral edges and exploit symmetries that reduce the analysis to a Sturm-Liouville operator on the real line. Abstract: We survey maximal directional averages and directional singular integrals, and their connections with Kakeya--Nikodym phenomena, differentiation theory and the Carleson operator. The unifying tool is almost orthogonality, which rests on the ordering of the circle in the plane and on polynomial partitioning in higher dimensions. We explain where it yields sharp or nearly sharp bounds and where new ideas are needed, notably for multiscale averages along algebraic curves and for vector-valued single-annulus estimates, and we collect open problems.

Abstract: We establish sharp bounds for rational points near compact smooth nondegenerate curves in $\mathbb R^n$, for every $n\geq4$. For a nondegenerate curve $\mathcal C$ in $\mathbb R^n$, let \[ \mathcal R_{\mathcal C}(δ,Q)

  1\leq q\leq Q,\ \operatorname{dist}(\mathbf p/q,\mathcal C)\leqδ/q\right\}.\] We prove that \[\mathcal R_{\mathcal C}(δ,Q) \lesssim_{\varepsilon,\mathcal C} δ^{n-1}Q^2+Q^\varepsilon \left(Q+\sum_{k=1}^{n-1} δ^{k^2/(k+1)}Q^{(2k+1)/(k+1)}\right).\] A key new ingredient is a wave packet method developed by Gan--Maldague--Oh.

</details>

Abstract: For a finite real alphabet $T$, let $g(T)$ be the supremum of the lengths of words over $T$ containing no two adjacent nonempty blocks of equal length and equal sum. Exhaustive finite searches give the isolated values, while 77 explicit words prove that every remaining real alphabet admits a word of length 139. The latter argument uses exact intersections of finite unions of lines, so it covers irrational parameters as well. The cutoff is sharp because $g(\{0,2,3,7\})=139$. We also obtain a sharp cutoff of 167 within the family $\{0,1,x,x+2\}$, $x>1$. The required supplement prints the word certificates and specifies the complete-search evidence.

Abstract: The least interval diameter needed to realize every attainable cardinality of an $h$-fold sumset of a four-element integer set is $\binom{h+2}{2}+(h\bmod2)$ for $2\le h\le10$ and $d_h=(h-3)(h-1)$ for $h\ge11$. We prove the formula uniformly for $h\ge17$ and use complete finite searches, with a relation-rank coverage argument, for the remaining orders. For the lower bound, we show that every integer four-set with $\binom{h+3}{3}-17$ distinct $h$-fold sums has normalized diameter in $\{d_h,d_h+1,d_h+2\}$ when $h\ge9$, and we construct sets attaining $d_h$. For the upper bound, a relation-lattice index argument restricts larger minimum-diameter representatives to two or three active directions. Two general constructions and four special defects then provide short sets realizing every remaining cardinality.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 若N无平方因子且A⊆Z_N非空、|A|=m，则A为谱集当且仅当A铺砌Z_N；进一步，这两条件恰在m|N且A是模m的完全剩余系时成立。该结论对N的素因子个数及其相对大小均无限制，并给出了Z_N中谱集与铺砌集的完整结构描述。

Abstract: We prove Fuglede's conjecture for finite cyclic groups of square-free order. Specifically, if \(N\) is square-free and \(A\subseteq \mathbb Z_N\) is nonempty with \(|A|=m\), then \(A\) is spectral if and only if it tiles \(\mathbb Z_N\); moreover, these conditions hold precisely when \(m\mid N\) and \(A\) is a complete set of representatives modulo \(m\). This gives a complete structural description of spectral and tiling sets in \(\mathbb Z_N\). The key new ingredient is a prime-coordinate elimination theorem: if \(P\nmid nm\) is an odd prime, then every \(m\)-point spectral pair in \(\mathbb Z_n\times\mathbb Z_P\) projects injectively to a spectral pair in \(\mathbb Z_n\). Its proof combines character-matrix orthogonality, cyclotomic coefficient comparison, and a \(P\)-adic divisibility argument for associated Gram matrices. Iterating this reduction yields the result for arbitrary square-free \(N\), with no restriction on the number or relative sizes of its prime factors.

</details>

Result: 证明：字母多重度均为偶数的smooth二元词，长度≥18即为shuffle square；恰有34个例外，全部长度≤16。推论：Kolakoski序列非空前缀为shuffle square当且仅当两符号出现次数均为偶且长度不为4或8，这样的前缀有无穷多个；此外整条无限Kolakoski序列可划分为两条相同的无限子序列，且单色块长度一致有界。

Abstract: A binary word is a shuffle square if its positions can be partitioned into two identical subsequences. A finite word over $\{1,2\}$ is smooth if repeated finite run-length differentiation remains binary until the empty word is reached. This paper proves that every smooth binary word in which both letters have even multiplicity is a shuffle square once its length is at least $18$. There are exactly $34$ exceptions, all of length at most $16$. The infinite part of the proof is a parity-return atom induction: a smooth even-Parikh word factors uniquely into smooth parity atoms, and every such atom has length at most $8$. A finite, independently checkable certificate handles lengths $18$ through $40$. As a consequence, a nonempty prefix of the classical Kolakoski sequence is a shuffle square if and only if both symbols occur an even number of times and its length is not $4$ or $8$. There are infinitely many such prefixes. In fact, the entire infinite Kolakoski sequence can be partitioned into two identical infinite subsequences with monochromatic blocks of uniformly bounded length.

</details>

Abstract: For a positive integer $n$, let $S(n)=\{σ(d): d\mid n\}$ be ordered by divisibility, and let $a(n)$ be its width. We study how much of the divisor lattice of $n$ survives under the map $d\mapsto σ(d)$. For every fixed prime-exponent pattern, the largest possible value of $a(n)$ is the width of a corresponding product of divisibility posets, and this maximum is attained infinitely often. In the squarefree case with $ω(n)=r$, every width from $1$ to $\binom{r}{\lfloor r/2\rfloor}$ occurs, again infinitely often.
  For repeated exponent patterns, Engel's limit theory reduces the asymptotic width problem to optimal representation variance. We show that for the divisibility poset $D_m=(\{1,\ldots,m\},\mid)$, the prime directions $2$ and $3$ already determine the exact variance. The structural reason is the critical dilation $4$, lying strictly between $3$ and the next prime $5$. This yields an exact finite formula, the limit $V(D_m)\to 1.0367934201598\ldots$, and asymptotics for repeated exponent patterns. The complementary minimum-width problem remains largely open.

Result: 证明了每个阶数的树都存在素数标号，即Entringer-Tout猜想成立，把已有结果从足够大的树扩展到所有阶的树。

Conclusion: 树的结构分解、标签算术组织、Hall匹配、计算机辅助证书与解析估计相结合，构成对所有阶树的完整证明。

Abstract: A prime labeling of a graph labels its vertices bijectively with the integers from one to the number of vertices, so that adjacent vertices receive coprime labels. I prove the Entringer-Tout conjecture that every tree admits a prime labeling, extending the result of Haxell, Pikhurko, and Taraz from sufficiently large trees to all orders. My proof combines structural decompositions of trees, arithmetic organization of the labels, and matching arguments. I reduce the labeling problem to a structured bipartite matching problem by separating a small part of the tree, distributing labels according to their divisibility properties, and applying Hall's theorem to complete the labeling. I handle the finite and intermediate ranges using exact computer-assisted certificates whose validity is justified mathematically, while the remaining range is covered by analytic estimates. Together, these ingredients yield a proof for trees of every order.

</details>


### [59] [Degree sequences of spectrally extremal connected nonregular graphs with prescribed maximum degree: the dense case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02272)
*Zejun Huang,Chenxi Yang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在最大度Δ=n-k（k≥4固定，n充分大）的稠密情形下，具有最大谱半径的n顶点连通非正则图的度序列，完整刻画了其度序列分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Liu和Li（2008）猜想：在3≤Δ≤n-2范围内，具有最大谱半径的n顶点连通非正则图G的度序列为(Δ,…,Δ,δ)，其中δ<Δ尽可能大。该猜想在稠密情形（Δ=n-k）下尚未得到解决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在Δ=n-k（k≥4固定，n充分大）的稠密情形下，通过谱图论方法分析连通非正则图的谱半径，结合度序列的约束条件进行分类讨论，按n与k的奇偶性分别确定最优度序列。

Result: 证明了度序列d(G)满足：当n为偶数且k为奇数时为(Δ,…,Δ,1)；当n为奇数且k为偶数时为(Δ,…,Δ,Δ-1)；当n与k奇偶性相同时为(Δ,…,Δ,2)。

Conclusion: 在稠密情形下完整刻画了最大谱半径连通非正则图的度序列，验证并推广了Liu-Li猜想的相应部分，揭示了度序列结构依赖于n与k奇偶性的规律。

Abstract: Let $G$ be an $n$-vertex connected nonregular graph with maximum degree $Δ$ that attains the maximum spectral radius. For $3\leΔ\le n-2$, Liu and Li (2008) conjectured that $G$ has the degree sequence $(Δ,\ldots,Δ,δ)$ with $δ<Δ$ as large as possible. In this paper, we study the dense case where $Δ=n-k$ with $k\ge4$ fixed and $n$ sufficiently large. We prove that \[ d(G)= \begin{cases} (Δ,\ldots,Δ, 1), & \text{if $n$ is even and $k$ is odd};\\ (Δ,\ldots,Δ,Δ-1), & \text{if $n$ is odd and $k$ is even};\\ (Δ,\ldots,Δ,2), & \text{if $n$ and $k$ have the same parity}. \end{cases} \]

</details>


### [60] [Greedy Uniformity on Trees: Exact Obstruction and Near-Uniform Spiders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02276)
*John Fairfax-Ball*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 随机顶点序下树上的贪心极大独立集算法所诱导的分布一般不等于均匀分布：精确均匀仅发生在一个顶点的树与单边上。证明是结构性的，并借由严格单射论证“刚性”。但偏离可以极小：对显式混合蜘蛛族$T_{k,l}$给出极大独立集计数与每个输出的精确概率，并在$l=2^k-k$时得到总变差偏差$b(T_{k,2^k-k})=O(\sqrt{k}/4^k)=O(\sqrt{\log n_k}/n_k^2)$，$n_k=2^k+k+1$。相关定理已在Lean中形式化并登记于Palomar，属机器检验记录而非同行评审或新颖性证书。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究随机顶点序下贪心极大独立集算法输出分布是否等于极大独立集上的均匀分布，并刻画其偏离结构与可逼近程度。

Method: 结构性证明：由直径端点暴露一个悬挂星；对剩余“一个悬挂叶”的情形，通过在置换纤维间交换悬挂叶与其支撑顶点构造严格单射，证明均匀性仅在单顶点树和单边成立；对混合蜘蛛族$T_{k,l}$显式计数极大独立集并计算每个输出的精确概率；定理在Lean中形式化并登记于Palomar。

Result: 精确均匀性仅对单顶点树和单边成立；对$T_{k,l}$可得精确输出概率，当$l=2^k-k$时总变差偏差为正且$b(T_{k,2^k-k})=O(\sqrt{k}/4^k)=O(\sqrt{\log n_k}/n_k^2)$，其中$n_k=2^k+k+1$。

Conclusion: 贪心极大独立集算法在树上具有“精确均匀刚性”，但可被指数级逼近；混合蜘蛛族给出正偏差且随规模快速衰减的显式例子。形式化验证记录仅为机器检验，不构成同行评审或新颖性认证。

Abstract: Choose a uniformly random ordering of the vertices of a finite tree and run the usual greedy maximal-independent-set algorithm. We compare the resulting law on maximal independent sets with the uniform law. We prove that exact uniformity occurs only for the one-vertex tree and the single edge. The proof is structural: a diameter endpoint exposes a pendant star, and the remaining one-pendant-leaf case is resolved by a strict injection between exact permutation fibres obtained by swapping the pendant leaf with its support vertex. Exact uniformity is therefore rigid, but it can be approached closely. For an explicit mixed-spider family $T_{k,l}$ we count the maximal independent sets and compute the exact probability of every output. With $l=2^k-k$ the total-variation bias is positive and satisfies [
b(T_{k,2^k-k})=O!\left(\frac{\sqrt{k}}{4^k}\right) =O!\left(\frac{\sqrt{\log n_k}}{n_k^2}\right), \qquad n_k=2^k+k+1. ]
The theorem package has also been formalised in Lean and registered with Palomar. These records document machine-checked formal verification and the checked axiom boundary; they are not peer review or a certificate of novelty.

</details>


### [61] [Local Shellings of Broken-Circuit Complexes via Boundary States and the Chromatic Specialization of the Negami Polynomial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02279)
*Iwao Mizukai*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究沿有限边界相接的边不相交图，构造具有给定边界连通性的 NBC 面的相对 shelling；当第一图的边全部先于第二图时，相对 shelling 组合为带连续连通块的 shelling，其 $h$-多项式的非负分解实现了 Negami 分裂恒等式的色多项式特化。此外给出跨边界缝合图 Orlik--Terao Gröbner 基的路径公式，并讨论复杂度与状态多面体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沿边界粘合图的 shelling 与色多项式/Orlik--Terao 代数结构之间的关系尚需统一的组合刻画；同时，显式基枚举与系数计算在固定树宽下的复杂度差异缺乏区分性结果。

Method: 对边不相交图构造相对 NBC 面的相对 shelling，并在辅助有根森林复形的限制面上加入生成森林中的路径；利用边序（第一图先于第二图）将相对 shelling 组合为连续连通块的 shelling；借助状态多面体及其 Cayley 提升刻画线性块序的障碍与提升实现。

Result: 得到 $h$-多项式的非负分解，实现 Negami 分裂恒等式的色特化；给出边界大小为二或三时缝合图 Orlik--Terao Gröbner 基的路径公式及任意边界的 virtual-clique 消去公式；在树宽为二处得到指数级输出下界。

Conclusion: 相对 shelling 提供了沿边界粘合图 NBC 复形的统一框架，并揭示组合 shelling、代数 Gröbner 基与色多项式恒等式之间的深层联系；树宽二的指数下界区分了显式基枚举与固定树宽下的系数计算。

Abstract: For edge-disjoint graphs meeting along a finite boundary, we construct relative shellings of NBC faces with prescribed boundary connectivity. The construction adds paths in spanning forests to the restriction faces of auxiliary rooted-forest complexes. When every edge of the first graph precedes every edge of the second, these relative shellings combine into a shelling with consecutive connectivity blocks. The resulting nonnegative decomposition of the $h$-polynomial realizes the chromatic specialization of the Negami splitting identity. We also give path formulas for gluing graphic Orlik--Terao Gröbner bases across boundaries of size two or three, and a virtual-clique elimination formula for arbitrary boundaries. An exponential output lower bound at treewidth two distinguishes explicit basis enumeration from coefficient computation at fixed treewidth. Finally, state polytopes and their Cayley lifts describe an obstruction to linear block orders in the original coordinates and a lifted realization of these orders.

</details>


### [62] [A linear gap for the maximal cp-rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02289)
*Yair Lavi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了最大cp-秩$p_n$的下界：奇数$n\ge5$时$p_n\ge n(n-3)/2$，偶数$n\ge6$时$p_n\ge n(n-3)/2-1$，结合已知上界$p_n\le\binom{n+1}{2}-4$，得到$p_n=n^2/2+O(n)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: cp-秩（完全正秩）的极值问题长期缺乏精确刻画，最大cp-秩$p_n$的确切值此前未知。本文旨在缩小上下界之间的差距，确定$p_n$的渐近阶。

Method: 通过构造性方法给出$p_n$的下界估计，分别处理奇数$n$与偶数$n$的情形，并与已有上界$p_n\le\binom{n+1}{2}-4$进行匹配分析。

Result: 对奇数$n\ge5$，$p_n\ge n(n-3)/2$；对偶数$n\ge6$，$p_n\ge n(n-3)/2-1$。结合上界可得$p_n=n^2/2+O(n)$。

Conclusion: 最大cp-秩的渐近阶被确定为$n^2/2$量级，$p_n=n^2/2+O(n)$，显著推进了该极值问题的理解。

Abstract: We prove that the maximal cp-rank $p_n$ satisfies $p_n\ge n(n-3)/2$ for odd $n\ge5$ and $p_n\ge n(n-3)/2-1$ for even $n\ge6$. Together with the known upper bound $p_n\le\binom{n+1}{2}-4$, this gives $p_n=n^2/2+O(n)$.

</details>


### [63] [Beyond Liu's 0.382709 threshold for the union-closed sets conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02295)
*Simone Costa,Ankan Sadhu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文在Liu的框架下采用不同协议（概率测度族），结合独立协议与Liu的Example 5，证明了并封闭集猜想的下界 \(c_{\mathrm{UC}}\ge c_{\mathrm L}>0.382709087918735\)，并进一步优化得到 \(c_{\mathrm{UC}}\ge 0.3828852549667978\)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并封闭集猜想断言：任何含非空集的有限并封闭族中，存在一个元素至少出现在一半成员中。此前最佳下界由Liu给出，高于0.3823455，其数值优化提示更强阈值 \(c_{\mathrm L}\)。本文旨在改进此下界。

Method: 在Liu的框架内使用不同的协议（合适的概率测度族），将独立协议与Liu的Example 5相结合；随后改变Example 5中的参数并优化两系数组合。

Result: 证明了 \(c_{\mathrm{UC}}\ge c_{\mathrm L}>0.382709087918735\)，并进一步得到 \(c_{\mathrm{UC}}\ge 0.3828852549667978\)；同时指出该证明所用不等式无法给出大于 \(0.3828852599667\ldots\) 的阈值。

Conclusion: 通过协议组合与参数优化，将Liu提出的数值阈值 \(c_{\mathrm L}\) 转化为严格下界，并确定了该方法对应不等式的阈值上限。

Abstract: The union-closed sets conjecture asks whether every finite union-closed family containing a nonempty set has an element contained in at least half of its members. Let $c_{\mathrm{UC}}$ denote the largest universal lower bound on this proportion. The best previously proved lower bound is due to Liu and is above $0.3823455$; his own numerical optimization suggested a stronger threshold $c_{\mathrm L}>0.382709087918735$. The main argument of this paper is to use different protocols (suitable families of probability measures) within Liu's framework. In particular, we combine the independent protocol with Liu's Example 5 to prove the conjectured bound $c_{\mathrm{UC}}\ge c_{\mathrm L}$. We then vary the parameter in Example 5 and optimize the choice of the two coefficients in the combination to obtain $c_{\mathrm{UC}}\ge 0.3828852549667978$. We also show that the inequality used in this proof cannot give a threshold larger than $0.3828852599667\ldots$.

</details>


### [64] [The Boolean lattice $\mathcal{B}_6$ is not a sphere order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02329)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球序（sphere order）是偏序集可表示性问题中的一类重要概念，此前缺乏明确的有限反例，作者希望给出一个具体可验证的非球序偏序集实例。

Method: 建立并利用偏序集的球序表示与闵可夫斯基空间嵌入之间的等价性，对布尔格 B6 进行分析论证。

Result: 证明了布尔格 B6 不是球序，给出首个显式的、有限的非球序偏序集例子。

Conclusion: 布尔格 B6 构成球序表示性的反例，说明并非所有有限偏序集都是球序，为该方向的进一步研究提供了基准实例。

Abstract: Using the equivalence of sphere order representations of posets with Minkowski space embeddings, we show that the Boolean lattice $\mathcal{B}_6$ is not a sphere order. This is the first explicit finite example of a poset that is not a sphere order.

</details>


### [65] [Note on near-spanning balanced antidirected trees missing from regular tournaments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02390)
*Gregory Gutin,Yiming Hao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 反例构造证明：满足半度/伪半度严格阈值的正则顶点传递竞赛图，仍不能嵌入近生成平衡反定向树，推翻Stein综述中两个猜想的均匀次线性度解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究竞赛图中反定向树嵌入的度条件，检验半度及伪半度猜想在亚线性度目标下的均匀解释是否成立。

Method: 对每个充分大奇整数n，构造n阶正则顶点传递竞赛图，配以n-1阶、最大度至多(1+o(1))n/log_2 n的平衡反定向毛虫树，验证宿主满足k/2严格半度与伪半度阈值但不含该树；并用Paley竞赛图给出具体小例。

Result: 构造出反例：正则顶点传递竞赛图满足严格k/2半度及伪半度阈值，却不含该近生成反定向树；得到n-1阶目标最优；六顶点平衡反定向双星不在七阶Paley竞赛图中，且七为固定双星的伪半度反例最小宿主阶。

Conclusion: 该族反例推翻Stein(2024)综述中猜想6.8和7.6的均匀次线性度解释；n-1阶在严格半度假设下最优，七阶为固定双星伪半度反例的最小宿主阶。

Abstract: We construct near-spanning balanced antidirected trees that fail to embed in highly symmetric tournaments despite the conjectured semidegree condition being satisfied. More precisely, for every sufficiently large odd integer $n$, we exhibit a regular vertex-transitive tournament on $n$ vertices and a balanced antidirected caterpillar on $n-1$ vertices with maximum degree at most $(1+o(1))n/\log_2 n$ that is not contained in the tournament. The host satisfies the strict $k/2$ threshold in both the semidegree and pseudo-semidegree settings, where $k$ is the number of arcs of the target tree. Consequently, this gives counterexamples to the uniform sublinear-degree interpretations of Conjectures~6.8 and~7.6 in Stein's survey (2024). The order $n-1$ of the target is best possible under the strict semidegree hypothesis. We also give a six-vertex balanced antidirected double-star missing from the seven-vertex Paley tournament, and show that seven is the smallest host order for a pseudo-semidegree counterexample with this fixed double-star.

</details>


### [66] [Hilbert fragments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02450)
*Csaba Biró,André E. Kézdy,Jen\Ho Lehel*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出"Hilbert fragment"概念，即区间序长度多面体Hilbert基支撑的相交图的诱导子图，并用公理化的"track-labeling"对其进行刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作证明区间序的长度多面体有唯一Hilbert基且基元素为二元向量，其支撑构成集合系统，相交图为余可比图。由此自然产生问题：哪些图可作为此类Hilbert基支撑的相交图出现。

Method: 定义Hilbert fragments为这些相交图的诱导子图，构成一类遗传的余可比图类，记录区间序长度多面体Hilbert基元素支撑间的相交模式；通过公理化的顶点标注（track-labeling）对其进行刻画。

Result: 给出了Hilbert fragments的track-labeling刻画，并通过应用展示即使Hilbert fragments本身不是区间图，该刻画也能揭示其潜在的一维结构。

Conclusion: track-labeling刻画为识别Hilbert fragments的内在结构提供了有效工具，凸显其隐含的一维性质。

Abstract: In earlier work, we proved that the length polyhedron of an interval order has a unique Hilbert basis and that every element of this basis is a binary vector. The supports of these binary vectors form a set system whose intersection graph is a cocomparability graph. This leads naturally to the question of which graphs can arise as intersection graphs of such Hilbert-basis supports. We introduce Hilbert fragments, defined as induced subgraphs of these intersection graphs. Thus Hilbert fragments form a hereditary class of cocomparability graphs recording the intersection patterns among the supports of Hilbert-basis elements of interval-order length polyhedra. We characterize Hilbert fragments by an axiomatically defined vertex-labeling, called a track-labeling, and give applications illustrating how this characterization can reveal an underlying one-dimensional structure even when the Hilbert fragments themselves are not interval graphs.

</details>


### [67] [Pattern restricted parking functions with limited preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02489)
*Kassie Archer,Robert Laudone*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文定义停车函数的模式避免概念，并枚举避免特定模式的 2-limited 停车函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 停车函数中每个元素出现次数不超过 ℓ 时称为 ℓ-limited。本文希望将经典的模式避免工具引入停车函数研究，从而在受限重复次数的情形下进行计数。

Method: 对停车函数定义模式避免（pattern avoidance）概念，然后针对 2-limited 停车函数，枚举避免某些给定模式的那些对象。

Result: 给出了避免特定模式的 2-limited 停车函数的枚举结果（具体计数公式未在摘要中列出）。

Conclusion: 模式避免可自然推广到停车函数，并在 ℓ-limited（此处 ℓ=2）条件下得到可枚举的计数结论。

Abstract: A parking function is said to have $\ell$-limited preferences if there are at most $\ell$ copies of each element in the parking function. In this paper, we define a notion of pattern avoidance for parking functions and enumerate those $2$-limited parking functions that avoid certain patterns.

</details>


### [68] [Burning Signed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02506)
*Shaun Fallat,Himanshu Gupta,Kamyar Khodamoradi,Sandra Zilles*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出并研究符号图上的双色竞争燃烧模型（黄/绿两火），玩家每步按所选颜色点燃新顶点，火沿边传播时由边的布尔符号决定是否变色；目标为最大化单一颜色烧毁的顶点数，称复多数（plurality number）。主要结果是用与符号图关联的二元码的Hamming距离刻画该量，并给出符号路径的精确值与多种问题的硬度结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典图燃烧模型只涉及单一火源，缺少竞争与"颜色对抗"的机制。本文引入符号图的布尔符号来刻画传播过程中的颜色翻转，从而将竞争性燃烧与编码理论联系起来，并探究该新参数的精确计算与近似难度。

Method: 在符号图Γ上定义双色竞争燃烧过程：每步一方按任意颜色点燃一个新顶点，原有火继续按边传播，边符号为真/假决定传播时是否变色。将该过程与Γ关联的二元码联系，利用Hamming距离和覆盖半径（covering radius）刻画参数；进一步对符号路径在特定signature条件下求解精确值，并对判定/近似复多数问题的多个变体做复杂度归约证明。

Result: 复多数可用到关联二元码的Hamming距离表示；Γ的切换类（switching class）上的最小复多数等于顶点数减去该码的覆盖半径。对满足特定signature条件的符号路径，给出了复多数的精确值；同时证明多个判定或近似复多数问题的变体为困难问题（hardness结果）。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了一类新的竞争性符号图燃烧模型，把复多数与二元码的Hamming距离/覆盖半径等价联系起来，从而在符号路径等特殊结构上得到精确结果，同时在一般情形下给出计算与近似的硬度下界，说明该参数兼具结构可解性与一般不可解性。

Abstract: We introduce and analyze a new model of graph burning, in which two competing fires (coloured yellow and green) ignite vertices of a given signed graph and propagate along its edges. The boolean sign of an edge determines whether a fire spreading along the edge changes colour or not. In each step, a player ignites a new vertex in a colour of their choice, while previously activated fires continue to spread. Given a signed graph $Γ$, the objective is to burn the maximum possible number of vertices in a single colour; the optimal achievable value is called the plurality number of $Γ$. We express the plurality number through Hamming distances to a binary code associated with $Γ$. In particular, the minimum plurality number over the switching class of $Γ$ equals the number of vertices minus the covering radius of this code.
  Under certain conditions on the signature, we determine exact values of the plurality number of signed paths. By contrast, we prove hardness results for multiple variants of the problem of determining or approximating the plurality number.

</details>


### [69] [123-Avoiding permutations with an adjacency constraint](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02526)
*Nathaniel Nadler*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究满足相邻差约束且避免123模式的排列，证明其被限制在反对角线附近，用有限状态机与转移矩阵给出精确枚举，并分析增长率的极限行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将模式避免（避免123）与相邻元素差的界约束结合，考察两种限制交互后产生的全局结构，并探讨该约束是否破坏经典Wilf等价性。

Method: 证明每个可行排列位于反对角线垂直距离m之内；对固定m构造恰有2^{m+1}-m-1个可实现状态的有限状态描述，建立转移矩阵与有理生成函数；用转移矩阵谱半径刻画指数增长率；并给出算法与图论解释（与路径图幂中受约束Hamilton路径的对应）。

Result: 枚举序列满足最终常系数线性递推，每个固定m有有理生成函数；指数增长率为谱半径ρ_m，满足ρ_m<4、随m非减且趋于Catalan增长常数4；有界相邻约束破坏普通长度三的Wilf等价。

Conclusion: 模式避免与有界相邻的交互带来强全局限制与有限状态可枚举性，增长率随界放宽单调趋近Catalan常数，且该附加约束改变了Wilf等价结构。

Abstract: We study permutations $π\in S_n$ that avoid $123$ while satisfying the adjacency constraint $\lvert π_{i+1}-π_i\rvert \le m$ for some $m\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}$. We first show that every admissible permutation is localized within vertical distance $m$ of the reverse diagonal, revealing a strong global restriction imposed by the interaction between pattern avoidance and bounded adjacency. For every fixed $m$, we then construct an exact finite-state description of the family, with precisely $2^{m+1}-m-1$ realizable states. This yields an explicit transfer-matrix enumeration and, in particular, a rational generating function for every fixed $m$; consequently, the enumeration sequence satisfies an eventual constant-coefficient linear recurrence. We further identify the exponential growth rate with the spectral radius $ρ_m$ of the corresponding transfer matrix and show that $ρ_m<4$, is nondecreasing in $m$, and converges to the Catalan growth constant $4$ as $m\to\infty$. Finally, we show that the bounded-adjacency constraint breaks the ordinary length-three Wilf equivalence, and we develop algorithmic and graph-theoretic interpretations of the family, including a correspondence with constrained Hamiltonian paths in powers of the path graph.

</details>


### [70] [Shuffle automata and the growth of 1324-avoiding permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02535)
*Robert Brignall*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文证明模式类 Av(1324) 的增长率上界为 13.167248，方法是将 domino 方法与 Bóna 的装饰词对技巧结合，并用 shuffle 自动机处理交错。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进 Av(1324) 增长率的已知上界。已有两类方法：Bevan 等基于 domino 与任意交错的方法，以及 Bóna 基于带附加限制的装饰词对的方法。本文试图融合二者以得到更紧的上界。

Method: 以 domino 方法为基础，将 Bevan 等人的任意交错替换为更接近 Bóna 形式的交错；引入 shuffle 自动机来处理这些交错；利用 domino 上的两个统计量——内部点数目（既非左到右最小也非右到左最大的点）与内部点连续段（runs）数目——来估计可能的 shuffle 数量。

Result: 得到 gr(Av(1324)) ≤ 13.167248，优于此前上界。作者指出所用 shuffle 自动机方法有潜力给出比本文更强的上界。

Conclusion: 通过 domino 与受限交错（经 shuffle 自动机处理）的结合，获得了 Av(1324) 增长率的新上界；该框架是可推广的，未来可能进一步改进上界。

Abstract: We show that $\text{gr}(\text{Av}(1324))\leq 13.167248$. This is achieved by combining the approach of Bevan, Brignall, Elvey Price and Pantone using the `domino', with ideas from earlier upper bounds by Bóna using pairs of decorated words with additional restrictions. More specifically, we replace the arbitrary interleavings of Bevan et. al. with interleavings more like those of Bóna. Our method uses shuffle automata to handle the interleavings, and this method has the potential to provide better upper bounds than the one established here. To estimate how many shuffles are possible, we use two key statistics on dominoes: the number of internal points (that is, points that are neither left-to-right minima not right-to-left maxima), and the number of runs of internal points.

</details>


### [71] [Homomesy of Tropical $T$-systems: Finite Type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02548)
*Ariana Chin,Pavlo Pylyavskyy*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究双分簇变异（bipartite belt）的tropical动力学，证明红蓝变异统计具有homomesy性质，并由此解决第一作者的一个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fomin与Zelevinsky于2007年提出的bipartite belt构成离散动力系统，对每个Dynkin图该系统中Zamolodchikov周期性而周期化。其tropical对应动力学中，沿belt的每次变异可按tropical交换关系中取最大值的项染成红或蓝，由此得到tropical轨道上的红蓝统计量。作者希望确定该统计量是否与轨道无关（homomesy），并验证第一作者提出的猜想。

Method: 在tropical交换关系中对每次变异按取最大值的项进行红/蓝着色，定义tropical轨道上的红蓝统计量；证明该统计量的homomesy性质；在此过程中引入Zamolodchikov fan，并说明其与正tropical Grassmannian的密切关系。

Result: 证明了红蓝统计量是homomesic的，即红色变异数与蓝色变异数的平均值均与轨道无关；并且红色变异的数目等于相关联根系中根的数目。

Conclusion: 该结果解决了第一作者的猜想，同时引入的Zamolodchikov fan与正tropical Grassmannian密切相关，可能具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: In 2007, Fomin and Zelevinsky introduced the bipartite belt, a sequence of bipartite cluster mutations whose exchange relations form a discrete dynamical system. For each Dynkin diagram, this system is periodic, as a special case of Zamolodchikov periodicity. In the associated tropical dynamics, every mutation along the belt can be naturally colored red or blue, according to which term attains the maximum in the tropical exchange relation. This gives a red-blue statistic on tropical orbits. We prove that this statistic is homomesic, i.e. that the average numbers of red and blue mutations are independent of the orbit. Moreover, the number of red mutations is given by the number of roots in the associated root system. This resolves a conjecture of the first author. In the process we introduce the Zamolodchikov fan, which is closely related to the positive tropical Grassmannian, and may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [72] [A Density Method for Bounding Heesch Numbers in $\mathbb{E}^3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02579)
*Aleksa Džuklevski*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过两个垂直方向上截面的平铺比例q比较限制棱柱Heesch数的方法，证明某类三维棱柱具有有限Heesch数，并给出计算与否定结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 限制特定图块族的Heesch数是平铺理论中的重要问题，但三维情形缺乏有效的结构性方法，作者希望借助比例q平铺的频率分析来处理此类问题。

Method: 对棱柱沿两个垂直方向取截面与冠状环，比较所得原型图块频率；若这些图块的所有平铺的极限比例至少为q>1，则棱柱的足够大冠状环在两侧需要不相容的图块计数，从而限制Heesch数；同时辅以计算搜索。

Result: 证明某类三维棱柱具有有限Heesch数，给出6个缺口棱柱的无限族，其Heesch数有限且至少为2，并报告若干关于具有给定Heesch数的棱柱不存在性的否定计算结论。

Conclusion: 该比例q截面比较方法为三维图块Heesch数提供了新的结构性工具，初步结果兼顾理论证明与计算验证，并为进一步研究非存在性提供依据。

Abstract: In this paper, we provide a novel approach to limiting Heesch numbers of a specific family of tiles. In particular, we show how a certain family of three--dimensional prisms has a finite Heesch number. In so doing, we initiate a structural study of ratio $q$ tilings by two prototiles, in which, loosely speaking, one tile appears $q$ times more often than the other. The method works as follows: we take planar intersections with coronas around the prism in two perpendicular directions and compare the frequencies of the resulting prototiles in those patches. If every tiling by these prototiles has limiting ratio at least $q >1$, then sufficiently large coronas of the prism would require incompatible tile counts in the two directions. We also provide some computational results and exhibit $6$ infinite families of notched prisms that have finite Heesch numbers greater or equal to $2$. Finally, we provide some negative computational results about the non--existence of certain prisms with given Heesch numbers.

</details>


### [73] [Positive discrepancy of graphs far from Turán graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02681)
*Leyou Xu,Bo Zhou*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了远离Turán图的n顶点图具有正discrpancy至少c_ε n^{5/4}，进而其正则图的第二特征值至少c'_ε n^{1/4}，解决了Räty、Sudakov和Tomon的两个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Turán图是极值图论的核心对象，刻画"接近Turán图"与谱/组合性质之间的关系具有基础意义。Räty、Sudakov和Tomon提出了关于远离Turán图的正discrpancy下界及对应正则图第二特征值下界的猜想，本文旨在证明这两个猜想。

Method: 证明思路为：先建立"需要至少εn²次边修改才能成为Turán图"这一距离条件与正discrpancy下界之间的联系，证明该discrpancy至少为c_ε n^{5/4}；再将该discrpancy结果转化为正则图的第二特征值下界，得到至少c'_ε n^{1/4}。

Result: 主要结果有两项：(1) 对任意ε>0，任何需至少εn²次边修改才能变为Turán图的n顶点图，其正discrpancy至少为c_ε n^{5/4}；(2) 由此推出此类正则图的第二特征值至少为c'_ε n^{1/4}。

Conclusion: 本文证明了Räty、Sudakov和Tomon提出的两个猜想，建立了远离Turán图与正discrpancy、第二特征值之间的定量联系。

Abstract: We prove that, for every $\eps>0$, any $n$-vertex graph that needs at least $\eps n^2$ edge changes to become a Turán graph has positive discrepancy at least $c_\eps n^{5/4}$. Consequently, every such regular graph has second eigenvalue at least $c'_\eps n^{1/4}$. These results prove two conjectures of Räty, Sudakov and Tomon.

</details>


### [74] [Typical growth of the Füredi-Hajnal and Stanley-Wilf limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02707)
*Jesse Geneson*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了随机置换矩阵的Füredi-Hajnal极限与Stanley-Wilf极限以趋于1的概率不超过exp(O(√k(log k)^{5/2}))，改进了Cibulka与Kynčl的界，并结合Fox的下界说明几乎对所有置换，两极限的对数均为k^{1/2+o(1)}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Füredi-Hajnal极限与Stanley-Wilf极限是置换模式（pattern avoidance）研究中的核心量，刻画了置换矩阵中最长递增/递减型结构及模式避免计数增长率。对随机置换矩阵给出更紧的高概率上界，是理解这些极限典型阶（typical order）的关键问题；此前最优上界为Cibulka–Kynčl的exp(O(k^{2/3}(log k)^{7/3}/(log log k)^{1/3}))，仍远离下界所暗示的k^{1/2+o(1)}量级。

Method: 针对均匀随机k阶置换矩阵，通过概率/组合方法建立两极限的高概率上界估计，结合Fox已有的下界进行匹配分析，得到对数阶的k^{1/2+o(1)}刻画。

Result: 以趋于1的概率，随机置换矩阵的Füredi-Hajnal极限与Stanley-Wilf极限均不超过exp(O(√k(log k)^{5/2}))，显著改进了此前exp(O(k^{2/3}(log k)^{7/3}/(log log k)^{1/3}))的界。

Conclusion: 结合Fox的下界，两极限的对数对几乎所有置换均为k^{1/2+o(1)}，确定了随机置换矩阵下这些极限的典型阶。

Abstract: We prove that the Füredi-Hajnal limit and the Stanley-Wilf limit of a uniformly random permutation matrix of order $k$ are at most $\exp\bigl(O(\sqrt{k}(\log k)^{5/2})\bigr)$ with probability tending to one as $k\to\infty$. This improves the bound $\exp\bigl(O(k^{2/3}(\log k)^{7/3}/(\log\log k)^{1/3})\bigr)$ of Cibulka and Kynčl. Together with the lower bound due to Fox, these bounds show that the logarithms of both limits are $k^{1/2+o(1)}$ for almost all permutations.

</details>


### [75] [Motzkin Numbers Count 2-Stack-Sortable Permutations Ending in Their Least Entry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02756)
*Ryota Inagaki,Michael Luo*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明 Zhang 猜想：以 0 结尾的 2-stack-sortable 排列数为 Motzkin 数，通过构造到宽度≤3 标准 Young 表之间的双射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zhang 提出猜想（Conjecture 6.1），断言以最小元结尾的 2-stack-sortable 排列计数为 Motzkin 数，但此前缺乏证明。本文旨在验证该猜想并揭示其组合对应关系。

Method: 利用 Zhang 的结论，建立以最小元结尾的 2-stack-sortable 排列与宽度至多为 2 的标准 composition tableaux 之间的双射；随后以双射方式证明这类 composition tableaux 与宽度至多为 3 的标准 Young tableaux 数量相等，而后者已知由 Motzkin 数计数。

Result: 对任意 n≥0，以 0 结尾的 {0,1,…,n} 的 2-stack-sortable 排列数目等于第 n 个 Motzkin 数；并通过显式双射将其与宽度≤3 的标准 Young 表联系起来。

Conclusion: Zhang 的 Conjecture 6.1 成立，2-stack-sortable 排列与 Motzkin 数之间存在新的双射解释。

Abstract: We prove the following conjecture of Zhang (arXiv:2604.10779, Conjecture 6.1): for $n \geq 0$, the number of $2$-stack-sortable permutations of $\{0,1,\dots,n\}$ ending in $0$ is the $n$th Motzkin number. By Zhang's result, there is a bijection between $2$-stack-sortable permutations ending in their least element and standard composition tableaux of width at most $2$. We then show bijectively that there are an equal number of these and standard Young tableaux of width at most $3$, which are known to be counted by the Motzkin numbers.

</details>


### [76] [Resolving Erdős-Ulam Monochromatic Union-Closed Family Conjectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02833)
*Deep Bhattacharjee,Priyabrata Mandal,Ushashi Bhattacharya*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了关于单色并封闭族的两个Erdős-Ulam猜想：任意二着色下存在规模增长快于任意固定幂次的单色并封闭族，而适当着色下不存在指数规模的单色并封闭族；结论对任意颜色数成立，并在Lean中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Erdős-Ulam猜想关注有限集子集的着色问题，核心在于单色并封闭族在规模上能达到的下界与上界。本文旨在完整解决该方向上的两个猜想，明确其规模增长的量级界限，并给出机器可验证的严格证明。

Method: 对有限集的子集进行多着色分析，构造并证明存在性下界：任意二着色均包含规模超多项式增长的单色并封闭族；同时构造特定着色以给出指数规模上界的不可能性。将上述论证推广至任意颜色数，并使用Lean证明助手完成形式化验证。

Result: 得到两个互补结论：下界方面，任意二着色下存在大小超过任意固定幂次的单色并封闭族；上界方面，存在着色使得不存在指数规模的单色并封闭族。两个结果对任意颜色数均成立，且均通过Lean验证。

Conclusion: 该工作完整证明了Erdős-Ulam关于单色并封闭族的两个猜想，刻画了单色并封闭族规模增长的多项式与指数之间的量级分界，并以形式化证明增强了结论的可靠性。

Abstract: We prove both Erdős-Ulam conjectures on monochromatic union-closed families. Every two-colouring of the subsets of a finite set contains a monochromatic union-closed family whose size grows faster than any fixed power of the size of the set, while suitable colourings admit no monochromatic union-closed family of exponential size. Both results hold for any number of colours and are verified in Lean.

</details>


### [77] [A finite threshold for the double-comet conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02913)
*Dongxiu Cai,Zhenbo Chen,Xiao-Dong Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明阶数 n≥200 的最小邻接谱隙树必为平衡双彗星，并给出连续时间马尔可夫链中逆谱隙与有效电阻—方差量的偏差界及 Perron 链的精确公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jovović、Koledin 与 Stanić 猜想：固定阶数下平衡双彗星在所有树中取得最小邻接谱隙。该猜想此前未被证明，本文旨在证明大阶数情形下的结论，并建立谱隙与马尔可夫链有效电阻之间的定量联系。

Method: 针对树的最小化问题，对 n≥200 的所有极小化树进行结构分析，证明其必为平衡双彗星。针对有限不可约可逆连续时间马尔可夫链，利用 Dirichlet 原理与杀死过程，将逆谱隙与有效电阻乘以命中概率的平稳方差进行比较，得到以杀死特征值的逆 Dirichlet 谱隙为上界的偏差。对 Perron 链，通过两顶点 Schur 补推导出电阻—方差量的精确公式。

Result: 证明了对 n≥200，所有最小化邻接谱隙的树都是平衡双彗星，从而在该阶数范围内验证了猜想。对一般有限不可约可逆连续时间马尔可夫链，证明了逆谱隙与（两状态间有效电阻）×（命中概率的平稳方差）之差不超过在这些状态处杀死过程的逆 Dirichlet 谱隙；对 Perron 链给出了该电阻—方差量的两顶点 Schur 补精确表达式。

Conclusion: 在大阶数下证实了最小邻接谱隙树的平衡双彗星结构猜想，并揭示了连续时间马尔可夫链逆谱隙与有效电阻—方差量之间的精细联系，为谱图理论与马尔可夫链混合时间分析提供了新工具。

Abstract: For a tree $T$, let $g(T)=λ_1(T)-λ_2(T)$ be the difference between its two largest adjacency eigenvalues. A balanced double comet is obtained by attaching equally many leaves to the two endpoints of a path. Jovović, Koledin and Stanić conjectured that such a tree attains the minimum adjacency spectral gap among trees of any fixed order. We prove that every minimizing tree of order $n\ge200$ is a balanced double comet. We also show that, for any finite irreducible reversible continuous-time Markov chain, the inverse spectral gap differs from the effective resistance between two states times the stationary variance of their hitting probability by at most the inverse Dirichlet gap for killing at those states. For Perron chains, we give an exact two-vertex Schur-complement formula for this resistance--variance quantity.

</details>


### [78] [Strict spectral supersaturation for cliques: extremal graphs and sharp thresholds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03337)
*Qi Wu,Yong Lu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文刻画了给定顶点数 n、团数少于 q·c_r(n) 的图的极大邻接谱半径，并确定极值图结构与严格/非严格阈值差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极值图论中，Turán 型问题与谱极值问题的交叉研究备受关注：对禁止过多 K_{r+1} 的图，其邻接谱半径上限及其极值结构尚需精确刻画。

Method: 结合稳定性分析与谱技术，按 q=o(n)、q/m→γ∈(0,1)、q/m→1 三种范围分别证明；对非严格约束构造 Turán 图加 q-边星，对严格约束构造几乎平衡完全多部图并在某部分加星，并单独处理小 q 值与 r=3,n≡2 mod 3 的额外过渡。

Result: 对固定 r≥3 及充分大 n，确定了 K_{r+1} 副本少于 q·c_r(n)（1≤q<n/r）时最大邻接谱半径，并给出全部极值图；大多数情形极值图为几乎平衡完全多部图加一星，非严格约束下唯一极值图为 Turán 图最大部加 q-边星；给出严格与非严格值之差，并证明匹配阈值恰为 √2(r-1)/r。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了受限团数下的谱 Turán 问题，揭示了极值结构的多样性及严格约束引起的相变，并确定了匹配阈值的精确值。

Abstract: For every fixed $r\ge3$ and all sufficiently large $n$, we determine the largest adjacency spectral radius of an $n$-vertex graph with fewer than $q c_r(n)$ copies of $K_{r+1}$, where $1\le q<n/r$. Here $c_r(n)$ is the number of copies created by adding one edge to a largest part of the Turán graph. We also determine all extremal graphs. In most cases the extremal graph is obtained from an almost balanced complete multipartite graph by adding a star in one part. Two small values of $q$ require separate constructions, and an additional transition occurs when $r=3$ and $n\equiv2\pmod3$. Under the non-strict constraint, the unique extremal graph is obtained by adding a $q$-edge star to a largest part of the Turán graph. We determine the difference between the strict and non-strict values and prove that the sharp matching threshold is $\sqrt2(r-1)/r$. The proof treats separately the ranges $q=o(n)$, $q/m\toγ\in(0,1)$, and $q/m\to1$.

</details>


### [79] [Coordinate-extension degrees and layered $k$-uniform hypergraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03347)
*Jiabao Yang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入坐标扩展度 π_t^{ext}(F) 并证明其为零当且仅当 F 是 t-层化的，推广了 Reiher–Rödl–Schacht 与 Lamaison 的刻画；同时构造了使 π_{k-2}=2(r-1)/(rk^k) 的有限 k-图，给出单禁 k-图的累积点 2/k^k，并否定 Lin–Wang–Zhou 的一个猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一并推广超图 Turán 密度中若干刻画（层化、消失阶、min/max-层化），需要一个新的定量参数来衡量 palette 在固定部分坐标后的扩展概率，从而回答 Lamaison 关于 3-图的公开问题，并厘清 π_{k-2} 的取值结构与累积点现象。

Method: 对 k-palette (C,T) 定义第 t 坐标扩展度：在所有 t 个坐标选择与配色下，剩余 k-t 个坐标可补全为容许 k-元组的赋值比例的最小值；对 k-图 F 取所有不被 F 容许的 palette 上该比例的上确界，得 π_t^{ext}(F)。随后用层化/消失阶等组合结构性质进行等价刻画，并通过构造有限 k-图 F_{k,r} 与特定匹配构造来计算密度下界。

Result: 证明 π_t^{ext}(F)=0 当且仅当 F 为 t-层化，并把 t-层化与消失阶、min-层化、max-层化、层化建立联系，恢复并扩展既有刻画且回答 Lamaison 的 3-图问题。t=0 时 π_0^{ext}(F)=π_{k-2}(F)。对任意 k≥3、r≥2 构造有限 k-图 F_{k,r} 使 π_{k-2}(F_{k,r})=2(r-1)/(rk^k)，故 2/k^k 是单禁 k-图的累积点；还证明违反 Lin–Wang–Zhou 条件 Sp 的 k-图最小密度为 4/(3k^k)，并构造对任意坐标对满足 Sp、无消失阶、密度为 2^m/k^k 的 k-图，否定其猜想（k≥3）。

Conclusion: 坐标扩展度 π_t^{ext}(F) 为超图层化结构提供了统一且定量的刻画：其消失等价于 t-层化，并在 t=0 时退化为 (k-2)-均匀 Turán 密度。构造结果揭示了 π_{k-2} 在单禁 k-图下的累积点 2/k^k 与条件 Sp 的阈值 4/(3k^k)，同时以反例否定了 Lin–Wang–Zhou 的猜想。

Abstract: Let $\Palt=(\C,\T)$ be a $k$-palette. For $0\le t\le k-1$, its $t$th coordinate-extension degree is the minimum, over every choice of $t$ coordinates and every assignment of colors to them, of the proportion of assignments to the remaining $k-t$ coordinates that complete the fixed colors to an admissible $k$-tuple. For a $k$-graph $F$, we define $π_t^{\ext}(F)$ as the supremum of this degree over all palettes not admitted by $F$. We prove that \[ π_t^{\ext}(F)=0 \quad\text{if and only if}\quad F\text{ is }t\text{-layered}. \] We also relate $t$-layeredness to vanishing orders, min-layeredness, max-layeredness, and layeredness. These results recover and extend previous characterizations of Reiher, Rödl, and Schacht and of Lamaison, and answer a question of Lamaison for $3$-graphs. At $t=0$, the parameter $π_0^{\ext}(F)$ is the $(k-2)$-uniform Turán density $π_{k-2}(F)$. For every $k\ge3$ and $r\ge2$, we construct a finite $k$-graph $F_{k,r}$ with \( π_{k-2}(F_{k,r})=2(r-1)/rk^k. \) Thus $2/k^k$ is an accumulation point for single forbidden $k$-graphs. We also show that the least density of a $k$-graph that fails condition $\Sp$ of Lin, Wang and Zhou is $4/(3k^k)$. Finally, for every admissible matching of size $m$, we construct a $k$-graph that satisfies $\Sp$ for every coordinate pair, has no vanishing order, and has density $2^m/k^k$. This disproves a conjecture of Lin, Wang and Zhou for every $k\ge3$.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Gaster、Greene与Vlamis关于曲线图色数工作的启发，研究全Kneser图的对称性，即其自同态与自同构结构。

Method: 类比曲线图色数研究的思路与技术，对全Kneser图的自同态进行刻画，证明所有自同态可逆，并分析其自同构群的结构。

Abstract: Inspired by work of Gaster, Greene, and Vlamis on the chromatic number of curve graphs, we prove that every endomorphism of the total Kneser graph is an automorphism. We also show that the automorphism group of the total Kneser graph is the product of a pair of permutation groups.

</details>


### [81] [Sharp bounds for off-diagonal and tripartite canonical Ramsey numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03358)
*Strahinja Gvozdić,Zach Hunter,Aleksa Milojević,Benny Sudakov*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究规范Ramsey定理的两个变体：多部超图情形下给出K_{t,t,t}^{(3)}规范着色副本的顶点数上界t^{151t^2}并证明指数意义下紧；对非对角规范Ramsey数ER(a,b,c)在多个参数区域给出紧界，并分析规避词典序三角形的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典规范Ramsey定理断言：对任意正整数t，存在足够大的n，使得K_n的任意边着色都含规范着色（单色、彩虹或词典序）的K_t副本。本文旨在探究该定理在两方面的推广与定量刻画：多部超图背景与含多种规范类型目标（单色/词典序/彩虹）的非对角形式，以理解这些极端着色参数间的相互作用及其增长阶。

Method: 1）多部超图：考虑3-一致完全超图的边着色，利用概率方法与随机着色构造下界、结合超图Ramsey型归纳与迭代嵌入技术证明上界。2）非对角规范Ramsey数ER(a,b,c)：通过构造性着色（例如基于分层/字典结构实现下界c^{Ω(ab)}）与递归/压缩论证得到上界2^{O_b(a)}；对b=3的词典序三角形情形，结合着色结构分析与计数论证导出(a-1)(c-2)+O(c^6)。

Result: 在3-一致完全超图K_N（N≥t^{151t^2}）的任意边着色中必存在规范着色的K_{t,t,t}^{(3)}；该估计在指数常数的意义下是紧的。对ER(a,b,c)：当c相对a足够大时ER(a,b,c)≥c^{Ω(ab)}；当b≥4固定且c≤a时ER(a,b,c)≤2^{O_b(a)}，从而在相应参数区域刻画其增长阶。规避词典序三角形时，ER(a,3,c)≤(a-1)(c-2)+O(c^6)。

Conclusion: 规范Ramsey性质在多部超图与非对角多目标设定下均具有可精确刻画的定量行为：三一致多部情形所需顶点数关于t呈双指数级t^{Θ(t^2)}且系数极限意义下紧，非对角ER(a,b,c)在不同参数区域呈现多项式指数（c^{Ω(ab)}）与单变量指数（2^{O_b(a)}）两类增长，词典序三角形边界近乎线性并带低阶多项式修正。

Abstract: The canonical Ramsey theorem establishes that for every positive integer $t$, there is a sufficiently large $n$ such that every edge-colouring of a complete graph $K_n$ contains a copy of $K_t$ that is canonically coloured, i.e. monochromatic, rainbow, or lexicographic. In this paper we investigate two variations of this theorem.
  First, we study canonical Ramsey numbers in the multipartite hypergraph setting. We prove that in every edge-colouring of the 3-uniform complete hypergraph on at least $t^{151t^2}$ vertices, there always exists a canonically coloured copy of $K_{t, t, t}^{(3)}$. This estimate is sharp up to the constant in the exponent.
  Second, we explore off-diagonal canonical Ramsey numbers. Let $ER(a, b, c)$ denote the minimum number of vertices $n$ required to guarantee a monochromatic $K_a$, a lexicographic $K_b$, or a rainbow $K_c$ in every edge-colouring of the complete graph $K_n$. We establish sharp bounds for these numbers across different parameter regimes. Specifically, we prove that $ER(a, b, c)\geq c^{Ω(ab)}$ when $c$ is sufficiently large relative to $a$, and that $ER(a, b, c)\leq 2^{O_b(a)}$ when $b\ge 4$ is a fixed constant and $c\le a$. Finally, we analyse the behaviour of the function when avoiding lexicographic triangles (i.e. $b=3$), showing that $ER(a, 3, c)\le (a-1)(c-2)+O(c^6)$.

</details>

*Alexander Kholosha,Mohit Pal*

TL;DR: 本文证明了两类与向量对偶bent函数相关的正规bent划分的一般构造，涵盖已有构造并产生大量新bent划分。

Conclusion: 该工作为正规bent划分提供了统一的一般构造框架，拓展了bent划分的存在范围，并刻画了相应bent函数的性质。

Abstract: Bent partitions were introduced as a generalization of partial spread construction of bent functions and they became a hot research topic recently. In this paper, we prove two general constructions of normal bent partitions that are related to vectorial dual-bent functions. They cover many of the currently known bent partitions of this type as partial cases. These constructions also provide a large number of new bent partitions. Relevant properties of bent functions obtained from such partitions are proven.

</details>


### [83] [$k$-Pairing: A Generalization of the Partition Pairing Theorems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03437)
*Xiaorui Niu,Diane Y. H. Shi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将 Andrews–Dastidar 的 partition pairing 理论由"成对"推广到"每 k 个相等部分为一组"，构造两个保权双射，给出 pairing index–width 联合分布、negative-rank 枚举的组合解释，并进一步推广到 k 个 Young 图的有序元组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Andrews 与 Dastidar 的 partition pairing 理论仅处理二阶（成对）情形，其 pairing index 与 width 的联合分布、负 pairing rank 的符号消去现象以及奇因子/奇 overpartition 结果缺乏更高阶、更统一的组合解释；同时其 diagonal pairing 构造有待推广到多重分量情形。

Method: 1) 将配对推广为 k 个相等部分的组，定义 k-pairing index 与 width；2) 构造第一个保权双射：结合共轭与 Stockhofe–Keith 对应，将 k-pairing index 和 width 分别映为部分个数与最大部分，得到联合分布由 Gaussian 多项式给出，并给出记录剩余统计量的有限加细；3) 构造第二个双射：将 negative k-pairing rank 的 simply k-paired partitions 映为带内部 gap 标记的 k-regular partitions，分析固定非零剩余类中的符号消去；4) 对 k 个 Young 图的有序元组定义 two-wing transfer 等价关系，分类等价类。

Result: 1) k-pairing index 与 width 的联合分布与 k 无关，由 Gaussian 多项式描述，并获得记录剩余统计量的有限加细；2) 第二个双射将负 k-pairing rank 的 simply k-paired partitions 对应到带标记内部 gap 的 k-regular partitions；在固定非零剩余类中符号消去后仅剩矩形分区，gap 标记对应"最小部分不加 overline"的 overline 选择，从而直接解释除数计数与 overpartition 枚举中的因子 1/2，推广了 Andrews–Dastidar 的奇因子与奇 overpartition 结论；3) 元组等价当且仅当 cell-multiplicity function 相同，每个等价类含唯一嵌套代表元，确定了等价类基数，并将嵌套代表元与矩形形状的 plane partitions 对应。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了 k 阶 partition pairing 的统一组合框架，用两个保权双射分别刻画联合 pairing index–width 分布与 negative-rank 枚举，给出符号消去、除数计数及 1/2 因子的组合解释，并将构造推广到 k 个 Young 图的有序元组，用 cell-multiplicity function 完全分类等价类并将其与矩形 plane partitions 联系起来。

Abstract: We extend the partition pairing theory of Andrews and Dastidar by replacing pairs with groups of $k$ equal parts. Two weight-preserving bijections give combinatorial interpretations of the joint pairing index--width distribution and the negative-rank enumerations. The first combines conjugation with the Stockhofe--Keith correspondence and sends the $k$-pairing index and width to the number of parts and the largest part, respectively. Consequently, their joint distribution is independent of $k$ and is given by a Gaussian polynomial. We also obtain finite refinements that record the residual statistics. The second bijection sends simply $k$-paired partitions of negative $k$-pairing rank to $k$-regular partitions with marked internal gaps. In a fixed nonzero residue class, sign cancellation leaves rectangular partitions, while gap markings correspond to overlining choices with the smallest part not overlined. This gives direct combinatorial explanations of the divisor counts and the factor $1/2$ in the overpartition enumeration, extending the odd-divisor and odd-overpartition results of Andrews and Dastidar. Finally, motivated by the diagonal pairing of Andrews and Dastidar, we extend the construction to ordered tuples of $k$ Young diagrams. Applying the two-wing transfer operation to pairs of component diagrams defines an equivalence relation on these tuples. We show that two tuples are equivalent if and only if they have the same cell-multiplicity function, and that every equivalence class contains a unique representative whose component diagrams are nested. We also determine the cardinalities of the equivalence classes and identify the nested representatives with plane partitions of rectangular shape.

</details>


### [84] [Schur positivity of three-legged spiders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03464)
*David G. L. Wang,Watson Z. Y. Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文完整分类了三足蜘蛛（three-legged spiders）的Schur正性：两条偶腿的蜘蛛均为Schur正；三条奇腿的蜘蛛必含一个负Schur系数；当a偶、b,c奇时，S(a,b,c)为Schur正当且仅当a≤5b+5c+2。Schur正性失效时恰有一个负系数，其值被显式给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望推广Thibon–Wang关于S(a,2,1)、S(a,4,1)以及Wang–Wang关于S(a,b,2)的已有结果，对更一般的三足蜘蛛给出Schur正性的完整分类与负系数刻画。

Method: 证明结合了已知的clique-spiders的Schur正性结果、Pieri规则以及同步归纳法（simultaneous induction）。

Result: 建立了三足蜘蛛Schur正性的完整分类：至少两条偶腿者恒为Schur正；三条奇腿者必有负Schur系数；a偶、b,c奇时S(a,b,c)Schur正 iff a≤5b+5c+2；且当正性失效时恰有一个负Schur系数，其值被显式确定。

Conclusion: 该工作将三足蜘蛛的Schur正性判定推广至一般情形，统一并扩展了此前关于S(a,2,1)、S(a,4,1)与S(a,b,2)的结果，并给出了负系数的精确刻画。

Abstract: We give a complete classification of Schur positivity for three-legged spiders. Every spider with at least two even legs is Schur positive, whereas every spider with three odd legs has a negative Schur coefficient. If $a$ is even and $b,c$ are odd, then the spider $S(a,b,c)$ is Schur positive if and only if $a\le 5b+5c+2$. Whenever Schur positivity fails, there is exactly one negative Schur coefficient, whose value we determine explicitly. These results extend those of Thibon and Wang for the families $S(a,2,1)$ and $S(a,4,1)$, and those of Wang and Wang for $S(a,b,2)$. The proof combines known Schur-positivity results for clique-spiders with Pieri's rule and simultaneous induction.

</details>


### [85] [On the Spectra of Generalized Token Graphs of Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03471)
*Cristina Dalfó,Miquel Àngel Fiol,Berenice Martínez-Barona,Xiaodi Song*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了令牌图（supertoken graph）的推广形式，通过正则划分方法，给出了若干情形下星图（star）的supertoken图的完整谱。


<details>

Motivation: 令牌图及其推广（supertoken graph）是图论中的研究对象，其顶点对应于放置在图上不同或相同顶点处的可区分或不可区分令牌。刻画这类图的谱有助于理解其结构与性质，但星图的supertoken图谱尚未被完全确定，因此作者希望填补这一空白。
