Conclusion: 利用正则划分可以有效地分析supertoken图的结构，并得到星图supertoken图的完整谱，为后续研究更一般图的supertoken图谱提供了方法基础。

Abstract: In the generalization of token graphs (or supertoken graphs), the vertices of a $k$-token graph of a graph $G$ correspond to $k$ distinguishable or indistinguishable tokens placed on $k$ different or equal vertices of $G$. In several cases, we provide the whole spectrum of a supertoken graph of a star by analyzing its regular partitions.

</details>


### [86] [Partitioning an $S$-packing coloring into broadcast dominating sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03477)
*Boštjan Brešar,Jasmina Ferme,Wenjie Hu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入packing k-domatic coloring这一新图着色概念，将packing coloring与domatic number相结合，证明χ_{ρ,3}(G)=χ_ρ(G)对最小度至少为2的连通图成立，并给出路径、环及无穷路径上的精确值与界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: packing coloring与domatic number是图着色中两个重要概念，本文希望将二者结合，构造一种同时满足packing约束与domatic覆盖约束的新着色不变量χ_{ρ,k}(G)，并刻画其与经典packing chromatic number的关系。

Method: 定义packing k-domatic coloring：要求同色顶点距离大于其颜色值(条件1)，且顶点集可划分为k个集合使每个顶点在各集合中均有颜色值不小于其距离的顶点(条件2)。研究最小颜色数χ_{ρ,k}(G)，通过构造与反例分析确定精确值并推广到一般S-packing k-domatic coloring框架。

Result: 证明χ_{ρ,3}(G)=χ_ρ(G)对任意最小度至少为2的连通图成立且界在两种意义下最优；对路径P_n，当k∈{3,4,5}且n≥8时χ_{ρ,k}(P_n)=k，而当n≥k≥12时χ_{ρ,k}(P_n)>k；给出P_∞在k∈{3,4,5}时的精确值及C_n在k∈{3,4}时的精确值。

Conclusion: packing k-domatic coloring是packing coloring与domatic number的自然推广，χ_{ρ,k}(G)提供了统一框架刻画两概念的交互；在最小度受限的连通图及路径、环类图上得到紧界与精确值，S-packing推广框架亦给出若干基础结论。

Abstract: Given a graph $G$ and a positive integer $k$, a packing $k$-domatic coloring of $G$ is a function $f: V(G) \to \{1,\ldots,t\}$ such that (1) for any color $j \in \{1,\ldots, t\}$ and any two distinct vertices $u,v\in V(G)$ with $f(u)=f(v)=j$ we have $d_G(u,v)>j$, and (2) $V(G)$ admits a partition into $k$ sets $A_1,\ldots,A_k$ such that for any $v\in V(G)$ and any $i\in\{1,\ldots,k\}$ there exists a vertex $w\in A_i$ such that $d_G(v,w)\le f(w)$. The minimum integer $t$ such that $G$ admits a packing $k$-domatic coloring of $G$ using colors in $\{1,\ldots,t\}$ is denoted by $χ_{ρ,k}(G)$. The condition (1) in the definition of a packing $k$-domatic coloring implies that $G$ is a packing coloring, hence $χ_{ρ,k}(G)\ge χ_ρ(G)$ holds for any graph $G$, where $χ_ρ(G)$ is the packing chromatic number of $G$. On the other hand, the new concept also leads to a generalization of the domatic number of a graph due to which one can easily see that $χ_{ρ,2}(G)=χ_ρ(G)$ holds for any graph with no isolated vertices. One of the main result in this paper is that $χ_{ρ,3}(G)=χ_ρ(G)$ holds in any connected graph $G$ with minimum degree at least $2$, and the bound is best possible in two different senses. In addition, we provide several exact values and bounds on the new invariant in paths and cycles. We prove that $χ_{ρ,k}(P_n)=k$ for any $k\in\{3,4,5\}$ as soon as $n\ge 8$, and, in contrast, $χ_{ρ,k}(P_n)>k$ for any $n\ge k\ge 12$. We also prove the exact values of $χ_{ρ,k}(P_\infty)$ when $k\in \{3,4,5\}$ for the two-way infinite path $P_\infty$, and exact values of $χ_{ρ,k}(C_n)$ when $k\in\{3,4\}$ for all cycles $C_n$. We also consider a general framework of $S$-packing $k$-domatic colorings, where $S$ is an arbitrary sequence of non-negative integers, and present some basic results in this context.

</details>


### [87] [Geometric triangle-free graphs of large chromatic number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03517)
*István Tomon*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过多种几何构造，给出无三角形及大围长图族具有快速增长色数的新结果，改进了多个经典下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对几何交集图与单位距离图中色数下界的长期开放问题，改进Burling、Davies等人的经典构造，并回应Walczak问题及有序图版本的Gyárfás-Sumner猜想。

Method: 采用几何构造方法：(1) R^3中n个盒子的无三角形交集图；(2) 由单位距离图诱导出任意大围长且色数相同的图；(3) R^3中n条直线的交集图；(4) 平面中n个圆的相切图；(5) 避免固定有序路径的无三角形有序图。

Result: (1) 独立数为n(log n)^{-1+o(1)}，色数为(log n)^{1-o(1)}，首次改进Burling 1965年的双重对数下界；(2) 单位距离图问题完全归约到无围长限制情形；(3) 与(4) 均达到Ω_g((log n)^{1-o(1)})色数，定量改进Davies等；(5) 色数达n^{Ω(1/log log n)}。

Conclusion: 这些构造显著推进了几何图色数下界的研究，几乎匹配已知上界，并强化了单位距离图与有序图的相关结论。

Abstract: We present several geometric constructions of triangle-free and large girth families of graphs with rapidly growing chromatic numbers.
  1. We construct a triangle-free intersection graph of $n$ boxes in $\mathbb{R}^3$ with independence number $n(\log n)^{-1+o(1)}$, and thus chromatic number $(\log n)^{1-o(1)}$. This is the first improvement over the double logarithmic lower bound of Burling from 1965, and almost matches the best known upper bound $O(\log n)$. Moreover, the bound $o(n)$ on the independence number answers a question of Walczak.
  2. We prove that if there exists a unit distance graph in $\mathbb{R}^d$ of chromatic number $r$, then there also exists an induced unit distance graph in $\mathbb{R}^d$ of girth at least $g$ and the same chromatic number. Thus, the problem of determining the maximum chromatic number of unit distance graphs with any prescribed lower bound on the girth reduces entirely to the unrestricted problem, thereby strengthening a long line of results.
  3. We construct an intersection graph of $n$ lines in $\mathbb{R}^3$ with girth at least $g$ and chromatic number $Ω_g((\log n)^{1-o(1)})$. This quantitatively improves a construction of Davies.
  4. We construct a set of $n$ circles in the plane such that the tangency graph of the circles has girth at least $g$ and chromatic number $Ω_g((\log n)^{1-o(1)})$. This quantitatively improves the result of Davies, Keller, Kleist, Smorodinsky, and Walczak, and provides an alternative solution of Ringel's circle problem.
  5. We construct triangle-free ordered graphs on $n$ vertices avoiding a fixed ordered path of length three as an induced subgraph, and having chromatic number $n^{Ω(1/\log \log n)}$. This is motivated by ordered analogues of the Gyárfás-Sumner conjecture.

</details>


### [88] [Counterexamples to the Strong Roberson Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03550)
*Arnar Á. Kristjánsson*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文否定了Strong Roberson猜想（及其浸入版本），通过构造特定连通图H，使得来自排除H作为minor的图类的同态计数能够确定H到任意目标图的同态数，并给出显式无限反例族及一种由模等价导出精确同态计数依赖的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Strong Roberson猜想断言：对于在minor和disjoint unions下封闭的图类，加入该类之外的任何图都会严格增强同态计数的区分能力。本文旨在检验并否定该猜想，同时探讨其以immersion替代minor的类比版本。

Method: 作者构造连通图H，使得排除H作为minor（或immersion）的图类上的同态计数可确定H到任意目标图的同态数；提出一种从模等价导出精确同态计数依赖的方法；借助图的prime-order自同构（其轨道商被排除为minor或immersion），对无穷多素数获得所需的模等价关系。

Result: 否定了Strong Roberson猜想，也否定了以immersion替代minor的类比猜想；给出了显式无限反例族，其中包括同时对两种关系都构成反例的立方二部图；并通过对无穷多素数建立模等价，得到了精确的同态计数依赖。

Conclusion: Strong Roberson猜想及浸入类比版本均不成立；排除单一图（作为minor或immersion）的图类可以具有与包含该图相同甚至不弱于其的同态计数区分能力，这一结论由显式无限族与模等价方法共同支撑。

Abstract: We refute the Strong Roberson Conjecture, which asserts that adding any graph outside a class closed under minors and disjoint unions strictly increases the distinguishing power of homomorphism counts from that class. More precisely, we construct connected graphs $H$ for which counts from graphs excluding $H$ as a minor determine the number of homomorphisms from $H$ to any target graph. We also refute the analogous conjecture with immersions in place of minors. We give explicit infinite families of excluded graphs, including cubic bipartite graphs that yield counterexamples for both relations. The proof introduces a method for deriving exact homomorphism count dependence from modular equivalences. We obtain these equivalences for infinitely many primes using prime-order automorphisms of graphs that exclude their orbit quotients as minors or immersions.

</details>


### [89] [On the Average Sizes of Ideals and Antichains in Some Infinite Families of Posets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03579)
*Miklos Bona,Ricardo Gomez Aiza*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究根无标号树所生成偏序集的理想与反链的平均规模，利用生成函数与奇点分析证明：对二次根分解家族，理想平均规模渐近 n/3；无限制平面树为 2n/5；反链常数依分支函数而定（如二叉平面树为 n/6）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画由根无标号树诱导的偏序集中理想（红点集）与反链（不可比蓝点集）的平均规模，揭示其随树族分支结构变化的渐近规律，并建立与分支函数/生成函数类型之间的定量联系。

Method: 将序理想编码为红点着色（红点的所有后代也为红）；用双变量生成函数建模，结合解析组合学中的奇点分析、解析隐函数法与光滑隐函数法，对平均红点数与平均反链规模做渐近估计；并比较不同树族（二叉平面树、平面1-2树、平面2树、0-1树、无限制根平面树及每点至多k个孩子的平面树）。

Result: 对每个具二次根分解的家族，规模为n的树中平均红点数渐近 n/3；无限制根平面树为 2n/5。反链的渐近常数依赖具体分支函数，例如二叉平面树平均反链规模渐近 n/6。对每点至多k个孩子的平面树，红点渐近比例随k严格递增，从k=2的1/3增至无限制情形的极限2/5。

Conclusion: 理想与反链的平均规模由树族的分支函数决定：二次根分解家族统一给出 n/3，而无限制情形为 2n/5，并随最大孩子数单调过渡。该框架用生成函数与隐函数解析方法系统刻画了偏序集规模的渐近行为。

Abstract: We study the average sizes of ideals and antichains in several families of posets arising from rooted unlabeled trees. For ideals, we encode order ideals by coloring vertices red, with the condition that every descendant of a red vertex is also red. Using generating functions and singularity analysis, we show that for every family governed by a quadratic root decomposition, the average number of red vertices in a tree of size $n$ is asymptotic to $n/3$. This includes binary plane trees, plane 1-2 trees, plane 2-trees, and 0-1-trees, while unrestricted rooted plane trees instead have average ideal size asymptotic to $2n/5$. For antichains, represented by pairwise incomparable blue vertices, the corresponding asymptotic constants depend on the particular branching function; for example, binary plane trees have average antichain size asymptotic to $n/6$. We also consider plane trees in which every vertex has at most $k$ children and prove that the asymptotic proportion of red vertices increases strictly with $k$, from $1/3$ when $k=2$ to the limiting value $2/5$ for unrestricted rooted plane trees. The proofs are based on bivariate generating functions together with the analytic implicit-function and smooth implicit-function methods of analytic combinatorics.

</details>


### [90] [Counting Hamiltonian Sturm permutations: generating functions and Gaussian distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03611)
*Bernold Fiedler,Carlos Rocha*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过哈密顿Sturm排列的组合分析，对一类具有耗散非退化非线性项的Neumann边值反应扩散方程的全局吸引子进行分类，并给出相关计数生成函数与渐近分布结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由PDE动力学 $u_t = u_{xx} + g(u)$（Neumann边界条件）驱动的组合问题。对于耗散非退化非线性项 $g$，全局吸引子可由其边界平衡点的排序来分类，因此需要引入并计数相应的组合对象——哈密顿Sturm排列，以刻画PDE解的结点性质与平衡点结构。同时希望借助计数与渐近分析揭示平衡点类型的概率分布规律。

Method: 将边界排序编码为哈密顿Sturm排列：\u201cSturm\u201d对应PDE解的结点性质，\u201cHamiltonian\u201d对应平衡点满足的二阶摆方程 $0 = v_{xx} + g(v)$。利用组合计数与生成函数方法：确定总计数 $a_n$ 的生成函数 $a(z)=\sum_n a_n z^n$；进一步按空间均匀平衡点数 $2r+1$ 与非均匀平衡点数 $2q$ 细化，$a_n=\sum b_{rq}$（$r+q=n$），并求显式二元生成函数 $b(x,y)=\sum_{r,q} b_{rq} x^r y^q$。渐近分析基于Flajolet与Sedgewick的方法，并给出均值和方差的渐近展开及 $1/n$ 阶误差估计。

Result: 得到哈密顿Sturm排列计数 $a_n$ 的生成函数 $a(z)$，并给出 $n\to\infty$ 时 $a_n$ 的显式渐近式；得到细化计数 $b_{rq}$ 的显式生成函数 $b(x,y)$。由此证明概率 $p_{nr}=b_{rq}/a_n$（$r+q=n$）在大 $n$ 下渐近服从高斯分布，并推导出均值与方差的渐近表达式，误差估计为 $1/n$ 阶。最后给出数值示例。

Conclusion: 哈密顿Sturm排列为分类Neumann边值下耗散反应扩散方程全局吸引子提供了有效组合框架；其计数具有可显式求出的生成函数，且平衡点类型分布在渐近意义下为高斯分布。文中还讨论了周期边界条件 $x\in\mathbb{S}^1=\mathbb{R}/2\mathbb{Z}$ 下非线性项 $g(u,u_x)$ 的情形，此时会出现旋转波。

Abstract: Our combinatorial analysis is motivated by the PDE dynamics \begin{equation} \mathbf{u_t} = \mathbf{u_{xx}} + \mathbf{g}(\mathbf{u}),\qquad 0<\mathbf{x}<1, \end{equation} of solutions $\mathbf{u}=\mathbf{u}(\mathbf{t},\mathbf{x}),\ \mathbf{t}\geq 0$, under Neumann boundary conditions. For dissipative nondegenerate nonlinearities $\mathbf{g}$, the global attractors $\mathcal{A}=\mathcal{A}_\mathbf{g}$ of the PDE can then be classified by the orderings of their $2n+1$ equilibria $\mathbf{v}$ at the boundaries $\mathbf{x}=0,1$. We encode the boundary orders as Hamiltonian Sturm permutations. The name ''Sturm'' refers to nodal properties of PDE solutions $\mathbf{u}(\mathbf{t},\mathbf{x})$. ''Hamiltonian'' refers to the second order pendulum ODE for equilibria $\mathbf{v}(\mathbf{x})$: \begin{equation} 0 = \mathbf{v_{xx}} + \mathbf{g}(\mathbf{v}). \end{equation} We determine the generating function $a(z)=\sum_n a_nz^n$ for the counts $a_n$ of Hamiltonian Sturm permutations. For $n\rightarrow\infty$, this provides explicit asymptotics of $a_n$.
  We refine these counts as $a_n=\sum b_{rq}$. Here $b_{rq}$ counts Hamiltonian Sturm permutations with $2r+1$ spatially homogeneous equilibria and $2q$ spatially non-homogeneous equilibria, such that $r+q=n$. We also determine the explicit generating function $b(x,y)=\sum_{r,q} b_{rq}x^ry^q$. This implies asymptotically Gaussian distributions of the probabilities $p_{nr}=b_{rq}/a_n$ with $r+q=n$, asymptotically for large $n$. We derive asymptotics for means and variances, with error estimates of order $1/n$. All asymptotics are based on work by Flajolet and Sedgewick.
  We conclude with numerical illustrations and remarks on nonlinearities $\mathbf{g}(\mathbf{u},\mathbf{u_x})$ under periodic boundary conditions $\mathbf{x}\in\mathbb{S}^1=\mathbb{R}/2\mathbb{Z}$, where rotating waves arise.

</details>


### [91] [Signed seeds and G-gradings on cluster algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03612)
*Lauren Williams,Alan Yan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将符号种子推广为一般阿贝尔群 $G$ 的 $G$-分次种子，统一刻画了簇代数中的符号胞腔、簇自同构/伸缩群与分次簇代数等概念，并研究了矩阵空间、positroid 簇、amplituhedron 瓦片等例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 簇代数理论与全正性理论密切相关：簇簇的坐标环具有簇结构时，可定义由所有簇变量取正值构成的"正部分"。作者希望说明，从簇理论角度看，簇簇中还自然存在其他类型的符号胞腔，从而将符号种子（源自 amplituhedron 研究的 $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$ 分次）纳入更一般的框架并统一已有的多种分次结构。

Method: 对任意阿贝尔群 $G$，引入簇代数的 $G$-分次种子概念，即以与簇结构相容的方式为每个簇变量赋予 $G$ 中元素。当 $G=\{-1,1\}$ 时还原为符号种子；$G=\mathbb{C}^*$ 时还原为簇自同构群/伸缩群；$G=\mathbb{Z}^d$ 时还原为 Grabowski-Launois、Grabowski 及 Gekhtman-Shapiro-Vainshtein 研究的分次簇代数。随后以方阵空间、对称矩阵空间、斜对称矩阵空间、positroid 簇和 amplituhedron 瓦片等为例进行考察，并在 $G=\mathbb{R}$ 或 $\mathbb{Z}$ 时建立与热带变异的联系。

Result: 构建了 $G$-分次种子的统一框架，统一了符号种子、簇自同构/伸缩群以及分次簇代数等多种已有概念，并在矩阵空间、positroid 簇、amplituhedron 瓦片等具体例子中给出体现；同时将 $G=\mathbb{R},\mathbb{Z}$ 情形与热带变异相联系。

Conclusion: 簇簇中存在多种从簇理论角度同样自然的符号胞腔，它们可由 $G$-分次种子统一刻画，该框架涵括并推广了若干已有理论，并为热带变异研究提供联系。

Abstract: The theory of cluster algebras is closely connected to the theory of total positivity; indeed, the desire to better understand total positivity was one of the main motivations for Fomin and Zelevinsky's introduction of cluster algebras [arXiv:math/0104151]. In particular, any cluster variety whose coordinate ring has a cluster structure has a natural notion of positive part: the subset of the variety where all cluster variables are positive. In this paper, we explain that there are other signed cells contained in cluster varieties that are equally natural from a cluster-theoretic point of view. These come from signed seeds, which can be thought of as a $\mathbb{Z}/2\mathbb{Z}$-grading on cluster variables, and which were introduced in [arXiv:2310.17727] in the context of the amplituhedron. More generally, given any abelian group $G$, we introduce the notion of a $G$-graded seed for a cluster algebra, which is a way of assigning elements of $G$ to each cluster variable which is compatible with the cluster structure. When $G$ is the multiplicative group $\{-1, 1\}$, this recovers the above notion of signed seed; when $G = \mathbb{C}^*$, this recovers the notion of cluster automorphism group [GSV10] or cluster dilation group [arXiv:2603.17890]; and when $G = \mathbb{Z}^d$, this recovers the notion of graded cluster algebra studied by Grabowski-Launois [arXiv:1301.2133], Grabowski [arXiv:1309.6170] and Gekhtman-Shapiro-Vainshtein [GSV10, Section 5.2] (which had previously appeared in special cases in work of Fomin-Zelevinsky [arXiv:math/0602259]). The examples we study include the space of square matrices, symmetric matrices, skew-symmetric matrices, positroid varieties, and amplituhedron tiles. We also connect this notion to tropical mutation when $G = \mathbb{R}$ or $\mathbb{Z}$.

</details>


### [92] [Non-isomorphic graphs have distinct vertex-Ramsey classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03704)
*Maria Axenovich,Patrick Schmitteckert*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了对于任意k，任意两个不同构图的k-色顶点Ramsey类互不相同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图的Ramsey类刻画了在任意k着色下必然出现单色（诱导）副本的图族。此前尚不清楚不同构图的Ramsey类是否会重合，本文旨在回答这一区分性问题。

Method: 组合论证。以顶点k着色的Ramsey类为对象，证明对任意非构图的H与H'，其对应的k-色顶点Ramsey类作为集合不相等。

Result: 对任意k，任意两个非构图的k-色顶点Ramsey类都不同，即映射H↦（其k-色顶点Ramsey类）是单射。

Conclusion: 构图的顶点Ramsey类能够唯一确定该图本身，说明Ramsey类包含了图的全部结构信息。

Abstract: For a graph $H$, its $k$-colour vertex Ramsey class is the set of all graphs $G$ such that any colouring of the vertices of $G$ in $k$ colours results in a monochromatic (induced) copy of $H$. We prove that for any $k$, Ramsey classes of any non-isomorphic graphs are distinct.

</details>
