### [93] [Arithmetic growth in partially commutative semigroups of endomorphisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02348)
*Wade Hindes*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究品种自同态的部分交换半群，建立了次数与高度增长率函数的渐近公式，证明了关于抽象函数增长率的一般性定理，并通过推广乒乓引理构造了多族新自同态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分交换半群（生成元间仅有交换关系的半群）的增长率渐近性质此前缺乏系统刻画，作者希望建立统一的渐近理论，并将其应用于品种自同态半群这一具体对象。

Method: 证明了一个联系两类抽象函数（一为乘性、一为非负）增长率的一般性结果，该结果依赖Tate望远镜（telescoping）假设与底层半群上足够良好的正规形；将元素版的经典乒乓引理（ping-pong lemma）推广到子半群版本。

Result: 得到了部分交换自同态半群的次数增长率与高度增长率的渐近公式；利用子半群乒乓引理构造出若干族新的自同态，上述渐近结果对这些族均适用。

Conclusion: 该工作将增长率渐近分析与组合半群方法（正规形、Tate telescoping、乒乓引理）相结合，为部分交换自同态半群的渐近行为提供了统一框架，并拓展了可应用该框架的自同态族。

Abstract: We establish asymptotics for the degree and height growth rate functions of partially commutative semigroups of endomorphisms of varieties, i.e., semigroups where the only relations between generators, if any, are commuting ones. To do this, we prove a general result connecting the growth rates of two abstract functions, one multiplicative and one simply nonnegative, subject to a Tate telescoping hypothesis and a sufficiently nice normal form on the underlying semigroup. Finally, we construct several new families of endomorphisms on which our asymptotics apply. To do this, we generalize the standard ping-pong lemma for elements to a ping-pong lemma for subsemigroups.

</details>


### [94] [Counterexamples for Rational Points Near Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02443)
*Mingfeng Chen,Rajula Srivastava,Niclas Technau*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究moment curve上有理点逼近问题中启发式估计 $N_{\mathcal{C}}(\delta,Q)\asymp\delta^{n-1}Q^2$ 成立的 $\delta$ 范围，证明Hickman与第二作者给出的下界范围在 $n\to\infty$ 的渐近意义下几乎是最优的，最多只能改进到 $\delta\in[Q^{\alpha(n)},1/2)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非退化曲线附近有理点计数 $N_{\mathcal{C}}(\delta,Q)$ 的渐近公式适用范围。此前Hickman与第二作者证明当 $\delta\in[Q^{\alpha(n)+\nu},1/2)$、$\alpha(n)=-4n^{-2}+O(n^{-3})$ 时上界 $N_{\mathcal{C}}(\delta,Q)\lesssim_\nu\delta^{n-1}Q^2$ 成立，且普遍认为指数可改进 $O(n^{-1})$。本文旨在确定该 $\delta$ 范围究竟能否显著扩大，即检验其是否接近最优。

Method: 针对moment curve这一具体情形，构造反例或下界论证，分析使启发式估计失效的 $\delta$ 与 $Q$ 的关系，从而给出该范围在渐近意义下的临界限制。

Result: 证明Hickman与第二作者给出的 $\delta$ 范围在 $n\to\infty$ 渐近意义下接近最优，至多可扩展到 $\delta\in[Q^{\alpha(n)},1/2)$，即指数至多改进 $O(n^{-3})$，而非此前普遍预期的 $O(n^{-1})$。剩余区间的互补上界已由Gan–Guo–Oh近期建立。

Conclusion: moment curve上有理点逼近的启发式估计之有效 $\delta$ 范围几乎无法显著扩大，$\alpha(n)$ 的 $O(n^{-3})$ 精度已接近最优；结合Gan–Guo–Oh的结果，该问题的渐近刻画基本完成。

Abstract: Let $δ\in [0,1/2]$ and $Q\geq 1$. Given a compact $C^{\infty}$-curve $\mathcal{C}$ in $\mathbb{R}^n$, denote by $N_{\mathcal{C}}(δ, Q)$ the number of pairs $(\mathbf{a}, q)\in \mathbb{Z}^n\times [Q/2,Q]$ such that the rational point $\mathbf{a}/q$ is $δ/q$-close to $\mathcal{C}$. We investigate the range of $δ$ in terms of $Q$ which is necessary for the heuristic $N_{\mathcal{C}}(δ, Q)\asympδ^{n-1}Q^2$ to be correct for the moment curve. For a nondegenerate curve $\mathcal{C}\subset \mathbb{R}^n$ and for sufficiently large $Q$, Hickman and the second author previously established that $N_{\mathcal{C}}(δ, Q)\lesssim_ν δ^{n-1}Q^2$ for all $δ\in[Q^{α(n+1)+ν},1/2)$ and any $ν>0$. Here $α(n)=-4n^{-2}+O(n^{-3})$ was explicitly computed. We show that this range is surprisingly close to being sharp in the asymptotic sense as $n\to\infty$, and can at most be extended to $δ\in[Q^{α(n)},1/2)$. In particular, the sharp exponent can only be about $O(n^{-3})$ better, even though it was widely believed before that an improvement of $O(n^{-1})$ should be possible. The complementary upper bound in this remaining range has been recently established by Gan--Guo--Oh.

</details>


### [95] [The proportion of polynomials over F_2 with positive orthogonal multiplicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02352)
*David Niedbala Giraudin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究 $\mathbb{F}_2[T]$ 中首一多项式的正交重数 $m(f)$，证明了关于微分留数 $\psi_P(f)$ 的互反关系与精确局部分布，并在局部条件独立汇合的假设下得到 $p(d) \sim L\,d^{-1/2}$，且给出 $1 \le d \le 33$ 的精确值与验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Blackburn (1998) 提出：首一多项式 $f$ 的正交重数 $m(f)>0$ 的比例 $p(d)$ 当 $d\to\infty$ 时如何变化？该问题此前缺乏定量答案，本文旨在给出 $p(d)$ 的渐近行为与精确数值证据。

Method: 令 $A=\mathbb{F}_2[T]$，对 $f$ 的每个素因子 $P$ 关联微分 $dT/(T(T+1)f)$ 的留数 $\psi_P(f)\in\mathbb{F}_2$。证明两个无条件结果：(i) 互反关系：当 $f$ 与 $T(T+1)$ 互素时，所有 $\psi_P(f)$ 之和为零；(ii) 精确局部分布：当 $P$ 恰整除 $f$ 时 $\psi_P=0$ 的容许补全比例为 $(|P|-2)/(2(|P|-1))$，当 $P^e$ 恰整除 $f$ 且 $e\ge 2$ 时该比例恰为 $1/2$。随后假设这些局部条件独立汇合，用 Euler 乘积计算比例。

Result: 在局部条件独立汇合的假设下，$p(d)\sim L\,d^{-1/2}$，其中 $L=\frac{9}{4\sqrt{\pi}}\prod_{P}(1+\frac{1}{2|P|})(1-\frac{1}{|P|})^{1/2}=1.2116120743\ldots$，乘积遍历 $A$ 中次数至少为 2 的首一不可约元。同一 Euler 乘积在第二参数处给出恒等式：给定次数的 $m(f)$ 之和等于 $2^d$，从而确定归一化。论文给出 $1\le d\le 33$ 的 $2^d p(d)$ 精确值，对 $27\le d\le 33$ 预测值与精确值的误差在 $5\times 10^{-5}$ 内。

Conclusion: 论文通过置换理论（正交重数与置换的循环结构相关）和微分留数的局部计算，将 Blackburn 问题归约为可计算的 Euler 乘积；在局部独立性假设下给出 $p(d)\sim L\,d^{-1/2}$，并由 $m(f)$ 之和恒为 $2^d$ 确定常数。数值验证支持该渐近式，尤其在高次范围内吻合极好。

Abstract: Let A = F_2[T]. The orthogonal multiplicity m(f) of a monic f in A of degree d >= 1 is the number of g in A with deg g < d, gcd(g,f) = 1, and all partial quotients of g/f of degree one. Blackburn (1998) asked how the proportion p(d) of monic polynomials of degree d with m(f) > 0 behaves as d tends to infinity.
  We attach to each prime divisor P of f a residue psi_P(f) in F_2 of the differential dT/(T(T+1)f), and prove two unconditional facts about this family: a reciprocity relation, namely that the psi_P(f) sum to zero whenever f is prime to T(T+1), and an exact local distribution, namely that psi_P vanishes for a proportion exactly (|P|-2)/(2(|P|-1)) of the admissible completions when P exactly divides f, and for exactly half of them when P^e exactly divides f with e >= 2.
  Granting that these local conditions pool independently, we obtain p(d) ~ L d^(-1/2) with L = (9/(4 sqrt(pi))) times the product over the monic irreducibles P of A of degree at least two of (1 + 1/(2|P|)) (1 - 1/|P|)^(1/2), equal to 1.2116120743... The same Euler product evaluated at a second parameter returns the exact identity that the m(f) of a given degree sum to 2^d, which fixes its normalisation. We give the exact value of 2^d p(d) for 1 <= d <= 33; the predicted values agree with these to within 5 10^(-5) for 27 <= d <= 33.

</details>


### [96] [Shimura Reciprocity for Real Quadratic Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02419)
*Joseph DiCapua*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文在实二次域上假设阶1阿贝尔Stark猜想成立，给出Shintani--Faddeev模余圈实乘值上Galois作用的条件显式公式，并提出关于该Galois作用的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shintani--Faddeev模余圈上Galois作用的显式描述是一个核心问题，但通常需要借助未证明的猜想（如Stark猜想）才能得到无条件结果之外的公式。本文旨在给出条件性显式公式并提炼新的猜想。

Method: 在阶1阿贝尔Stark猜想对实二次域成立的假设下，推导Shintani--Faddeev模余圈实乘值（real multiplication values）上Galois作用的显式公式；据此提出关于Galois作用的新猜想。

Result: 得到条件性的Galois作用显式公式，并提出一个关于Galois作用的自有猜想；数值证据将在后续工作中给出。

Conclusion: 本文在Stark猜想假设下刻画了Shintani--Faddeev模余圈实乘值的Galois作用，并提出可供后续数值验证的新猜想。

Abstract: The purpose of this note is to give conditional explicit formulas for the Galois action on real multiplication values of the Shintani--Faddeev modular cocycle. We assume the order 1 abelian Stark conjecture for real quadratic fields. We conclude this note by presenting our own conjecture about the Galois action. Numerical evidence for this conjecture will be provided in forthcoming work.

</details>


### [97] [The Prime Wavelet Tree: Compressing the Carmichael Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02550)
*Ankur Gupta,Jonathan Webster,Sylvia Webster*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 提出局部与全局两种数论压缩技术，基于素数小波树压缩满足类Korselt准则的数集，在全部$10^{24}$以下Carmichael数上实现31.2倍压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对大规模数论对象集合（如Carmichael数）的存储以文本形式占据巨大空间（18.4 GB），缺乏针对性压缩手段，需要利用数论结构降低存储开销。

Method: 提出两种压缩技术：局部方法压缩单个数字；全局方法使用素数小波树（prime wavelet tree，独立有趣的数据结构）压缩整个数集。适用对象为满足类Korselt准则（Korselt-like criterion）的任意数集，并结合标准压缩技术与启发式除数选择。

Result: 在全部308,279,939个小于$10^{24}$的Carmichael数（文本18.4 GB）上验证：结合标准压缩与启发式除数选择后文件为588.7 MB，较文本小31.2倍；初始80位数字压缩后平均约15.3位。

Conclusion: 数论结构可有效指导压缩，素数小波树作为具独立价值的数据结构，能在类Korselt数集上取得显著压缩比，显著降低大规模数论数据的存储成本。

Abstract: We present two number-theoretic compression techniques. The first is \textit{local}, and compresses individual numbers. The second is \textit{global}, and compresses the entire set of numbers using a \textit{prime wavelet tree}, which is a data structure of independent interest. Our techniques apply to any set of numbers satisfying a Korselt-like criterion. We demonstrate our compression techniques using the recently completed tabulation of all $308{,}279{,}939$ Carmichael numbers less than $10^{24}$ that occupies $18.4$ gigabytes as text. When combined with standard compression techniques and a heuristic choice of divisors, the resulting file is $588.7$ megabytes ($31.2$ times smaller than the text file). The initial $80$-bit numbers now use about $15.3$ bits.

</details>


### [98] [Extreme values of Dirichlet $L$-functions on the critical line with a growing prime modulus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02709)
*Zikang Dong,Qiyu Yang,Shengbo Zhao*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究了素数模递增情形下Dirichlet L-函数在临界线上的极值问题，所得极值下界优于经典共振方法，并证明Bondarenko等人（2023）二分法中的一支可以在该函数族中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dirichlet L-函数在临界线上的极值大小是解析数论的核心问题之一。Soundararajan的共振方法给出了经典下界，而Bondarenko、Darbar、Hagen、Heap和Seip（2023）建立了一个二分法框架。本文旨在对素数模递增这一函数族改进极值下界，并判定该二分法中哪一支可以实现。

Method: 证明方法基于共振方法（resonance method），并结合对GCD和（GCD sums）的精确估计。

Result: 对素数模递增的Dirichlet L-函数族，得到了比Soundararajan经典共振方法更大的极值下界，并据此实现了Bondarenko等（2023）二分法中的一支。

Conclusion: 本文改进了Dirichlet L-函数临界线极值的下界，验证了在素数模族中二分法某一分支的可实现性，表明共振方法与GCD和估计的结合是处理此类极值问题的有效工具。

Abstract: In this paper, we obtain extreme values of Dirichlet $L$-functions on the critical line with a growing prime modulus. Our result yields a larger lower bound for extreme values than that from Soundararajan's classical resonance method. Consequently, we show that one branch of the dichotomy established by Bondarenko, Darbar, Hagen, Heap, and Seip (2023) can be realized for this family of Dirichlet $L$-functions. The proof relies on the resonance method together with precise estimates for GCD sums.

</details>


### [99] [A uniform degree bound for modular rank-two Nahm sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02743)
*Cetin Hakimoglu-Brown*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究秩二对称可对称化Nahm和的模性，证明若$q^cF$是模形式，则其Nahm点生成的域的次数$[K:\mathbb{Q}]\le 24$，并在特殊情形下给出更精细的界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模形式的Nahm和与其Nahm点之间存在深刻联系，模性对Nahm点所在数域的次数施加了强限制。论文旨在定量刻画这种限制，回答模性何时迫使Nahm点落在低次域中这一问题。

Method: 设$F$为秩二可对称化Nahm和，对称化子为$\operatorname{diag}(d_1,d_2)$，$m=d_2/d_1$，$K$为Nahm点生成域。若$q^cF$模，则对数径向渐近展开的高阶系数必须消失；作者通过Newton多边形及沿行列式圆锥的极点阶分析研究所得多项式方程。等行和情形用Bloch群挠；互补Nahm坐标情形用前两个渐近修正。部分步骤依赖精确计算。

Result: 一般情形证明$[K:\mathbb{Q}]\le 24$；对$m=1$，公共分支仅出现在一个显式族上且其Nahm点有理。等行和情形给出$[K:\mathbb{Q}]\le 2$。互补Nahm坐标情形：$m\ne1$时为$2$，$m=1$时为$4$。

Conclusion: 模性显著限制了秩二Nahm和对应Nahm点的算术复杂度，一般次数上界为24，并在等行和、互补坐标、$m=1$等结构条件下收缩到2或4。

Abstract: Let $F$ be a rank-two symmetrizable Nahm sum, with any symmetrizer $\operatorname{diag}(d_1,d_2)$, a positive-definite rational matrix and rational shifts, and let $K$ be the field generated by its Nahm point; write $m=d_2/d_1$. We prove that if $q^cF$ is modular, then $[K:\mathbb{Q}]\le 24$. Modularity forces the higher coefficients of the logarithmic radial asymptotic expansion to vanish; we study the resulting polynomial equations through their Newton polygons and a pole-order analysis along the determinant conic, and for $m=1$ show that their common components occur only on one explicit family, whose Nahm point is rational. For equal row sums, Bloch-group torsion gives $[K:\mathbb{Q}]\le 2$. For complementary Nahm coordinates, the bound is $2$ when $m\ne 1$ and $4$ when $m=1$, using the first two asymptotic corrections in the latter case. Several steps of the proofs rely on exact computation.

</details>


### [100] [The Wang--Sun Sum over Affine Derangements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02806)
*Octavio A. Agustín Aquino*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文计算了仿射群上错排集的Wang--Sun型符号和，给出闭式公式并证明若干整性与符号模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wang--Sun型求和此前仅在具体小参数下由定义直接计算，缺乏一般公式；本文旨在推导仿射群错排和的闭式表达并揭示其算术性质。

Method: 对仿射映射π(x)=vx+u进行算术分组：乘积仅依赖σ=gcd(1-v,n)及u模σ的剩余类；对平移符号求和后，整体和化为对偶除数的求和，带有显式局部重数。剩余解析部分为交错偶余切幂和，利用Liu--Xin公式以Bernoulli多项式与通用系数表达，并借助Cvijović--Klinowski结果实现纯有理算术计算。

Result: 得到仿射错排和的通用闭式公式，与早期工作由定义算出的初值一致；并据此证明：当指数为2的幂时和为整数，当为2p（p>3素数）时分母为素数，且和具有交错符号。

Conclusion: 该闭式公式统一并推广了先前仅靠定义计算的初值结果，同时揭示了和的整性、分母结构及符号交替等一般规律。

Abstract: Let $n$ be even, let $ζ$ be a primitive $n$-th root of unity, and let $Δ_n$ be the set of derangements in the affine group $\overrightarrow{GL}(\mathbb{Z}/n\mathbb{Z})$. We evaluate the Wang--Sun type sum \[
  S_n=\sum_{π\inΔ_n}\mathrm{sign}(π)
  \prod_{x\in\mathbb{Z}/n\mathbb{Z}}
  \frac{1+ζ^{x-π(x)}}{1-ζ^{x-π(x)}}. \]
  The main step is an arithmetic grouping: for an affine map $π(x)=vx+u$, the product depends only on $σ=\gcd(1-v,n)$ and on the residue class of $u$ modulo $σ$. After summing the translation signs, the full sum becomes a sum over even divisors with explicit local multiplicities. The only remaining analytic blocks are alternating even cotangent power sums; a recent formula of Liu and Xin then gives a closed expression in Bernoulli polynomials and universal coefficients. Moreover, using results by Cvijović and Klinowski we can evaluate the cotangent power sums using exclusively rational arithmetic. This supplies a general formula for the full affine sum whose initial values were computed using the definition only in an earlier work. The formula also lets us prove some general patterns of the sum: it has integral value for powers of $2$, prime denominator for $2p$ for primes $p>3$ and it has alternating signs.

</details>


### [101] [A new upper bound for the irrationality exponent of $ζ(2)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02912)
*David Niedbala Giraudin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明 ζ(2)=π²/6 的无理性指数满足 μ(ζ(2))<5.0193784，改进了已发表的 5.095412（Zudilin, 2014）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无理性指数刻画无理数被有理数逼近的程度。ζ(2)=π²/6 的该指数此前最好已发表结果为 5.095412（Zudilin, 2014），作者希望进一步压低这一上界。

Method: 在 Zudilin 的两个超几何构造中选择新的参数取值，得到两族关于 1 与 ζ(2) 的线性型；对充分大的指标二者重合，此重合是 Zudilin 猜想恒等式的一例，通过比较差的分母的算术界与其大小的解析界来证明，未使用 creative telescoping；全部数值常数用球算术（ball arithmetic）验证。

Result: 证明 μ(ζ(2))<5.0193784，优于已发表界 5.095412；对比近期公开的带机器验证证明的界 5.0495243（J. Kleid, 2026年9月）。

Conclusion: 借助新参数下两族线性型的重合（Zudilin 猜想恒等式的一例），并以球算术严格认证常数，获得 ζ(2) 无理性指数的新上界 5.0193784，同时提供了该恒等式的一个无需 creative telescoping 的证明。

Abstract: We prove that the irrationality exponent of $ζ(2)=π^2/6$ satisfies $μ(ζ(2))<5.0193784$. The best published bound is 5.095412 (Zudilin, 2014); a bound 5.0495243 with a machine-checked proof was made public in September 2026 by J. Kleid. We use Zudilin's two hypergeometric constructions at a new choice of parameters. The two families of linear forms in 1 and $ζ(2)$ so obtained coincide for every sufficiently large index: this coincidence, an instance of an identity conjectured by Zudilin, is established by comparing an arithmetic bound for the denominators of the difference with an analytic bound for its size, without creative telescoping. All numerical constants are certified in ball arithmetic.

</details>


### [102] [some arithmetical aspects of the two dimensional jacobian conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02961)
*Ronen Peretz*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明二维雅可比猜想（整系数、雅可比行列式为1）的任意反例，其渐近值路径至多包含有限多个平面整格点；并把某类Theta函数理论应用于实数域与复数域上的二维雅可比猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维雅可比猜想长期未决，需要在特定系数与行列式约束下刻画潜在反例的结构，限制其几何形态，从而缩小反例搜索范围并寻找新的证明途径。

Method: 对满足整系数且雅可比行列式等于1的假设反例，分析其渐近值路径与平面整格点的相交性质；并引入特定Theta函数理论，将其应用于实数域和复数域上的二维雅可比猜想。

Result: 证明了此类反例的渐近值路径只能包含有限多个平面整格点；同时给出Theta函数理论在实数域、复数域二维雅可比猜想中的若干非平凡应用。

Conclusion: 在整系数与单位雅可比行列式条件下，反例的渐近值路径受整格点限制，为二维雅可比猜想提供了结构性约束；Theta函数方法为其在实数域与复数域上的研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We prove that any counter-example to the two dimensional Jacobian Conjecture with integral coefficients and determinant of its Jacobian matrix equals one, has only paths of asymptotic values that can contain only finitely many points of the planar integral lattice. The second part of our paper gives some nontrivial applications of the theory of certain Theta functions to the two dimensional Jacobian Conjecture over the real field, and over the complex field.

</details>


### [103] [Telescopic Structure of Numerical Semigroups Generated by Unsigned Stirling Numbers of the First Kind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03070)
*Takao Komatsu,Kyunghwan Song*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了由第n行非平凡无符号第一类Stirling数生成的数值半群S_n，确定了其约化生成系统的完整gcd滤链，并证明了S_n的Apéry集为矩形、给出Frobenius数的显式公式，同时说明S_n是自由且对称的，具有显式的亏格与导子公式，其类型为1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究由组合数列（第n行无符号第一类Stirling数）生成的数值半群的结构。此类半群的生成元具有组合意义，作者希望刻画其约化生成系统的gcd滤链，从而揭示该半群的代数不变量（Frobenius数、亏格、导子、类型等），将数论（欧拉函数、p进估值）与半群理论结合起来。

Method: 令a=[n n-1]=C(n,2)，b_j=[n n-2j]。定义M_j=1/2 lcm{m≥1: φ(m)≤2j}，证明gcd(a,b_1,…,b_j)=a/gcd(a,M_j)。证明使用素幂块多项式（prime-power block polynomials）与阈值次数处的精确p进支撑（exact p-adic support）。随后建立规范混合基数约化（canonical mixed-radix reductions）的除数支撑性质，并结合初等对称增长估计，证明在去掉非活跃生成元后所得生成序列是telescopic的。

Result: 确定了约化生成系统的完整gcd滤链：gcd(a,b_1,…,b_j)=a/gcd(a,M_j)。证明生成序列（去除非活跃生成元后）是telescopic的，从而S_n的Apéry集为矩形，得到Frobenius数的显式公式；同时S_n是自由且对称的，并给出亏格与导子的显式公式，其类型为1。

Conclusion: 由Stirling数生成的数值半群S_n具有高度规则的结构：gcd滤链由欧拉函数阈值控制，生成序列为telescopic，Apéry集矩形，半群自由、对称、类型为1，各种不变量均有显式表达。

Abstract: Let $$ S_n=\left\langle \genfrac{[}{]}{0pt}{}{n}{1},\ldots,\genfrac{[}{]}{0pt}{}{n}{n-1}\right\rangle $$ be the numerical semigroup generated by the nontrivial unsigned Stirling numbers of the first kind in the $n$th row. Writing $a=\genfrac{[}{]}{0pt}{}{n}{n-1}=\binom n2$ and $b_j=\genfrac{[}{]}{0pt}{}{n}{n-2j}$, we determine the complete gcd filtration of the reduced generating system. More precisely, if \[ M_j=\frac12\operatorname{lcm}\{m\ge1:\varphi(m)\le2j\}, \] then $$ \gcd(a,b_1,\ldots,b_j)=\frac{a}{\gcd(a,M_j)}. $$ The proof uses prime-power block polynomials and exact $p$-adic support at threshold degrees. We then establish a divisor-support property for canonical mixed-radix reductions and combine it with an elementary-symmetric growth estimate to prove that, after inactive generators are removed, the resulting generating sequence is telescopic. Consequently, the Apéry set of $S_n$ is rectangular, yielding an explicit Frobenius formula. The same structure shows that $S_n$ is free and symmetric and gives explicit formulas for its genus and conductor, while its type is one.

</details>


### [104] [Cohomology of Linearly Constrained Kloosterman Families: Newton Polytopes, Weights, and Boundary Monodromy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03081)
*Hamed Ebadi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究任意维数上一族线性约束Kloosterman型指数和的上同调结构，通过Newton多面体与无穷远多面体的面逐层非退化论证，确定其集中性、秩、边界贡献、Swan导子、光滑性及权的完全滤过；证明坐标边界驯顺性并计算驯顺单幂Jordan块，识别最高权层的限制为中间卷积；利用Cayley三次曲面的四阶矩计算得到M_4=2（n≥3），结合非平凡边界单幂与Larsen替代定理，推出在指定特征范围内对一切n≥2有G^0_geom(W_n)=SL_{r(n)}，且论证不依赖有限群分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性约束Kloosterman型指数和的高权上同调、单值群与矩，需要统一处理任意维数并避免分类定理，理解其几何单值群的结构。

Method: 以相位函数的Newton多面体与无穷远多面体为工具，通过面逐层非退化论证分析临界点几何与紧支上同调；用局部Fourier变换证明坐标边界驯顺性以实施中间卷积；借助Cayley三次曲面的四阶矩计算与Larsen替代定理确定单值群。

Result: 确定集中性、秩、边界贡献、Swan导子、lissité及权的完全滤过，最高权秩为2^n−C(n,⌊n/2⌋)；计算驯顺单幂Jordan块，识别最高权层限制为中间卷积；四阶矩M_4=2（n≥3），进而G^0_geom(W_n)=SL_{r(n)}对一切n≥2成立。

Conclusion: 该工作给出任意维线性约束Kloosterman型指数和的完整上同调与权结构描述，并将几何单值群确定为SL_{r(n)}，方法避免有限群分类。

Abstract: We develop the cohomological structure of a family of linearly constrained Kloosterman-type exponential sums in arbitrary dimension. The phase is analyzed through its Newton polytope and the polytope at infinity, whose distinct normalized volumes govern respectively the critical-point geometry and compactly supported cohomology. After a face-by-face non-degeneracy argument, we determine concentration, rank, boundary contribution, Swan conductors, lissité, and the full weight filtration; the top-weight rank is $2^n-\binom{n}{\lfloor n/2\rfloor}$. We then prove the coordinate-boundary tameness needed for middle convolution by a local Fourier-transform argument, compute the tame unipotent Jordan blocks, and identify the restriction of the top-weight sheaf with the middle convolution. A complete fourth-moment calculation based on the Cayley cubic gives $M_4=2$ for $n\ge3$; together with the nontrivial boundary unipotent and Larsen's alternative this yields $G^0_{\mathrm{geom}}(\mathcal W_n)=\mathrm{SL}_{r(n)}$ for every $n\ge2$ in the stated characteristic range. The argument avoids finite-group classification.

</details>


### [105] [Cubic arithmetic-geometric mean and isogenies of Hesse elliptic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03100)
*Noriyuki Otsubo*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文构造了特征不等于3的任意域上Hesse椭圆曲线的三次同源，对应于三次算术-几何平均的一步；并应用该结果，用3进三次算术-几何平均刻画了特征3下普通椭圆曲线的Serre-Tate典范提升，同时讨论了Hesse曲线与复超几何函数及有限域超几何函数的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算术-几何平均（AGM）是联系椭圆曲线、模形式与超几何函数的经典工具，但其在三次情形的类比以及在正特征、p进提升中的应用尚需系统发展。作者希望建立Hesse椭圆曲线的三次同源与三次AGM的对应，并将其用于刻画特征3下普通椭圆曲线的Serre-Tate典范提升。

Method: 在特征不为3的任意域上，构造Hesse形式椭圆曲线的三次同源，证明其对应于三次算术-几何平均的一步；利用该构造，在特征3情形下通过3进三次AGM描述普通椭圆曲线的Serre-Tate典范提升；并结合复超几何函数与有限域超几何函数讨论Hesse曲线的关系。

Result: 给出了特征≠3域上Hesse椭圆曲线三次同源的显式构造，建立了其与三次AGM一步的对应；用3进三次AGM刻画了特征3普通椭圆曲线的Serre-Tate典范提升；揭示了Hesse曲线与复、有限超几何函数之间的联系。

Conclusion: 三次AGM可作为研究Hesse椭圆曲线同源、p进提升及超几何函数统一框架的有效工具，并将经典AGM理论推广至三次与正特征情形。

Abstract: We construct degree-three isogenies of Hesse elliptic curves over an arbitrary field of characteristic different from three, which correspond to a step of the cubic arithmetic-geometric mean. As an application, we describe the Serre-Tate canonical lift of an ordinary elliptic curve in characteristic three by the 3-adic cubic arithmetic-geometric mean. We also discuss the relations of the Hesse curves with complex and finite hypergeometric functions.

</details>


### [106] [Split-Cubic Curves from D(n)-Triples: A Bounded Finite-Field Census](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03121)
*Sompong Chuysurichay,Sawian Jaidee,Chatchawan Panraksa,Teerapol Sukhonwimolmal*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 对附着于正整数D(n)-三元组的split-cubic椭圆曲线E_{a,b,c}^{(n)}进行了有限域普查，记录了164个三元组在10个素数下的1640条约化记录及群阶、因子分解等信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对与D(n)-三元组相关的split-cubic椭圆曲线进行确定性的、描述性的有限域普查，收集标准split-cubic与二次扭转恒等式，用于审计点计数。

Method: 取n∈{-3,3,5,8,12,20}、1≤a<b<c≤100（允许零平方）的164个三元组，在NextPrime(10^k)，3≤k≤12的十个素数下约化，得到1640条记录；采用标准化λ=b(c-a)/(c(b-a))和δ=cn(b-a)的split-cubic与二次扭转恒等式审计点计数。

Result: 1640条记录中有45条相对于群阶最大素因子具有cofactor four；归档记录了群阶、因子分解、cofactor、嵌入次数和transfer-field比特长度；并以一个实例比较主曲线与其非平方扭转。

Conclusion: 该普查是确定性与描述性的，不提供渐近密度估计，也不给出密码学参数建议。

Abstract: We record a finite-field census of the split-cubic elliptic curves $E_{a,b,c}^{(n)}:y^2=(ax+n)(bx+n)(cx+n)$ attached to positive integral $D(n)$-triples. For $n\in\{-3,3,5,8,12,20\}$ there are $164$ such triples with $1\le a<b<c\le100$, allowing zero squares. Their reductions at the ten primes $\operatorname{NextPrime}(10^k)$, $3\le k\le12$, give $1640$ records, of which $45$ have cofactor four relative to the largest prime divisor of the group order. We collect the standard split-cubic and quadratic-twist identities in the explicit normalization $λ=b(c-a)/(c(b-a))$ and $δ=cn(b-a)$, and use them to audit the point counts. The archive records group orders, factorizations, cofactors, embedding degrees and transfer-field bit lengths. A worked example compares the main curve with its nonsquare twist. The census is deterministic and descriptive; it provides neither an asymptotic density estimate nor cryptographic parameter recommendations.

</details>


### [107] [Pellarin's Identity, Carlitz Period, and Anderson Generating Functions over Arbitrary Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03134)
*Chuangqiang Hu,Stephen S. -T. Yau,Lishan Yu*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文研究光滑射影曲线系数环上秩一Drinfeld模的特殊函数模与Pellarin L(1)-级数，证明特殊函数模含有Tate代数单位当且仅当其为秩一自由（解决Gazda–Maurischat猜想），给出基本周期与Drinfeld对数的留数公式，并将Pellarin L(1)-级数表示为生成微分的首次Frobenius扭。结果对任意N≥1成立，且源周期格不必自由。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究秩一Drinfeld模的特殊函数模结构及其与Pellarin L(1)-级数的关系，解决Gazda–Maurischat猜想，并将周期、对数与L(1)-级数统一在shtuka微分与留数的框架下，且推广到任意度数的无穷远点、源周期格非自由的一般情形。

Method: 以Cauchy核求值：在周期格上所有此类求值一致，其公共特征留数恢复周期；对到主周期目标的合适同源数据，使用严格标准化shtuka乘积与一个特征留数给出基本周期的显式公式；同一亏格方程导出Drinfeld对数的留数公式；并将标准化shtuka微分的首次Frobenius扭与由有限挠迹构成的配对等同，其系数为定义域上的有限étale迹。

Result: 证明特殊函数模含Tate代数单位当且仅当其为秩一自由，等价于周期格同构于正则微分模，解决Gazda–Maurischat猜想；给出基本周期（自由格生成元）的显式公式与Drinfeld对数的留数公式；将标准化shtuka微分的首次Frobenius扭与有限挠迹配对等同，并据此把Pellarin L(1)-级数精确表示为基本周期处生成微分的首次Frobenius扭。

Conclusion: 三个主要结果统一了特殊函数模、周期、Drinfeld对数与Pellarin L(1)-级数，且对任意N≥1成立，源周期格无需自由，推广了以往限制性假设。

Abstract: Let \(A\) be the coefficient ring of a smooth projective curve over \(\mathbb{F}_q\) with a closed point at infinity of arbitrary degree \(\mathrm{N}=°(\infty)\ge 1\), and let \(\varphi\) be a rank-one Drinfeld \(A\)-module. In this paper, we prove three main results concerning the module of special functions and its relation to Pellarin's \(\mathcal L(1)\)-series. First, the module \(\mathrm{sf}(\varphi)\) of special functions contains a Tate-algebra unit exactly when it is free of rank one, equivalently when its period lattice is isomorphic to the module of regular differentials. This settles the Gazda--Maurischat conjecture. The proof evaluates Cauchy kernels; on the period lattice all such evaluations agree, and their common characteristic residue recovers the period. Second, for suitable isogeny data to a principal-period target \(ψ\), a strictly normalized shtuka product, together with one characteristic residue, gives an explicit formula for the fundamental period \(\tildeπ\) (the generator of the free lattice \(Λ_ψ\)). The same defect equation also yields the residue formula for the Drinfeld logarithm. Third, the first Frobenius twist of the normalized shtuka differential is identified with a pairing built from finite torsion traces, whose coefficients are finite étale traces over the field of definition. As an application, Pellarin's \(\mathcal L(1)\)-series is expressed exactly as the first Frobenius twist of the generating differential at a fundamental period. A notable feature of these results is that they hold for arbitrary $\mathrm{N} \geq 1$, and the source period lattice need not be free.

</details>


### [108] [The quality plane of $abc$-triples and the decomposition into gains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03212)
*R. Laniewski,K. Müller*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文为互素三元组 $a+b=c$ 引入质量坐标 $X$ 与对数质量坐标 $Y$，其比值 $\lambda$ 渐近位于 $2$ 与 $3$ 之间；在此坐标系下 $abc$ 猜想与 Frey 曲线 Szpiro 猜想化为阈值 $X=1$、$Y=3$ 的渐近界，并把 Müller、Taktikos、de Weger 的逼近与幂增益纳入同一平面，得到若干渐近上界与等价刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望为 $abc$ 猜想与 Szpiro 猜想建立一个统一的几何/坐标框架，使两者的直接蕴含关系由单一参数 $\lambda$ 的取值范围自然导出，并整合已有的逼近增益结果，以便更清晰地比较不同猜想、不同呈现方式下的强弱与等价性。

Method: 对互素三元组 $a+b=c$ 定义质量坐标 $X$ 与对数质量坐标 $Y$，用比值 $\lambda$ 衡量三元组平衡；在此坐标系中重写 $abc$ 猜想与 Frey 曲线 Szpiro 猜想为渐近阈值条件，并引入相对给定呈现的指数因子 $f$，将 Müller、Taktikos、de Weger 的逼近与幂增益纳入同一平面分析。

Result: $abc$ 猜想对应渐近界 $X=1$，Szpiro 猜想对应 $Y=3$，两者直接蕴含关系由 $\lambda\in(2,3)$ 的范围推出；相对固定呈现，Szpiro 猜想等价于增益因子 $f\le 3$ 的一致渐近界；在两个独立的渐近增益界下，当 radical 趋于无穷时得到 $\limsup X\le 9/2$ 与 $\limsup Y\le 27/2$；更强结论依赖于呈现选择或增益的联合界。

Conclusion: 该坐标框架统一刻画了 $abc$ 与 Szpiro 猜想的关系，并给出可量化的渐近上界；进一步改进取决于呈现方式或增益的联合估计，文末附数值数据与两个研究目标。

Abstract: To a coprime triple $a+b=c$ we attach two coordinates, the quality $X$ and the logarithmic mass $Y$, whose ratio $λ$ measures the balance of the triple and lies asymptotically between $2$ and $3$. In these coordinates the $abc$ conjecture and Szpiro's conjecture for Frey curves become asymptotic bounds with thresholds $X=1$ and $Y=3$, and the direct implications between them follow from the range of $λ$. We then place the approximation and power gains of Müller, Taktikos and de Weger in the same plane. Relative to a chosen presentation of the triple, one exponent factor $f$ relates the gains to the coordinates, and Szpiro's conjecture for this family is equivalent to the asymptotic bound $f\le 3$ uniformly over all presentations. Under the two separate asymptotic gain bounds for a fixed choice of presentations, we obtain $\limsup X\le 9/2$ and $\limsup Y\le 27/2$ as the radical tends to infinity. Stronger conclusions depend on the presentation or on a joint bound for the gains. We close with numerical data and two research objectives.

</details>


### [109] [Gosper's and Ramanujan's infinite products and generalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03238)
*Jean-Paul Allouche,John M. Campbell,Simon R. Holcombe,Lubomir Markov,Manon Stipulanti*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文对Gosper未给出完整证明的无穷乘积公式进行推广，构造含三个自由参数的广义公式，可评价其任意多分拆，并研究涉及高次多项式的高阶推广，联系Ramanujan、Kurokawa与Rovinskiĭ的乘积结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gosper提出了大量涉及无穷乘积的著名公式，其中一个涉及$$\prod_{n \geq 1} \frac{1}{e}\left(\frac{1}{3n} + 1\right)^{3n + \frac{1}{2}}$$的求值公式似乎在文献中尚无完整证明；同时作者希望将此类结果向更一般的形式及相关经典乘积推广。

Method: 引入若干技巧以扩展Gosper的求值结果：证明一个含三个自由参数的Gosper公式推广，可用于求Gosper乘积的任意多分拆；进一步研究以更高次多项式（相对于Gosper乘积中的3n与3n+1/2）为核心的高阶扩展，并借助作者先前工作考察与Kurokawa和Rovinskiĭ无穷乘积的联系。

Result: 建立了含三个自由参数的Gosper公式推广；该推广可求Gosper乘积的任意多分拆；得到高阶扩展，推广了Ramanujan的一个无穷乘积求值结果（该结果近期由Bradley–Thrush研究）；并给出与Kurokawa和Rovinskiĭ无穷乘积的联系。

Conclusion: 本文为Gosper乘积公式提供了完整证明路径与多参数推广，将其拓展至任意多分拆与高阶多项式情形，并统一联系到Ramanujan、Kurokawa及Rovinskiĭ等人的无穷乘积结果，深化了相关理论。

Abstract: Gosper introduced many remarkable formulas involving infinite products. One such formula provides an evaluation of the product $$\prod_{n \geq 1} \frac{1}{e}\left(\frac{1}{3n} + 1\right)^{3n + \frac{1}{2}}. $$ It appears that a complete proof of this evaluation has not appeared in the literature. In this paper, we introduce some techniques to extend Gosper's evaluation. We prove a generalization of Gosper's formula involving three free parameters, which can also be applied to evaluate arbitrary multisections of Gosper's product. Then we investigate higher-order extensions involving higher-degree polynomials in $n$, relative to the polynomials $3n$ and $3n+\frac{1}{2}$ involved in Gosper's product, yielding a generalization of an infinite product evaluation due to Ramanujan and recently studied by Bradley--Thrush [\textit{\it Ramanujan J.} (2025)]. We also consider connections with infinite products due to Kurokawa and Rovinskiĭ, building on the recent work of the first and fourth authors [\textit{\it Ramanujan J.} (2025)].

</details>


### [110] [A cubic Chabauty criterion for hyperelliptic curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03255)
*Lee Berry*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了有理Weierstrass点的超椭圆曲线在深度3 Chabauty--Kim集有限性的判别准则，并给出Jacobian Néron--Severi秩1、Mordell--Weil秩4的亏格2曲线实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chabauty--Kim方法在高亏格曲线上确定有理点有限性时，深度3的情形尚缺乏可操作的判别条件，尤其在Néron--Severi秩与Mordell--Weil秩较高的情形下，有限性验证更为困难。

Method: 针对具有有理Weierstrass点的超椭圆曲线，建立深度3 Chabauty--Kim集X(Q_2)_3有限性的判别准则，并通过具体亏格2曲线实例验证准则的适用性。

Result: 得到了X(Q_2)_3有限性的判别准则，并构造出满足该准则的亏格2曲线，其Jacobian的Néron--Severi秩为1、Mordell--Weil秩为4。

Conclusion: 所提出准则可有效判断深度3 Chabauty--Kim集的有限性，且适用于Néron--Severi秩较低而Mordell--Weil秩较高的非平凡情形。

Abstract: We establish a criterion for finiteness of the depth 3 Chabauty--Kim set $X(\mathbb{Q}_2)_3$ for hyperelliptic curves $X$ over $\mathbb{Q}$ with a rational Weierstrass point. To illustrate its applicability, we include an example of a genus $2$ curve satisfying the criterion whose Jacobian has Néron--Severi rank $1$ and Mordell--Weil rank $4$.

</details>


### [111] [Eisenstein congruences in tame families and the class group of a metabelian extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03401)
*Catherine Hsu,Alice Pozzi,Preston Wake,Carl Wang-Erickson*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文利用驯顺族（tame families）中Eisenstein同余的新存在性结果，解释了一个关于素数在某个亚阿贝尔数域中分裂行为的计算观察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ribet 1976年对Herbrand定理逆命题的证明开创了代数数论的新范式：利用与Eisenstein同余（即与Eisenstein级数同余的尖点特征形式）相伴的伽罗瓦表示来构造数域的未分歧阿贝尔伽罗瓦扩张。近年研究表明，当此类Eisenstein同余数量众多时，可获得关于相关扩张算术结构的更精细信息。本文延续此思路，旨在解释一个具体的计算观察。

Method: 采用关于驯顺族中Eisenstein同余存在性的新结果，将其应用于分析某个亚阿贝尔数域中素数分裂行为的计算观察。

Result: 成功解释了该亚阿贝尔数域中素数分裂行为的计算观察。

Conclusion: Eisenstein同余的存在性结果可用于阐明数域扩张的算术性质，进一步印证并拓展了Ribet所开创的研究范式。

Abstract: Ribet's 1976 proof of the converse to Herbrand's theorem created a new paradigm in algebraic number theory by illustrating that unramified abelian Galois extensions of number fields can be constructed using Galois representations associated to Eisenstein congruences, i.e., cuspidal eigenforms that are congruent to Eisenstein series. In recent years, further progress has been made in investigating the quantity of such Eisenstein congruences, showing that when there are many such congruences, additional and finer information can be gleaned about the arithmetic structure of the related extensions. In this vein, we use new results on the existence of Eisenstein congruences in tame families to explain a computational observation about splitting behavior of primes in a certain metabelian number field.

</details>


### [112] [Sum-product patterns in the shifted primes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03417)
*Florian K. Richter,Joni Teräväinen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了平移素数集合 $\mathbb{P}-1$ 中包含无穷多个形如 $\{x,x+y,xy\}$ 的和积模式，且对任意 $k\geq 1$ 包含更长的 $\{x,x+y,\ldots,x+ky,xy\}$ 模式，该结论涵盖 Green–Tao 定理作为特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将加法结构（等差数列）与乘法结构（乘积）结合，研究平移素数集合中同时具有加法与乘法特征的模式存在性，并推广 Green–Tao 定理。

Method: 数论/加性组合方法，研究 $\mathbb{P}-1$ 中形如 $\{x,x+y,\ldots,x+ky,xy\}$ 的和积配置，通过构造使得 $x, x+y,\ldots,x+ky$ 落在 $\mathbb{P}-1$ 中且 $xy\in\mathbb{P}-1$。

Result: $\mathbb{P}-1$ 包含无穷多个 $\{x,x+y,xy\}$ 和积模式，且对任意 $k\geq1$ 包含无穷多个 $\{x,x+y,\ldots,x+ky,xy\}$ 模式，$x,y$ 为任意大的相异整数；其中长度 $k+1$ 的等差部分蕴含 Green–Tao 定理。

Conclusion: 平移素数集合具有丰富的加法-乘法联合结构，存在任意长的等差数列与乘积相结合的配置，从而统一并推广了素数中算术级数存在性的经典结果。

Abstract: We show that the set $\mathbb{P}-1$ of shifted primes contains infinitely many sum-product patterns of the form $\{x,x+y,xy\}$ with $x,y$ arbitrarily large distinct integers. More strongly, we can also show that, for any $k\geq 1$, the set $\mathbb{P}-1$ contains longer patterns of the form $\{x,x+y,\ldots, x+ky,xy\}$ with $x,y$ arbitrarily large distinct integers, a statement that contains the Green--Tao theorem as a special case.

</details>


### [113] [Hausdorff dimension for exactly approximable matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03442)
*Yubin He,Bixuan Li,Baowei Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文确定了精确可逼近矩阵的Hausdorff维数，覆盖所有维数及非齐次情形，在$\tau_\psi>n/m$时给出显式维数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Diophantine逼近理论研究可逼近集合的维数（如Jarník-Besicovitch型结果），但"精确可逼近"集合（可被$\psi$逼近却不能被任意$c\psi$逼近）在矩阵及非齐次情形下的维数尚待建立。该文旨在统一刻画这类精确逼近集的Hausdorff维数。

Method: 采用概率/平均计数的视角：通过平均计数论证量化有理向量（$\frac{\mathbf p+\boldsymbol\theta}{q}$）与有理子空间（满足$A\mathbf q-\mathbf p-\boldsymbol\theta=0$的矩阵集）的分布规律，进而估计精确可逼近集的覆盖与维数。

Result: 证明了对任意$m,n\ge1$、$\boldsymbol\theta\in\mathbb R^m$，当$\tau_\psi>n/m$时有 $$\dim_H \Exact_{m,n}(\psi,\boldsymbol\theta)=m(n-1)+\frac{m+n}{1+\tau_\psi}.$$ 该结果同时涵盖齐次（$\boldsymbol\theta=0$）与非齐次情形。

Conclusion: 该工作将精确可逼近集的Hausdorff维数刻画推广到一般矩阵及非齐次设定，维数由近似函数的低阶$\tau_\psi$与维数$m,n$显式决定，验证了精确逼近与普通逼近维数分裂的普遍性。

Abstract: We establish the Hausdorff dimension for exactly approximable matrices for all dimensions, including the inhomogeneous case. More precisely, let $ψ:\N\to(0,\infty)$ be a non-increasing approximation function and let $τ_ψ$ denote its lower order. For $m,n\ge1$ and $\boldsymbolθ\in\mathbb R^m$, denote $\Exact_{m,n}(ψ,\boldsymbolθ)$ the set of $m\times n$ matrices that are $ψ$-approximable with shift $\boldsymbolθ$, but are not $cψ$-approximable for any $0<c<1$. It is shown that \[ \hdim \Exact_{m,n}(ψ,\boldsymbolθ)=m(n-1)+\frac{m+n}{1+τ_ψ},\ \ \ {\text{for}}\ τ_ψ>\frac{n}{m}. \] We adopt a probabilistic viewpoint, i.e. by an averaged counting argument to quantify the distribution of rational vectors and subspaces $$\left\{\frac{\mathbf p+\boldsymbolθ}{q}: (\mathbf p, q)\in \mathbb{Z}^{m+1}\right\},\ \ \ \bigg\{\big\{A\in \Mat_{m, n}(\R): A\mathbf q-\mathbf p-\boldsymbolθ=0\big\}: (\mathbf p, \mathbf q)\in \mathbb{Z}^{m+n}\bigg\}.$$

</details>


### [114] [Explicit second moments of symplectic Siegel transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03499)
*Kristian Holm,Seungki Kim*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文给出了 $\mathrm{Sp}(2n,\mathbb{Z}) \backslash \mathrm{Sp}(2n,\mathbb{R})$ 上 Siegel 变换二阶矩公式的两种替代表述，并展示了若干应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始 Siegel 变换二阶矩公式由 Kelmer 与 Yu 建立，但其形式可能不便直接应用；本文旨在提供等价但更便于使用的替代表述，以拓展其应用范围。

Method: 在辛群商空间 $\mathrm{Sp}(2n,\mathbb{Z}) \backslash \mathrm{Sp}(2n,\mathbb{R})$ 上，重新推导并给出 Siegel 变换二阶矩公式的两种不同表述形式。

Result: 得到了 Siegel 变换二阶矩公式的两种替代表述，并给出若干应用以说明其有效性。

Conclusion: 新的表述形式与 Kelmer–Yu 的结果等价，且在实际应用中更为便利。

Abstract: We give two alternate formulations for the second moment formula of the Siegel transform over $\mathrm{Sp}(2n, \mathbb{Z}) \backslash \mathrm{Sp}(2n,\mathbb{R})$, originally established by Kelmer and Yu. We also provide several applications that demonstrate their usefulness.

</details>


### [115] [Quantitative Gowers uniformity of the primes in intervals of length $X^{5/8+\varepsilon}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03707)
*Joni Teräväinen*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了von Mangoldt函数减去Siegel修正模型函数在短区间上Gowers范数的首个定量上界，适用于$H\geq X^{5/8+\varepsilon}$，并给出$U^k$范数的拟多项式节省；核心是非阿贝尔Type II逆定理的高效版本，显式依赖于nilsequence维数，并作为证明移位素数$\mathbb{P}-1$中无穷多$\{x,x+y,xy\}$和积模式的一个要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在短区间上量化von Mangoldt函数（减去Siegel修正）的Gowers范数，以刻画素数分布的更高阶一致性与结构，并将此类定量估计应用于移位素数中的和积模式问题。

Method: 证明非阿贝尔Type II逆定理的高效版本，显式给出对nilsequence维数的依赖；方法是将Leng的四参数高效等分布定理直接应用于四变量无权重角相关，从而替代Matomäki、Shao、Tao及作者在非阿贝尔Type II证明中的因子分解与子群论证。

Result: 对区间$(X,X+H]$且$H\geq X^{5/8+\varepsilon}$，得到$U^k(X,X+H]$范数的拟多项式节省，这是该类Gowers范数定量上界的首个结果；该定量结果被用作证明移位素数$\mathbb{P}-1$中存在无穷多个形如$\{x,x+y,xy\}$的和积模式的一个要素（与Florian Richter合作）。

Conclusion: 建立了短区间上von Mangoldt函数（减去Siegel修正模型函数）Gowers范数的首个定量上界，提供了更高效、依赖维数显式的非阿贝尔Type II逆定理，并据此推进了移位素数中$\{x,x+y,xy\}$和积模式无穷性的研究。

Abstract: We prove the first quantitative bounds for the Gowers norms of the von Mangoldt function minus a Siegel corrected model function in short intervals. The result applies to intervals $(X,X+H]$ with $H\geq X^{5/8+\varepsilon}$ and gives quasipolynomial savings for the $U^k(X,X+H]$ norm. The main new ingredient is an efficient non-abelian Type II inverse theorem with explicit dependence on the nilsequence dimension. We prove this inverse theorem by applying Leng's efficient equidistribution theorem in four parameters directly to an unweighted corner correlation in four variables, hence replacing the factorisation and subgroup argument in the non-abelian Type II proof of Matomäki, Shao, Tao and the author.
  In joint work with Florian Richter, we use this quantitative result as one ingredient in proving the existence of infinitely many sum-product patterns of the form $\{x,x+y,xy\}$ in the shifted primes $\mathbb{P}-1$.

</details>


<div id='math.FA'></div>

# math.FA [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Orthogonality, Spectral Filling, and Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02484)
*Travis Alvarez,Trevor Camper,Mishko Mitkovski*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文利用Parseval框架系数满足的"基本排他原理"（总质量等于状态数且逐点≤1），为族的总代价给出显式下界；对径向代价得到集体均值-色散、雨伞与传输不等式，并将结论推广到Bessel族与半经典变框架情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在再生核Hilbert空间的相空间中，希望以纯框架/算子方法统一刻画正交族的能量代价与低代价区域的体积之间的关系，并推广到框架测度随参数变化的半经典极限，从而覆盖Bargmann–Fock、加权Bergman、Gabor–Paley–Wiener以及正交多项式与行列式系综（含GUE）等情形。

Method: 设归一化连续Parseval框架刻画相空间；利用框架系数逐点≤1、总质量等于状态数的排他原理导出正交族总代价关于低代价区域体积的下界。对径向代价，通过该下界推出集体均值-色散、雨伞与传输型不等式；对归一化Bessel族以Bessel界刻画允许重叠。半经典情形下，假设归一化测度弱收敛且归一化核平均局部化，进而证明连续符号Szegő极限与最低Toeplitz能量的精确渐近。

Result: 建立了基于排他原理的显式总代价下界；得到径向代价下的集体均值-色散、雨伞与传输不等式；给出归一化Bessel族的重叠界推广；在弱收敛与平均局部化条件下证明了连续符号Szegő极限与最低Toeplitz能量的尖锐渐近；结果适用于Bargmann–Fock与加权Bergman空间、Gabor与Paley–Wiener空间，以及正交多项式与行列式系综（包括GUE）。

Conclusion: 框架系数的基本排他原理是导出代价下界与集体不等式的有效统一工具，并可自然延伸至半经典变框架情形，为多种经典函数空间与随机矩阵系综提供统一的Szegő极限与能量渐近框架。

Abstract: Let a normalized continuous Parseval frame describe the phase space of a reproducing kernel Hilbert space. The frame coefficients of an orthonormal family have total mass equal to the number of states and pointwise mass at most one. This elementary exclusion principle gives explicit lower bounds for the total cost of a family in terms of the volume of low-cost regions. For radial costs, it yields collective mean-dispersion, umbrella, and transport inequalities. The same argument applies to normalized Bessel families, with the Bessel bound measuring the permitted overlap.
  We also consider semiclassical families for which the frame measures vary. Weak convergence of the normalized measures, together with averaged localization of the normalized kernels, yields continuous-symbol Szegő limits and sharp asymptotics for the lowest Toeplitz energies. The results apply to Bargmann--Fock and weighted Bergman spaces, Gabor and Paley--Wiener spaces, and orthogonal-polynomial and determinantal ensembles, including the GUE.

</details>


### [117] [Input-Endpoint Compactness and Essential Norms for Weighted Bilinear Hardy Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02649)
*Saikat Kanjilal*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究加权Hardy原算子乘积映射到$L^q(u)$的紧性，聚焦输入指数含端点$1$或$\infty$的边界情形，给出紧性的阈值刻画与度量刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权Hardy型算子的有界性已有较多结果，但乘积算子进入$L^q$的紧性在输入指数取端点（$1$或$\infty$）时结构不同，需明确紧性成立的阈值及临界权重的非紧反例。

Method: 使用累积Stieltjes测度及初始原子、严格Hardy-Copson括号进行端点化归；通过输出质量分配论证证明局部有限秩稠密性（含拟Banach目标）；推广内点输入情形的局部化原理，将本质距离、有限秩距离与Hausdorff非紧半径等同于耦合active tail；对有限左端点用线性节点逼近完成标量转移。

Result: 对$L^1\times L^p$（$1<p<\infty$），当$q<p/(p+1)$时有界蕴含紧；$L^1\times L^1$对应范围为$q<1/2$。在阈值及以上，紧性由附加的active-tail衰减刻画；临界幂权给出有界非紧例子。本质/有限秩距离与Hausdorff非紧半径等于耦合active tail，$q<1$时取相应度量幂。

Conclusion: 端点输入下乘积Hardy原算子的紧性可由阈值条件与active-tail衰减完全刻画，且非紧性度量与active tail一致，统一了内点与端点输入情形。

Abstract: We study compactness of the product of two weighted Hardy primitives into $L^q(u)$, $0<q<\infty$, on the input boundary $1\le p_1,p_2\le\infty$, where at least one input exponent is $1$ or $\infty$. For $L^1\times L^p$, $1<p<\infty$, boundedness implies compactness when $q<p/(p+1)$. For $L^1\times L^1$, the corresponding range is $q<1/2$. At and above these boundaries compactness is characterized by additional active-tail decay, and critical power weights give bounded noncompact examples. The endpoint reductions use cumulative Stieltjes measures with their initial atoms and strict Hardy-Copson brackets. An output-mass allocation argument proves local finite-rank density, including quasi-Banach targets. Applying the localization principles developed for the interior-input regime identifies the essential distance, finite-rank distance and Hausdorff noncompactness radius with the coupled active tail, with the appropriate metric power when $q<1$. For a finite left endpoint, ordinary $L^\infty$ inputs admit an exact scalar transfer completed by linear nodal approximation.

</details>


### [118] [Hilbert-Schmidt Norms of Self-Commutator and Cross-Commutator on $N_η$-type Quotient Modules in Polydisc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02692)
*Anjian Xu,Yuchao Xiang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究Hardy空间$H^2(\mathbb{D}^d)$上由单变量内函数诱导的子模$M_\eta$及其商模$N_\eta$，刻画了压缩乘法算子$S_{z_i}$的自交换子$[S_{z_i}^*,S_{z_i}]$与互交换子$[S_{z_i}^*,S_{z_j}]$（$i\neq j$）的Hilbert-Schmidt性质，并在成立时给出其Hilbert-Schmidt范数的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩移位算子的交换子性质是算子理论与函数空间论的核心问题之一，Hilbert-Schmidt性关系到算子的紧性、本质正规性等结构特征。对多变量Hardy空间上内函数诱导的商模，其压缩乘法算子交换子的Hilbert-Schmidt刻画此前尚不完整，本文旨在补足这一理论空白并给出可计算的范数公式。

Method: 设$\eta_1,\cdots,\eta_d$为非平凡单变量内函数且$\eta_i(0)=0$，由$\{\eta_i(z_i)-\eta_{i+1}(z_{i+1})\mid 1\le i\le d-1\}$生成子模$M_\eta$，取商模$N_\eta=H^2(\mathbb{D}^d)\ominus M_\eta$，定义压缩乘法算子$S_{z_i}=P_{N_\eta}M_{z_i}|_{N_\eta}$。利用再生核函数刻画、内函数的结构性质及交换子的核函数表达式，分析其Hilbert-Schmidt性，并借助核函数的积分与求和计算范数。

Result: 刻画了自交换子$[S_{z_i}^*,S_{z_i}]$与互交换子$[S_{z_i}^*,S_{z_j}]$（$i\neq j$）为Hilbert-Schmidt的充要条件；在交换子为Hilbert-Schmidt时，建立了其Hilbert-Schmidt范数的显式公式。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了内函数诱导商模上压缩乘法算子交换子的Hilbert-Schmidt性判别准则及范数精确表达，深化了对多变量压缩移位算子结构及其本质正规性的理解。

Abstract: Let $η_{1},\cdots,η_{d}$ be nonconstant one-variable inner functions satisfying $η_{i}(0)=0$ for all $1\leq i\leq d$. Denote by $M_η$ the submodule of the Hardy space $H^{2}(\mathbb{D}^{d})$ generated by the function family $\{η_{i}(z_{i})-η_{i+1}(z_{i+1})\mid 1\leq i\leq d-1\}$. For the corresponding quotient module $N_η=H^{2}(\mathbb{D}^{d})\ominus M_η$, we define the compressed multiplication operators $S_{z_{i}}=P_{N_η}M_{z_{i}}\vert_{N_η}$, where $P_{N_η}$ is the orthogonal projection from $H^{2}(\mathbb{D}^{d})$ onto $N_η$. In this paper, we characterize the Hilbert-Schmidt property of the self-commutators $[S_{z_{i}}^{\ast},S_{z_{i}}]$ and cross-commutators $[S_{z_{i}}^{\ast},S_{z_{j}}]$ with $i\neq j$. Moreover, explicit formulas for their Hilbert-Schmidt norms are established whenever these commutators are Hilbert-Schmidt.

</details>


### [119] [Compactness and Essential Distances of Weighted Bilinear Hardy-Steklov Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02937)
*Saikat Kanjilal*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究半直线上的加权双线性Hardy-Steklov算子，建立了精确的输出局部化原理，将紧性与输出尾不变量的消失等价，并在五个加权有界性区域中刻画了该不变量的行为，揭示了P=Q与P>Q之间的尖锐结构性转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双线性Hardy-Steklov算子的紧性刻画此前缺乏系统的框架。作者希望在一般加权设定下，找到紧性、本质距离与有限秩逼近之间的精确关系，并厘清加权有界性区域中紧性何时需要额外条件、何时自动成立。

Method: 提出精确的输出局部化原理：对有界双线性映射到L^q，用紧的中间截断将紧性等价于某个有向输出尾不变量消失；该不变量以常数1等于本质距离及基于半径的Hausdorff非紧性测度。在局部有限秩逼近假设下，该不变量也等于有限秩距离。随后在Hardy-Steklov映射上验证这些假设，并穿越五个加权有界性区域进行翻译。对0<q<1的拟Banach情形使用幂度量d_q(f,g)=||f-g||_q^q处理。

Result: 在区域I、II_1、II_2、III_1中，有界性须补充下端与上端点的消失条件，且本质距离与较大的端点缺陷可比。在严格次临界区域III_2中，有界性本身即蕴含紧性。由此在等式边界P=Q与可和区域P>Q之间得到尖锐的结构性转变。

Conclusion: 加权双线性Hardy-Steklov算子的紧性可由统一的有向输出尾不变量完全刻画，该不变量同时控制本质距离、Hausdorff非紧性测度与有限秩距离。五个有界性区域呈现两种截然不同的紧性机制，P=Q与P>Q之间存在尖锐相变。

Abstract: We study weighted bilinear Hardy-Steklov operators on the half-line for $1<p_1,p_2<\infty$ and $0<q<\infty$. The operator-theoretic core is an exact output-localisation principle. For a bounded bilinear map into $L^q$, compact middle truncations identify compactness with vanishing of a directed output-tail invariant and, with constant one, identify that invariant with the essential distance and a radius-based Hausdorff measure of noncompactness. Under the additional hypothesis of local finite-rank approximability, the same invariant also equals the finite-rank distance and governs the rank-approximation conclusions. We verify these hypotheses for the Hardy-Steklov map and then translate the tail invariant through the five weighted boundedness regions. In Regions $I,\mathrm{II}_1,\mathrm{II}_2,\mathrm{III}_1$, boundedness must be supplemented by lower- and upper-endpoint vanishing, and the essential distance is comparable to the larger endpoint defect. In the strict subcritical Region $\mathrm{III}_2$, boundedness alone implies compactness. This yields a sharp structural transition between the equality boundary $P=Q$ and the summable regime $P>Q$. The quasi-Banach range $0<q<1$ is treated throughout with the powered metric $d_q(f,g)=\|f-g\|_q^q$.

</details>


### [120] [A modular theory of contractive projections on Banach spaces with applications to noncommutative $\mathrm{L}^p$-spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03375)
*Cédric Arhancet*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该论文基于多复变函数（多重次调和函数）的Levi形式，建立了一套处理复Banach空间上收缩投影的微分几何方法，并将其应用于迹非交换L^p空间，刻画了2-收缩投影与完全收缩投影的等价性及投影值域的代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 收缩投影与完全收缩投影在算子空间理论中具有重要意义，但缺乏统一的几何框架来理解其结构。本文旨在借助Levi形式提供的Hilbertian几何，从微分几何角度统一刻画复Banach空间及非交换L^p空间上收缩投影的性质，并揭示其与Jordan三元正交性、模算子及W*-TRO等代数结构的联系。

Method: 引入基于多重次调和函数Levi形式的微分几何方法：若投影P收缩一个C^2多重次调和函数Φ，则在值域上Levi形式定义了使P成为正交投影的切Hilbertian几何；在值域有限维且Levi形式非退化时，通过比较不同点的Levi几何构造正的相对算子及单参数群。将此框架应用于迹非交换L^4空间，利用对偶映射的三次形式，用Jordan三元正交性刻画收缩投影；在有限维von Neumann代数情形，将相对算子与链接von Neumann代数上模算子的逆平方根限制相联系，并结合W*-TRO进行三元描述。

Result: 证明了L^4(M)上的投影是2-收缩的当且仅当它是完全收缩的；有限维情形下，2-收缩投影的值域与某W*-TRO对应的矩形非交换L^4空间完全等距，且每个此类投影都是可收缩分解的；对任意1<p<∞且p≠2，迹非交换L^p空间上的有限秩2-收缩投影均为完全收缩的，且当p>2时其值域可由有限维W*-TRO给出加权三元描述。

Conclusion: Levi形式的微分几何方法为复Banach空间上的收缩投影提供了统一的几何刻画，并在非交换L^p空间上导出了2-收缩与完全收缩的等价性以及值域的W*-TRO三元结构描述，深化了对收缩投影代数与几何性质的理解。

Abstract: We develop a differential-geometric approach to contractive projections on complex Banach spaces based on Levi forms of plurisubharmonic functions. We show that if a projection $P$ contracts a $\mathrm{C}^2$ plurisubharmonic function $Φ$, then, at any point of its range, the Levi form of $Φ$ defines a tangent Hilbertian geometry for which $P$ becomes an orthogonal projection. When the range is finite-dimensional and the relevant Levi forms are nondegenerate, comparing the Levi geometries associated with different points leads naturally to positive relative operators and associated one-parameter groups. We apply this framework to tracial noncommutative $\mathrm{L}^4$-spaces. Exploiting the cubic form of the duality mapping, we characterize contractive projections by a Jordan triple orthogonality property and prove that a projection on $\mathrm{L}^4(\mathcal{M})$ is $2$-contractive if and only if it is completely contractive. When the ambient von Neumann algebra is finite-dimensional, we identify the relative operator associated with the left and right tangent forms on the range with a restriction of an inverse square root of a modular operator on a linking von Neumann algebra. This entails that the range of any $2$-contractive projection is completely isometric to a rectangular noncommutative $\mathrm{L}^4$-space associated with a $\mathrm{W}^*$-ternary ring of operators. As a consequence, every such projection is contractively decomposable. Finally, for every $1 < p < \infty$ with $p \neq 2$, we prove that every finite-rank $2$-contractive projection on a tracial noncommutative $\mathrm{L}^p$-space is completely contractive. For $p > 2$, we obtain a weighted ternary description of its range in terms of a finite-dimensional $\mathrm{W}^*$-$\mathrm{TRO}$.

</details>


### [121] [Norm-Additive Maps on the Positive Unit Sphere of $C^1([0,1])$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02862)
*Kazuki Ezumi,Takeshi Miura*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 在赋予特定范数的 $C^1(I)$ 正单位球面 $\mathcal{S}_p^+$ 上，证明了当 $1<p\le\infty$ 时任意满射范数可加映射具有与同胚相关的积分形式，并可延拓为线性等距同构；而当 $p=1$ 时构造反例说明该结论不成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究映射的范数可加性（norm-additivity）是Mazur-Ulam型定理与等距理论的重要问题。本文旨在刻画连续可微函数空间上正单位球面之间保持范数和的映射结构，并考察参数 $p$ 对结果的影响。

Method: 在 $C^1(I)$ 上定义依赖于 $|f(0)|$ 与 $\|f'\|_I$ 的 $p$-型范数 $\|\cdot\|_{(p)}$，取正单位球面 $\mathcal{S}_p^+$，考虑满足 $\|T(f)+T(g)\|_{(p)}=\|f+g\|_{(p)}$ 的满射范数可加映射，通过分析该等式对范数结构与函数单调性的约束，推导 $T$ 的积分表示并给出 $p=1$ 的反例。

Result: 当 $1<p\le\infty$ 时，每个满射范数可加映射 $T:\mathcal{S}_p^+\to\mathcal{S}_p^+$ 均可表示为 $T(f)(t)=f(0)+\int_0^t f'(\tau(s))\,ds$，其中 $\tau:I\to I$ 是同胚；反之该形式均为满射范数可加映射。由此每个此类映射可延拓为 $(C^1(I),\|\cdot\|_{(p)})$ 上的满射线性等距同构。当 $p=1$ 时，构造出满射范数可加映射，其不具上述形式且无线性延拓。

Conclusion: 范数可加映射的结构定理在 $1<p\le\infty$ 时成立并可线性延拓为等距同构，而 $p=1$ 时结论失效，说明条件 $p>1$ 是本质的。

Abstract: Let $C^1(I)$ denote the space of all continuously differentiable real-valued functions on the closed unit interval $I=[0,1]$. For $1\le p\le\infty$, we equip $C^1(I)$ with the norm \[ \Vert{f}\Vert_{(p)} := \begin{cases} \left(|f(0)|^p+\Vert{f'}\Vert_{I}^p\right)^{1/p}, &
1\le p<\infty, \\ \max\{|f(0)|,\Vert{f'}\Vert_{I}\}, &
p=\infty, \end{cases} \qquad (f\in C^1(I)), \] where $\Vert{\cdot}\Vert_I$ denotes the supremum norm on $I$. Let $\mathcal{S}_p^+:=\{f\in C^1(I):f(0)\ge0,\,f'\ge0,\,\Vert{f}\Vert_{(p)}=1\}$ be the positive unit sphere of $C^1(I)$. A map $T:\mathcal{S}_p^+\to \mathcal{S}_p^+$ is called norm-additive if \[ \|T(f)+T(g)\|_{(p)}=\|f+g\|_{(p)} \qquad (f,g\in \mathcal{S}_p^+). \] For $1<p\le\infty$, we prove that every surjective norm-additive map $T\colon\mathcal{S}_p^+\to\mathcal{S}_p^+$ is of the form \[ T(f)(t) =
f(0)+\int_0^t f'(τ(s))\,ds \] for all $f\in\mathcal{S}_p^+$ and $t\in I$, where $τ\colon I\to I$ is a homeomorphism. Conversely, every map of this form is a surjective norm-additive map. Consequently, every such map extends to a surjective linear isometry from $(C^1(I),\Vert{\cdot}\Vert_{(p)})$ onto itself. For $p=1$, however, we construct a surjective norm-additive map on $\mathcal{S}_1^+$ which is not of the above form and admits no linear extension to $C^1(I)$, showing that the assumption $p>1$ is essential.

</details>


### [122] [Azumaya descent and étale-local cyclotomic comparison: a noncommutative filtration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02870)
*Hông Vân Lê,Alexander Zuevsky*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 将Bhatt-Morrow-Scholze (BMS) motivic filtration从quasisyntomic环上的THH推广到Azumaya代数，并证明Azumaya descent定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BMS motivic filtration是拓扑Hochschild同调(THH)研究中的重要工具，但此前主要针对交换环情形。作者希望将其推广到非交换的Azumaya代数情形，探索THH在Azumaya下降下的行为。

Method: 证明Azumaya descent定理：Azumaya代数的THH作为底层谱等价于其中心的THH，并在π_0上由reduced trace而非unit map归一化。考察比较映射Φ_A的cyclotomic性质，引入Morita trace构造传输的filtration，并对connective E_∞环上的derived Azumaya代数在连通性假设下建立类似结论。

Result: THH of Azumaya algebra ≃ THH of its center（作为底层谱），归一化用reduced trace。比较映射Φ_A未被证明是étale-locally cyclotomic，但存在独立的Morita trace是cyclotomic étale-locally，仅在π_0上与Φ_A一致。传输的filtration恰为中心BMS filtration，在Φ_A的自同构模糊性下良定义；它对Brauer类不敏感，Brauer类改由THH不能看到的K-理论与模范畴配对反映。

Conclusion: Azumaya descent定理成立，BMS filtration可推广到Azumaya代数并在底层谱层面与中心的THH一致；所得filtration对Brauer类不敏感，Brauer信息由K-理论与模范畴配对承载。对于connective E_∞环上的derived Azumaya代数，在连通性假设下类似结论成立。

Abstract: Bhatt-Morrow-Scholze motivic filtration on topological Hochschild homology ($THH$) is extended to Azumaya algebras over quasisyntomic rings. We prove the Azumaya descent theorem:
  $THH$ of such an algebra is equivalent, as an underlying spectrum, to $THH$ of its center,
  normalized on $π_0$ by the reduced trace instead of the unit map. This comparison $Φ_A$ is not shown to be étale-locally cyclotomic.
  But what is cyclotomic étale-locally is a separate Morita trace, agreeing with $Φ_A$ only on $π_0$. The resulting transported filtration is exactly the center BMS filtration, well defined up to the automorphism ambiguity of $Φ_A$. It is insensitive to the Brauer class, reflected instead by $K$-theoretic and module-categorical pairings $THH$ does not see. We establish the analogous statement for derived Azumaya algebras over connective $\mathbb{E}_\infty$-rings, under a connectivity hypothesis.

</details>


### [123] [Spectral Properties of Convolution Operators on Non-Separable Sequence Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02919)
*Sayan Saha,Arnab Patra*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 刻画了非可分空间σ_∞上有界卷积算子的各类谱，并给出序列空间上算子多项式精细谱的统一描述框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对偶性与可分性方法在非可分空间σ_∞（Hahn序列空间的对偶）上失效，需发展新的谱分析途径。

Method: 采用线性泛函方法，结合单侧移位算子的精细谱分解。

Result: 完整刻画了σ_∞上有界卷积算子的谱、点谱、连续谱与剩余谱；并作为主要应用，建立了无需可分性条件下一般序列空间上算子多项式精细谱的完全描述。

Conclusion: 该结果提供统一框架，涵盖各类序列空间上一般带状三角矩阵的经典谱判定，并实现非可分空间上精细谱的显式计算。

Abstract: This paper provides a complete characterization of the spectrum, point spectrum, continuous spectrum, and residual spectrum of bounded convolution operators acting on the non-separable space $σ_\infty$, the dual of the Hahn sequence space. Because standard duality and separability-based techniques fail in this setting, a linear functional approach is employed with the fine spectral decomposition of the unilateral shift operator. Furthermore, as a major application of this methodology, we establish a complete description of the fine spectrum for operator polynomials acting on general sequence spaces without requiring separability. This result provides a unified framework that subsumes classical spectral determinations for general banded triangular matrices across various sequence spaces and enables the explicit computation of fine spectrum on non-separable spaces.

</details>


### [124] [Langevin-type dynamics and hypocoercivity on $\mathrm{SE}(3)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03005)
*Martin Grothaus,Andrea V. Hurtado-Quiceno*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 在欧氏运动群 SE(3) 上构造亚强制性 Kolmogorov 型算子，利用抽象亚强制性方法证明其半群在 L²(SE(3), μ_Φ) 中以指数速度收敛到平衡态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究具退化扩散与输运、扭矩耦合的 Kolmogorov 型算子在非平凡李群（欧氏运动群 SE(3)）上的长时间行为，需借助亚强制性方法处理对称部分退化导致的收敛性分析难点。

Method: 采用 ZXZ 欧拉角参数化 SE(3)=ℝ³⋊SO(3)；将算子 L=S−A 分解为对称部分 S（SO(3) 上的退化扩散）与反对称部分 A（由李群结构诱导的输运与扭矩）。不变概率测度为 μ_Φ=γ_Φ⊗ν₃。在 Φ∈C^∞(ℝ³) 下有界、γ_Φ 满足 Poincaré 不等式（常数 Λ>0）、且满足梯度增长条件 |∇²Φ(ξ)| ≤ c(1+|∇Φ(ξ)|) 的前提下，运用抽象亚强制性方法分析闭包算子 L 生成半群的收敛性。

Result: 在上述条件下，L 的闭包生成的半群在 L²(SE(3), μ_Φ) 中指数快速收敛到平衡态。

Conclusion: 该工作将亚强制性框架推广至 SE(3) 上的退化扩散—输运算子，给出了指数收敛到不变测度的充分条件。

Abstract: We construct hypocoercive Kolmogorov-type operators on the Euclidean motion group $\mathrm{SE}(3)=\mathbb{R}^3\rtimes\mathrm{SO}(3)$ using ZXZ Euler angles and study their long-time behaviour using the abstract hypocoercivity method. The operator $L=S-A$ decomposes into a symmetric part $S$, corresponding to a degenerate diffusion on the rotation component $\mathrm{SO}(3)$, and an antisymmetric part $A$, which captures transport and torque effects induced by the Lie group structure. The associated invariant probability measure is $μ_Φ=γ_Φ\otimesν_3$, where $γ_Φ$ is a probability measure on $\mathbb{R}^3$ with density $Z(Φ)^{-1}e^{-Φ}$ with respect to the Lebesgue measure, and $ν_3$ is the normalized Haar measure on $\mathrm{SO}(3)$.
  As a consequence, if $Φ\in C^\infty(\mathbb{R}^3)$ is bounded from below, $γ_Φ$ satisfies a Poincaré inequality with constant $Λ>0$, and there exists $c<\infty$ such that \begin{equation*}
  |\nabla_ξ^2Φ(ξ)|
  \leq c\bigl(1+|\nabla_ξΦ(ξ)|\bigr),
  \quad ξ\in\mathbb{R}^3, \end{equation*} then the semigroup generated by the closure of $L$ converges exponentially fast to equilibrium in $L^2(\mathrm{SE}(3),μ_Φ)$.

</details>


### [125] [Skew Schäffer Type Constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03050)
*Ying Xu,Qi Liu*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 引入斜 Schäffer 型常数，作为新近提出的斜 James 型常数的下确界对偶，并在 Banach 空间中建立其基本几何理论，得到精确值与一致非方性、一致正规结构的刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斜 James 型常数已被提出，但其下确界对偶（斜 Schäffer 型常数）尚缺系统研究；而已知 Schäffer 型常数与 James 型常数在 Banach 空间几何理论中互为补充。本文旨在补全这一对偶框架，并借此获得一致非方性与一致正规结构的新刻画。

Method: 定义斜 Schäffer 型常数为斜 James 型常数的下确界对偶；建立其基本几何性质，推导普适上界与下界；通过严格间隙条件刻画一致非方性；在 Hilbert 空间与 Lebesgue 空间中计算精确值并建立与斜 James 型常数的精确关系；给出 James 模估计。

Result: 该常数具有尖锐的普适界，并通过严格间隙条件刻画一致非方性；在 Hilbert 空间与 Lebesgue 空间中获得精确值，并建立其与斜 James 型常数的精确关系；由 James 模估计导出一致正规结构的充分条件。

Conclusion: 斜 Schäffer 型常数构成斜 James 型常数的对偶理论，为 Banach 空间一致非方性与一致正规结构提供了新的定量刻画工具，并在经典空间中给出可计算的精确表达。

Abstract: We introduce the skew Schäffer type constant as an infimal counterpart of the recently developed skew James type constant \cite{RaoWuLiuNiWang} and establish its basic geometric theory in Banach spaces. The new constant admits sharp universal bounds and characterizes uniform nonsquareness through a strict gap condition. Exact values are obtained in Hilbert and Lebesgue spaces, where an exact relation with the skew James type constant is established. We further derive a James-modulus estimate leading to sufficient conditions for uniform normal structure.

</details>


### [126] [Basic Theory of Tower Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03179)
*Quanfeng Liu,Nikica Uglešić*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究Banach空间偶数次迭代对偶的完备正向极限与奇数次对偶的有界逆向极限，给出显式构造、对偶关系及比较映射，纠正Uglešić预印本中的若干断言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统刻画沿典范嵌入的迭代对偶化所诱导的极限结构（完备正向极限与有界逆向极限），澄清其中未完备极限、Banach完备化及不同同构含义的差异，并检验相关文献断言的正确性。

Method: 对偶偶次迭代对偶的完备正向极限与奇次对偶的有界逆向极限进行显式构造，建立二者对偶关系，描述迭代对偶化对应的比较映射；构造反例检验Uglešić关于维数稳定性与双对偶与正向极限交换性的断言；并对有限商形态进行同构分类。

Result: 给出两类极限的显式构造与对偶关系；对Uglešić预印本中关于维数稳定性和双对偶化与正向极限交换的断言给出反例；证明有限商形态由连续对偶的代数维数分类；计算ℓ^∞(N)的有限迭代对偶及完备塔的维数，并证明塔空间中较高层的典范拷贝未必是M-投影的像。

Conclusion: 迭代对偶化的极限结构需明确区分未完备极限、Banach完备化与同构的不同含义；Uglešić的部分断言不成立，所构造的反例澄清了维数稳定性等问题，并对有限商形态给出同构分类。

Abstract: For a Banach space $X$, we study the completed direct limit of its even iterated duals along the canonical embeddings and the bounded inverse limit of the corresponding odd duals. We give explicit constructions, establish their duality relation, and describe the comparison maps associated with iterated dualization. We then examine several assertions in three preprints of Uglešić, giving counterexamples concerning dimension stability and the commutation of bidualization with direct limits. The finite quotient shape (the category whose objects are the finite-dimensional quotients, with all continuous linear maps between them) is classified by the algebraic dimension of the continuous dual. We also compute the dimensions of the finite iterated duals and the completed tower of $\ell^{\infty}(\mathbb{N})$, and prove that, inside the tower space, the canonical copy of a higher layer need not be the range of an $M$-projection. Throughout, uncompleted limits, Banach completions, and the different meanings of isomorphism are distinguished explicitly.

</details>


### [127] [A Balian-Low theorem for nilpotent Lie groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03200)
*Ulrik Enstad,Jordy Timo van Velthoven*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了连通单连通幂零Lie群轨道上光滑相干框架与Riesz序列的严格必要密度条件，将同质群的结果推广至一般幂零Lie群；核心创新在于通过形变表示而非指标集来证明稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相干框架与Riesz序列的密度条件此前主要在同质群（homogeneous groups）上得到，对一般幂零Lie群尚不明确；本文旨在推广这一框架，建立更一般的严格必要密度条件。

Method: 针对连通、单连通的幂零Lie群，研究其表示轨道上的光滑相干框架与Riesz序列；关键手段是证明一个稳定性结果，其做法是对表示本身进行形变，而非像以往工作那样形变指标集。

Result: 证明了幂零Lie群情形下的严格必要密度条件，并建立了基于表示形变的框架与Riesz序列稳定性结论。

Conclusion: 结果将同质群上的密度条件推广到一般幂零Lie群，且表示形变这一新思路为相干框架与Riesz序列的稳定性分析提供了新的技术途径。

Abstract: We prove strict necessary density conditions for smooth coherent frames and Riesz sequences in the orbit of a connected, simply connected, nilpotent Lie group. This extends previous results for homogeneous groups to general nilpotent Lie groups. The main novelty in our approach is that we prove a stability result for coherent frames and Riesz sequences based on deforming their representation rather than their index set.

</details>


### [128] [A polynomial bound in Dvoretzky's theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03204)
*Boaz Klartag,Shahar Moshe*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文给出 ε-Dvoretzky 猜想的一个简单证明，证明 Dvoretzky 定理中逼近参数 ε 的依赖是多项式级别的，并对有限族凸体给出同时化结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dvoretzky 定理保证高维凸体存在近欧氏截面，但此前对逼近参数 ε 的依赖是否为 1/ε 的多项式（即 ε-Dvoretzky 猜想）尚需简单而直接的证明，同时希望获得对 ε 更优的依赖以及多凸体的同时化结果。

Method: 采用概率方法，但与以往使用的概率模型不同，使用了新的概率模型进行证明；并进一步将该方法推广到内部含原点的有限族凸体，构造公共线性子空间。

Result: 若 n ≥ (C/ε)^{ℓ/2+1}，则任意 n 维凸体通过任意给定内点都存在 ℓ 维截面与欧氏球 ε-接近，其中 C 为通用常数；实际获得对 ε 更优的依赖。对有限族凸体得到同时化定理：存在公共线性子空间使所有凸体均有近欧氏截面。此外将逼近欧氏球半径与凸体及其对偶的平均宽度等几何参数进行比较。

Conclusion: 该工作以更简单的概率证明确立了 ε-Dvoretzky 猜想，并给出同时化版本及逼近球半径与已知几何量的联系，深化了对高维凸体截面欧氏结构的理解。

Abstract: We present a simple proof of the $\varepsilon$-Dvoretzky conjecture, which asserts that the dependence on the approximation parameter $\varepsilon$ in Dvoretzky's theorem is polynomial in $1/\varepsilon$. In particular, if $n \geq (C/\varepsilon)^{\ell/2+1}$, then any $n$-dimensional convex body has, through any given interior point, an $\ell$-dimensional section that is $\varepsilon$-close to a Euclidean ball. Here, $C > 0$ is a universal constant. We in fact obtain a sharper dependence on $\varepsilon$. The proof is probabilistic, but uses a different probabilistic model from those employed previously. We also prove a simultaneous version of our theorem for a finite family of convex bodies with the origin in their interior, yielding a common linear subspace on which all of the bodies have nearly-Euclidean sections. Finally, we compare the radius of the approximating Euclidean ball with familiar geometric parameters, such as the mean widths of the body and its dual.

</details>


### [129] [Another characterization of $\dot W^{1,p}(\mathbb R^d)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03426)
*Rupert L. Frank*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文改进了Sobolev空间$\dot W^{1,p}(\mathbb R^d)$（$1<p<\infty$）基于变中心变半径球上函数振荡的刻画，将原先的supremum有限性假设减弱为liminf有限性，从而回答了Brezis提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期对Sobolev空间的刻画依赖于振荡量supremum的有限性，Brezis提出是否可用形式上更弱的liminf有限性来替代，这一问题构成了本文的研究动机。

Method: 在已有基于球上函数振荡的刻画基础上，将条件从supremum有限性替换为liminf有限性，并证明该较弱条件仍足以刻画Sobolev空间$\dot W^{1,p}(\mathbb R^d)$。

Result: 证明了以liminf有限性替代supremum有限性后，Sobolev空间$\dot W^{1,p}(\mathbb R^d)$（$1<p<\infty$）的振荡刻画依然成立，即两个条件在刻画该空间时等价。

Conclusion: 肯定回答了Brezis的问题：在球上振荡刻画中，supremum有限性可被liminf有限性替代，改进了此前的特征刻画结果。

Abstract: We improve an earlier characterization of the Sobolev space $\dot W^{1,p}(\mathbb R^d)$, $1<p<\infty$, in terms of the oscillations of a function on balls of varying centers and radii. In contrast to earlier work, finiteness of the supremum is replaced by the formally weaker assumption of finiteness of the liminf, thereby answering a question by Brezis.

</details>


### [130] [A Hardy-space operator acting on $ζ$-zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03568)
*Juan Manzur,Waleed Noor*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文在Hardy空间框架下，构造了一个有界算子 $T$，将Riemann假设转化为关于子空间 $\mathcal{N}$ 闭包的 $T$-不变性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的Nyman-Beurling与Báez-Duarte判据通过Hardy空间方法将RH等价于某函数序列张成空间 $\mathcal{N}$ 在 $H^2$ 中的稠密性。作者希望把这一稠密性问题进一步转化为算子不变子空间问题，从而给出算子论形式的RH判据。

Method: 在 $H^2$ 上构造一个有界算子 $T$；考察序列 $(h_k)_{k\geq2}$ 张成空间 $\mathcal{N}$ 的闭包，并分析其是否为 $T$-不变子空间。

Result: 证明了若 $\overline{\mathcal{N}}$ 是 $T$-不变的，则要么Riemann假设成立，要么存在无穷多个 $\zeta$ 零点（构成无穷递进）。由此得到RH的一个算子论等价判据。

Conclusion: RH可表述为一个关于有界算子 $T$ 的不变子空间问题，为RH研究提供了算子理论的新视角。

Abstract: A Hardy space approach to the Nyman-Beurling and Báez-Duarte criterion for the Riemann Hypothesis (RH) was introduced in [7]. It states that the RH holds if and only if the span of a particular sequence of functions $(h_k)_{k\geq2}$, denoted $\mathcal{N}$, is dense in the Hardy space $H^2$. In this note we construct a bounded operator $T$ on $H^{2}$ such that if the closure of $\mathcal{N}$ is $T$-invariant, then either the RH holds or there exists an infinite progression of $ζ$-zeros. In particular, this yields an operator-theoretic criterion for the Riemann hypothesis as an invariant subspace question.

</details>

### [131] [Weak type $(1,1)$ boundedness of Bochner--Riesz means at the critical index on quantum tori](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03107)
*Xudong Lai*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文在量子环面 $\mathbb{T}^d_\theta$ 上，对临界指标 $\delta=\frac{d-1}{2}$ 的 Bochner--Riesz 平均算子，在核粗糙的情形下建立了弱型 $(1,1)$ 估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bochner--Riesz 平均算子在临界指标处的弱型 $(1,1)$ 估计是调和分析中的经典问题欧氏空间上已有成熟理论。将其推广到非交换几何背景下的量子环面，并允许核粗糙，具有理论意义但存在困难。

Method: 在量子环面 $\mathbb{T}^d_\theta$ 的框架下，研究临界指标 $\delta=\frac{d-1}{2}$ 处的 Bochner--Riesz 平均算子，针对核粗糙（不光滑）的情形进行分析与估计。

Result: 对临界指标 $\delta=\frac{d-1}{2}$ 的 Bochner--Riesz 平均算子，在核粗糙的条件下，成功建立了弱型 $(1,1)$ 估计。

Conclusion: 该结果将临界 Bochner--Riesz 算子的弱型 $(1,1)$ 估计推广至量子环面，并在核粗糙情形下依然成立。

Abstract: We establish a weak type $(1,1)$ estimate for the Bochner--Riesz mean operator at the critical index $δ=\frac{d-1}{2}$ on the quantum torus $\mathbb{T}^d_θ$, where the associated kernel is rough.

</details>


### [132] [Talagrand type for noncommutative $L_1$ spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03407)
*Sijie Luo,Yusen Wang,Dejian Zhou*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文证明了半有限冯诺依曼代数上的 $L_1(\mathcal{M})$ 具有 Talagrand 型 $(1,\psi_{1,1})$，其中 $\psi_{1,1}(t)=t/\log(e+t)$，由此解决了 Cordero-Erausquin 与 Eskenazis (2023) 提出的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cordero-Erausquin 与 Eskenazis (2023) 提出了关于 Schatten 迹类 $S_1$ 的 Talagrand 型的公开问题，本文旨在解决该问题，并将其推广到更一般的半有限冯诺依曼代数情形。

Method: 证明基于 Ricard 所引入的非交换 Mazur 映射。

Result: 证明了 $L_1(\mathcal{M})$ 具有 Talagrand 型 $(1,\psi_{1,1})$，其中 $\psi_{1,1}(t)=t/\log(e+t)$；作为推论，Schatten 迹类 $S_1$ 也具有 Talagrand 型 $(1,\psi_{1,1})$。

Conclusion: 作为推论，本文解决了 Cordero-Erausquin 与 Eskenazis (2023) 中 Question 2 的公开问题。

Abstract: Let $(\mathcal{M},τ)$ be a semi-finite von Neumann algebra. We prove that $L_1(\mathcal{M})$ has Talagrand type $(1,ψ_{1,1})$, where $ψ_{1,1}(t)=t/\log(e+t)$. As an consequence, the Schatten trace class $S_1$ has Talagrand type $(1,ψ_{1,1})$, thus settled an open problem of Question 2 in Cordero-Erausquin and Eskenazis (2023). Our proof is based on the noncommutative Mazur maps initiated by Ricard.

</details>


### [133] [QWEP stability under twisted crossed products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03424)
*Kai ZENG*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究扭叉积的QWEP性质及其与非交换L_p空间上Fourier乘子转移的关系，构造了QWEP不被超线性群的叉积保持的反例，并给出标量扭下QWEP的等价刻画与正向上限制定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QWEP（弱期望性质）是算子代数中的重要逼近性质，其在叉积运算下的保持性尚不清楚。同时，Fourier乘子转移理论需要QWEP作为输入，因此刻画扭叉积何时具有QWEP对两方面均有意义。

Method: 通过构造无挠剩余有限Kazhdan群在扩散交换代数及超有限II_1因子上的保迹作用，给出QWEP不被超线性群叉积保持的反例；对含非平凡标量余圈的情形得到非QWEP叉积；对有限保迹标量扭叉积证明其QWEP等价于普通叉积与扭群von Neumann代数均QWEP；并对正则圈积的标量扭建立限制定理，同时用完全有界乘子范数刻画QWEP障碍。

Result: 证明QWEP不被QWEP von Neumann代数经超线性群的叉积保持；构造出非QWEP的扭叉积；给出标量扭下有限保迹叉积QWEP的充要条件；建立标量扭正则圈积的限制定理；识别出完全有界乘子范数这一QWEP障碍；并用算子值余圈构造出作用群非超线性的有限QWEP叉积。

Conclusion: 叉积一般不保持QWEP，但在标量扭情形下QWEP可由普通叉积与扭群代数共同刻画；这些结果既为Fourier乘子转移提供QWEP输入，也揭示了完全有界乘子范数作为QWEP障碍，并给出作用群非超线性的QWEP叉积实例。

Abstract: We study the QWEP property of twisted crossed products and its relation to Fourier multiplier transference on noncommutative $L_p$-spaces. We show that QWEP is not preserved by crossed products of QWEP von Neumann algebras by hyperlinear groups. Our examples include trace-preserving actions of a torsion-free residually finite Kazhdan group on a diffuse abelian algebra and on the hyperfinite type II$_1$ factor. We also obtain non-QWEP crossed products with nontrivial scalar cocycles. For scalar twists, we prove that a finite trace-preserving crossed product is QWEP if and only if its ordinary crossed product and its twisted group von Neumann algebra are QWEP. In the positive direction, we establish a restriction theorem for scalar twists of regular wreath products. These results provide QWEP inputs for Fourier multiplier transference and identify a completely bounded multiplier norm obstruction to QWEP. Finally, operator-valued cocycles give finite QWEP crossed products whose acting groups are not hyperlinear.

</details>
